class Any (*args, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class Any(metaclass=_AnyMeta):
    """Special type indicating an unconstrained type.

    - Any is compatible with every type.
    - Any assumed to have all methods.
    - All values assumed to be instances of Any.

    Note that all the above statements are true from the point of view of
    static type checkers. At runtime, Any should not be used with instance
    checks.
    """

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if cls is Any:
            raise TypeError("Any cannot be instantiated")
        return super().__new__(cls)

class BaseAttention (config: Any | None = None,
rank: int | None = None,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
class BaseAttention(nn.Module):
    """Every custom attention module must inherit from this."""

    def __init__(self, config: Any | None = None, rank: int | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.rank = rank
        self.num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or (
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(config, "num_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12)),
                )
                if config is not None
                else 12
            )
        )
        self.hidden_size = (
            kwargs.get("hidden_size")
            or kwargs.get("dim")
            or kwargs.get("d_model")
            or (
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_size",
                    getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 768)),
                )
                if config is not None
                else 768
            )
        )
        self.head_dim = getattr(
            config,
            "d_kv",
            getattr(
                config,
                "head_dim",
                self.hidden_size // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64,
            ),
        )

    @abstractmethod
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states,
        attention_mask=None,
        head_mask=None,
        output_attentions=False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> tuple[Any, Any | None]:
        """Must return (output, attention_weights_or_None)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Subclasses of BaseAttention must implement forward()")

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        """Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer."""
        if old_attn is None:
            return
        import torch

        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        adapter = UniversalAdapter()

        try:
            w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "q")
            w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "k")
            w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "v")
            w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "o")

            with torch.no_grad():
                dim = self.hidden_size
                for q_name in ["W_q", "q_proj", "q_lin", "Wq", "query", "q"]:
                    if hasattr(self, q_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, q_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_q.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_q)
                            if b_q is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_q)

                for k_name in ["W_k", "k_proj", "k_lin", "Wk", "key", "k"]:
                    if hasattr(self, k_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, k_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_k.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_k)
                            if b_k is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_k)

                for v_name in ["W_v", "v_proj", "v_lin", "Wv", "value", "v"]:
                    if hasattr(self, v_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, v_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_v.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_v)
                            if b_v is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_v)

                for o_name in ["out", "out_proj", "o_proj", "out_lin", "proj", "dense"]:
                    if hasattr(self, o_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, o_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_o.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_o)
                            if b_o is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_o)

                # Handle fused in_proj
                if hasattr(self, "in_proj") and hasattr(self.in_proj, "weight"):
                    if self.in_proj.weight.shape == torch.Size([dim * 2, dim]):
                        self.in_proj.weight[:dim].copy_(w_v[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[dim:].copy_(w_q[:dim])
                    elif self.in_proj.weight.shape == torch.Size([dim * 3, dim]):
                        self.in_proj.weight[:dim].copy_(w_q[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[dim : 2 * dim].copy_(w_k[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[2 * dim :].copy_(w_v[:dim])
        except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as e:
            logger.debug("BaseAttention.copy_weights failed: %s", e)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Unexpected error in BaseAttention.copy_weights: %s", e)

    def extra_repr(self) -> str:
        """Extra repr."""
        return f"num_heads={self.num_heads}, head_dim={self.head_dim}, rank={self.rank}"

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
    """Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer."""
    if old_attn is None:
        return
    import torch

    from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

    adapter = UniversalAdapter()

    try:
        w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "q")
        w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "k")
        w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "v")
        w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "o")

                        mod.weight.copy_(w_q)

                            mod.bias.copy_(b_q)

                if hasattr(self, k_name):

class DualStageStripeAttention (cfg: NEXUSConfig,
head_dim: int,
num_heads: int = 1,
is_decoder: bool = False,
is_nested_encoder: bool = False,
**kwargs)
Expand source code 
class DualStageStripeAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        cfg: NEXUSConfig,
        head_dim: int,
        num_heads: int = 1,
        is_decoder: bool = False,
        is_nested_encoder: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.is_decoder = is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = is_nested_encoder
        self.xattn_scorer = XAttentionScorer()

    def _inter_block_topk(self, scores: torch.Tensor, top_k: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, nb, nb2 = scores.shape
        tk = min(top_k, nb2)
        scores_inter = scores.clone()
        min_dim = min(nb, nb2)
        diag_idx = torch.arange(min_dim, device=scores.device)
        scores_inter[:, :, diag_idx, diag_idx] = float("-inf")
        _, idx = scores_inter.topk(tk, dim=-1)
        mask = torch.zeros_like(scores, dtype=torch.bool).scatter_(-1, idx, True)
        mask[:, :, diag_idx, diag_idx] = True
        return mask

    def forward(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,
        K: torch.Tensor,
        V: torch.Tensor,
        token_valid: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        seer_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        bs = self.cfg.block_size
        nb = (N + bs - 1) // bs
        top_k = self.cfg.top_k_blocks
        scale = D**-0.5

        if self.cfg.xattn_enabled:
            block_scores = self.xattn_scorer.score_blocks(Q, K, bs, self.cfg.xattn_stride)
            inter_mask = self._inter_block_topk(block_scores, top_k)
        else:
            inter_mask = torch.ones(B, H, nb, nb, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.bool)

        # Handle boolean base mask
        inter_mask[:, :, :, 0] = True
        diag_idx = torch.arange(nb, device=Q.device)
        inter_mask[:, :, diag_idx, diag_idx] = True

        scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        expanded_inter_mask = inter_mask.repeat_interleave(bs, dim=2).repeat_interleave(bs, dim=3)[:, :, :N, :N]
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~expanded_inter_mask, -10000.0)

        if seer_mask is not None:
            # seer_mask is an STE float mask [B, H, nb, nb]
            expanded_seer = seer_mask.repeat_interleave(bs, dim=2).repeat_interleave(bs, dim=3)[:, :, :N, :N]
            # Inject differentiable mask via additive penalty
            scores = scores + (expanded_seer - 1.0) * 10000.0

        if self.cfg.stripe_enabled and N > 256:
            sw = self.cfg.stripe_width
            idx = torch.arange(N, device=Q.device)
            stripe_group = idx // sw
            q_stripe = stripe_group.unsqueeze(1)
            k_stripe = stripe_group.unsqueeze(0)
            stripe_mask = (q_stripe - k_stripe).abs() <= 1
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~stripe_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -10000.0)

        if token_valid is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill((~token_valid.bool()).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0)

        if self.cfg.softpick_enabled:
            attn = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=self.cfg.softpick_temperature).to(Q.dtype)
        else:
            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(Q.dtype)

        context = torch.matmul(attn, V)
        return context

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Expand source code 
def forward(
    self,
    Q: torch.Tensor,
    K: torch.Tensor,
    V: torch.Tensor,
    token_valid: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    seer_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    bs = self.cfg.block_size
    nb = (N + bs - 1) // bs
    top_k = self.cfg.top_k_blocks
    scale = D**-0.5

    if self.cfg.xattn_enabled:
        block_scores = self.xattn_scorer.score_blocks(Q, K, bs, self.cfg.xattn_stride)
        inter_mask = self._inter_block_topk(block_scores, top_k)
    else:
        inter_mask = torch.ones(B, H, nb, nb, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.bool)

    # Handle boolean base mask
    inter_mask[:, :, :, 0] = True
    diag_idx = torch.arange(nb, device=Q.device)
    inter_mask[:, :, diag_idx, diag_idx] = True

    scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

    expanded_inter_mask = inter_mask.repeat_interleave(bs, dim=2).repeat_interleave(bs, dim=3)[:, :, :N, :N]
    scores = scores.masked_fill(~expanded_inter_mask, -10000.0)

    if seer_mask is not None:
        # seer_mask is an STE float mask [B, H, nb, nb]
        expanded_seer = seer_mask.repeat_interleave(bs, dim=2).repeat_interleave(bs, dim=3)[:, :, :N, :N]
        # Inject differentiable mask via additive penalty
        scores = scores + (expanded_seer - 1.0) * 10000.0

    if self.cfg.stripe_enabled and N > 256:
        sw = self.cfg.stripe_width
        idx = torch.arange(N, device=Q.device)
        stripe_group = idx // sw
        q_stripe = stripe_group.unsqueeze(1)
        k_stripe = stripe_group.unsqueeze(0)
        stripe_mask = (q_stripe - k_stripe).abs() <= 1
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~stripe_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -10000.0)

    if token_valid is not None:
        scores = scores.masked_fill((~token_valid.bool()).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0)

    if self.cfg.softpick_enabled:
        attn = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=self.cfg.softpick_temperature).to(Q.dtype)
    else:
        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(Q.dtype)

    context = torch.matmul(attn, V)
    return context

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class DynamicLowRankProjector (rank: int = 32,
in_dim: int = 768,

bias: bool = True,

dtype: torch.dtype | None = None)
Expand source code 
class DynamicLowRankProjector(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    r"""SVD-initialized low-rank projector with dynamic variational gate scaling and LoRA adapters."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        rank: int = 32,
        in_dim: int = 768,
        out_dim: int = 768,
        bias: bool = True,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        freeze_base: bool = True,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        factory_kwargs = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.rank = rank
        self.in_dim = in_dim
        self.out_dim = out_dim
        self.lora_rank = lora_rank
        self.scaling = lora_alpha / max(1, lora_rank)

        self.U = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(out_dim, rank, **factory_kwargs) * 0.02)
        self.S = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(rank, **factory_kwargs))
        self.V = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_dim, rank, **factory_kwargs) * 0.02)

        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(out_dim, **factory_kwargs))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((lora_rank, in_dim), **factory_kwargs))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_dim, lora_rank), **factory_kwargs))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        if freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.U.requires_grad = False
        self.S.requires_grad = False
        self.V.requires_grad = False
        if self.bias is not None:
            self.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            if self.lora_A is not None:
                self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            if self.lora_B is not None:
                self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def set_weight(self, weight: torch.Tensor, bias: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = weight.float()
            U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            eff_rank = min(self.rank, U.size(1), Vh.size(0))

            self.U.data.zero_()
            self.S.data.zero_()
            self.V.data.zero_()

            min_u = min(self.U.size(0), U.size(0))
            min_v = min(self.V.size(0), Vh.size(1))

            self.U.data[:min_u, :eff_rank].copy_(U[:min_u, :eff_rank])
            self.S.data[:eff_rank].copy_(S[:eff_rank])
            self.V.data[:min_v, :eff_rank].copy_(Vh[:eff_rank, :min_v].t().contiguous())

            if bias is not None and self.bias is not None:
                b_val = bias.data if isinstance(bias, nn.Parameter) else bias
                min_b = min(self.bias.size(0), b_val.size(0))
                self.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(b_val[:min_b])

            rec = (self.U[:, :eff_rank] * self.S[:eff_rank].unsqueeze(0)) @ self.V[
                :, :eff_rank
            ].t().contiguous()
            f_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(rec))
            if f_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"DynamicLowRankProjector: reconstructed norm is near zero ({f_norm}), re-initializing randomly"
                )
                nn.init.orthogonal_(self.U.data)
                nn.init.orthogonal_(self.V.data)
                self.S.data.fill_(1.0)

    def copy_lora_from(self, sub: Any) -> None:
        """Preserve source LoRA adapters."""
        if (
            hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
            and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
            and sub.lora_A is not None
            and sub.lora_B is not None
        ):
            src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
            src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
            if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.lora_A is not None:
                r_min = min(self.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                in_min = min(self.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                self.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min])
                self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.lora_B is not None:
                out_min = min(self.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                r_min = min(self.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                self.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min])
                self.lora_B.requires_grad = True
            if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                self.scaling = sub.lora_scaling

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, gate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        v = self.V.to(x.dtype)
        s = self.S.to(x.dtype)
        u = self.U.to(x.dtype)
        g = gate.to(x.dtype)
        z = torch.matmul(x, v)
        z = z * s.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0) * g.unsqueeze(1)
        out = torch.matmul(z, u.t().contiguous())
        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias.to(x.dtype)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            la = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype)
            lb = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype)
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, la), lb) * self.scaling
            out = out + lora_out

        return out

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

class ExpertChoiceMoE (d_model: int,
n_experts: int = 2,
capacity_factor: float = 1.0,
noise_std: float = 0.05)
Expand source code 
class ExpertChoiceMoE(nn.Module):
    r"""
    Expert Choice Routing MoE layer.
    Each expert selects top-k tokens with the highest affinity scores.
    Equipped with zero-initialized final layers for step-0 representation preservation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        d_model: int,
        n_experts: int = 2,
        capacity_factor: float = 1.0,
        noise_std: float = 0.05,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.capacity_factor = capacity_factor
        self.noise_std = noise_std
        self.aux_loss: torch.Tensor = torch.tensor(0.0)

        self.router_ln = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model)
        self.router = nn.Linear(d_model, n_experts, bias=False)
        nn.init.uniform_(self.router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)

        expert_hidden_dim = max(32, d_model // 4)
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList(
            [
                nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(d_model, expert_hidden_dim),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    nn.Linear(expert_hidden_dim, d_model),
                )
                for _ in range(n_experts)
            ]
        )

        # Active MoE expert representation
        for expert in self.experts:
            nn.init.normal_(expert[2].weight, std=0.02)
            if expert[2].bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(expert[2].bias)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        r"""Forward execution using Expert Choice routing."""
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_flat = x.view(-1, D)
        T = x_flat.size(0)

        if self.d_model == D:
            logits = self.router(self.router_ln(x_flat))
        else:
            normed = F.layer_norm(x_flat, (D,))
            if not hasattr(self, "_dynamic_router") or self._dynamic_router.in_features != D:
                self._dynamic_router = nn.Linear(D, self.n_experts, bias=False).to(
                    device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype
                )
                nn.init.uniform_(self._dynamic_router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)
            logits = self._dynamic_router(normed)

        if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
            logits = logits + torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std

        # FIXED: Converted to Causal Token-Choice MoE routing to prevent future token leakage.
        # Expert-Choice applies softmax over the sequence (dim=0), which catastrophically leaks future tokens into past states.
        # We now apply softmax over the experts (dim=-1) and select top-k experts per token.
        gate_scores = F.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1).to(dtype=logits.dtype)
        
        # Select top-2 experts per token (standard MoE)
        top_k_experts = min(2, self.n_experts)
        topk_vals, topk_indices = torch.topk(gate_scores, top_k_experts, dim=-1)

        out_flat = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
        for e in range(self.n_experts):
            # Find tokens assigned to expert e
            expert_mask = topk_indices == e
            token_indices = expert_mask.any(dim=-1).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
            
            if token_indices.numel() == 0:
                continue

            # Get the gating values for the selected tokens
            # expert_mask is [T, top_k], so we need to extract the matching topk_vals
            matched_vals = topk_vals[expert_mask]

            selected_x = x_flat[token_indices]
            
            if selected_x.size(-1) == self.d_model:
                expert_out = self.experts[e](selected_x)
            else:
                if (
                    not hasattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                    or getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}").in_features != D
                ):
                    setattr(
                        self,
                        f"_expert_in_proj_{e}",
                        nn.Linear(D, self.d_model).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                    )
                    setattr(
                        self,
                        f"_expert_out_proj_{e}",
                        nn.Linear(self.d_model, D).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                    )
                proj_in = getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                proj_out = getattr(self, f"_expert_out_proj_{e}")
                expert_out = proj_out(self.experts[e](proj_in(selected_x)))

            update_val = (
                expert_out.to(out_flat.dtype) * matched_vals.unsqueeze(-1).to(out_flat.dtype)
            ).to(dtype=out_flat.dtype)
            
            out_flat.index_add_(0, token_indices, update_val)

        # Auxiliary load balancing loss
        prob_mean = F.softmax(logits.float() / 1.0, dim=-1).mean(dim=0)
        self.aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(prob_mean**2)

        return out_flat.view(B, L, D)

Expert Choice Routing MoE layer. Each expert selects top-k tokens with the highest affinity scores. Equipped with zero-initialized final layers for step-0 representation preservation.

    B, L, D = x.shape

                device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype
            )

                )
                setattr(

                    f"_expert_out_proj_{e}",
                    nn.Linear(self.d_model, D).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                )
            proj_in = getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
            proj_out = getattr(self, f"_expert_out_proj_{e}")

            expert_out.to(out_flat.dtype) * matched_vals.unsqueeze(-1).to(out_flat.dtype)
        ).to(dtype=out_flat.dtype)

    prob_mean = F.softmax(logits.float() / 1.0, dim=-1).mean(dim=0)
    self.aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(prob_mean**2)

    return out_flat.view(B, L, D)

class FactorisedLinear (in_features: int,
out_features: int,
bias: bool = True,

Expand source code 
class FactorisedLinear(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        energy: float = 0.95,
        min_rank: int = 4,
        max_rank: int = 64,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        freeze_base: bool = False,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.energy = energy
        self.min_rank = min_rank
        self.max_rank = max_rank
        self.lora_rank = lora_rank
        self.lora_alpha = lora_alpha
        self.lora_scaling = (lora_alpha / lora_rank) if lora_rank > 0 else 1.0
        self.freeze_base = freeze_base

        rank = max(min_rank, min(max_rank, min(in_features, out_features)))
        self.rank = rank
        self.svd_rank = rank  # alias for framework FLOPs detection

        factory_kwargs = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.down = nn.Linear(in_features, rank, bias=False, **factory_kwargs)
        self.up = nn.Linear(rank, out_features, bias=bias, **factory_kwargs)
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.down.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.up.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        if self.up.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.up.bias)

        if lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((lora_rank, in_features), **factory_kwargs))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, lora_rank), **factory_kwargs))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        if freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.matmul(self.up.weight, self.down.weight)

    @property
    def bias(self) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        return self.up.bias

    def decompose_from_weight(
        self,
        weight: torch.Tensor,
        bias: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        out_dim, in_dim = weight.shape
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = weight.float()
            # Shape alignment
            if W.shape[0] != self.out_features or W.shape[1] != self.in_features:
                padded = torch.zeros(
                    self.out_features, self.in_features, device=W.device, dtype=W.dtype
                )
                r = min(self.out_features, W.shape[0])
                c = min(self.in_features, W.shape[1])
                padded[:r, :c] = W[:r, :c]
                W = padded

            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            total = (S**2).sum()
            cum = torch.cumsum(S**2, dim=0) / torch.clamp(total, min=1e-8)
            rank = int((cum < self.energy).sum().item()) + 1
            max_avail_rank = self.down.weight.shape[0]
            rank = max(
                self.min_rank,
                min(
                    rank,
                    min(self.out_features, self.in_features),
                    self.max_rank,
                    max_avail_rank,
                ),
            )
            self.rank = rank

            device = self.down.weight.device
            dtype = self.down.weight.dtype

            A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
            B = Vt[:rank, :]

            self.down.weight.data.zero_()
            self.up.weight.data.zero_()
            self.down.weight.data[:rank, : self.in_features].copy_(B.to(device=device, dtype=dtype))
            self.up.weight.data[: self.out_features, :rank].copy_(A.to(device=device, dtype=dtype))

            if bias is not None and self.up.bias is not None:
                min_b = min(self.up.bias.numel(), bias.numel())
                self.up.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(bias[:min_b].to(device=device, dtype=dtype))
            elif self.up.bias is not None:
                self.up.bias.data.zero_()

            if self.freeze_base:
                self.freeze_base_weights()

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.down.weight.requires_grad = False
        self.up.weight.requires_grad = False
        if self.up.bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None:
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_B is not None:
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.down.weight.requires_grad = True
        self.up.weight.requires_grad = True
        if self.up.bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.requires_grad = True

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        out = self.up(self.down(x))
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.lora_scaling
            out = out + lora_out
        return out

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def decompose_from_weight(self, weight: torch.Tensor, bias: torch.Tensor | None = None) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def decompose_from_weight(
    self,
    weight: torch.Tensor,
    bias: torch.Tensor | None = None,
) -> None:
    out_dim, in_dim = weight.shape
    with torch.no_grad():
        W = weight.float()
        # Shape alignment
        if W.shape[0] != self.out_features or W.shape[1] != self.in_features:
            padded = torch.zeros(
                self.out_features, self.in_features, device=W.device, dtype=W.dtype
            )
            r = min(self.out_features, W.shape[0])
            c = min(self.in_features, W.shape[1])
            padded[:r, :c] = W[:r, :c]
            W = padded

        U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
        total = (S**2).sum()
        cum = torch.cumsum(S**2, dim=0) / torch.clamp(total, min=1e-8)
        rank = int((cum < self.energy).sum().item()) + 1
        max_avail_rank = self.down.weight.shape[0]
        rank = max(
            self.min_rank,
            min(
                rank,
                min(self.out_features, self.in_features),
                self.max_rank,
                max_avail_rank,
            ),
        )
        self.rank = rank

        device = self.down.weight.device
        dtype = self.down.weight.dtype

        A = U[:, :rank] * S[:rank].unsqueeze(0)
        B = Vt[:rank, :]

        self.down.weight.data.zero_()
        self.up.weight.data.zero_()
        self.down.weight.data[:rank, : self.in_features].copy_(B.to(device=device, dtype=dtype))
        self.up.weight.data[: self.out_features, :rank].copy_(A.to(device=device, dtype=dtype))

        if bias is not None and self.up.bias is not None:
            min_b = min(self.up.bias.numel(), bias.numel())
            self.up.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(bias[:min_b].to(device=device, dtype=dtype))
        elif self.up.bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.data.zero_()

        if self.freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor

    if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.lora_scaling
        out = out + lora_out
    return out

class FactorisedLinear_CUR (in_features: int,
out_features: int,
cur_rank: int = 16,
bias: bool = True,
lora_rank: int = 16,
lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
freeze_base: bool = False,
device: torch.device | None = None,
dtype: torch.dtype | None = None)
Expand source code 
class FactorisedLinear_CUR(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    r"""Linear projection compressed via leverage-score CUR matrix decomposition with LoRA support."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        cur_rank: int = 16,
        bias: bool = True,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        freeze_base: bool = False,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        factory_kwargs = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.cur_rank = max(1, min(cur_rank, in_features, out_features))
        self.lora_rank = lora_rank
        self.lora_alpha = lora_alpha
        self.lora_scaling = (lora_alpha / lora_rank) if lora_rank > 0 else 1.0
        self.freeze_base = freeze_base

        self.down = nn.Linear(in_features, self.cur_rank, bias=False, **factory_kwargs)
        self.up = nn.Linear(self.cur_rank, out_features, bias=bias, **factory_kwargs)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((lora_rank, in_features), **factory_kwargs))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, lora_rank), **factory_kwargs))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        self.reset_parameters()
        if freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.down.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.up.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        if self.up.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.up.bias)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_B is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.down.weight.requires_grad = False
        self.up.weight.requires_grad = False
        if self.up.bias is not None:
            self.up.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            if self.lora_A is not None:
                self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            if self.lora_B is not None:
                self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def copy_cur(self, weight: torch.Tensor, bias: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = weight.float()
            out_dim, in_dim = W.shape
            rank = min(self.cur_rank, out_dim, in_dim)

            try:
                U_full, S_full, Vt_full = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
                col_leverage = (Vt_full**2).sum(dim=0)
                col_leverage = col_leverage / torch.clamp(col_leverage.sum(), min=1e-5)
                row_leverage = (U_full**2).sum(dim=1)
                row_leverage = row_leverage / torch.clamp(row_leverage.sum(), min=1e-5)

                _, col_idx = torch.topk(col_leverage, k=rank, largest=True)
                _, row_idx = torch.topk(row_leverage, k=rank, largest=True)

                C = W[:, col_idx]
                R = W[row_idx, :]
                W_intersection = W[row_idx][:, col_idx]
                U_int = torch.linalg.pinv(W_intersection, rcond=1e-3)
                C_Uint = C @ U_int
                rec_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(C_Uint @ R))
                orig_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(W))

                if rec_norm > 2.0 * orig_norm or not math.isfinite(rec_norm):
                    # Ill-conditioned CUR intersection -> fallback to stable truncated SVD
                    U_r = U_full[:, :rank]
                    S_r = S_full[:rank]
                    Vt_r = Vt_full[:rank, :]
                    sqrt_s = torch.sqrt(S_r)
                    self.down.weight.data.zero_()
                    self.down.weight.data[:rank, :in_dim].copy_(
                        (sqrt_s.unsqueeze(1) * Vt_r).to(dtype=self.down.weight.dtype)
                    )
                    self.up.weight.data.zero_()
                    self.up.weight.data[:out_dim, :rank].copy_(
                        (U_r * sqrt_s.unsqueeze(0)).to(dtype=self.up.weight.dtype)
                    )
                else:
                    self.down.weight.data.zero_()
                    self.down.weight.data[:rank, :in_dim].copy_(R.to(dtype=self.down.weight.dtype))
                    self.up.weight.data.zero_()
                    self.up.weight.data[:out_dim, :rank].copy_(
                        C_Uint.to(dtype=self.up.weight.dtype)
                    )
            except Exception:
                # SVD fallback
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.down.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.up.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))

            if bias is not None and self.up.bias is not None:
                self.up.bias.data.copy_(bias.to(dtype=self.up.bias.dtype))

        if self.freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.matmul(self.up.weight, self.down.weight)

    @property
    def bias(self) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        return self.up.bias

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        out = self.up(self.down(x))
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.lora_scaling
            out = out + lora_out
        return out

def copy_cur(self, weight: torch.Tensor, bias: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> None:

class GatedRecurrentMemory (d_model: int, chunk_size: int = 16)
Expand source code 
class GatedRecurrentMemory(nn.Module):
    """
    Gated recurrent memory with chunked parallel scan.

        g_t = sigmoid(W_g x_t + b_g)
        h_t = (1 - g_t) * h_{t-1} + g_t * tanh(W_x x_t + b_x)
        y_t = h_t

    Vectorized via chunked associative scan:
      - Intra-chunk: sequential (chunk_size steps)
      - Inter-chunk: parallel cumprod of (1-g) between chunks
    """

    def __init__(self, d_model: int, chunk_size: int = 16):
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.chunk_size = max(1, int(chunk_size))
        self.gate_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model, bias=True)
        self.value_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model, bias=True)
        # Start gate slightly open so memory doesn't dominate attention
        nn.init.constant_(self.gate_proj.bias, -1.0)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.value_proj.bias)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        # x: (B, L, D)
        target_dtype = x.dtype
        gw = self.gate_proj.weight.to(target_dtype)
        gb = self.gate_proj.bias.to(target_dtype) if self.gate_proj.bias is not None else None
        vw = self.value_proj.weight.to(target_dtype)
        vb = self.value_proj.bias.to(target_dtype) if self.value_proj.bias is not None else None

        g = torch.sigmoid(F.linear(x, gw, gb))   # (B, L, D)
        v = torch.tanh(F.linear(x, vw, vb))       # (B, L, D)
        B, L, D = x.shape

        if _env_flag("GOLDFINCH_DISABLE_RECURRENCE", False):
            # Ablation: return v (no recurrence)
            return v.to(target_dtype)

        if L <= self.chunk_size:
            # Short sequence: simple sequential
            h = torch.zeros(B, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
            outs = []
            for t in range(L):
                h = (1.0 - g[:, t]) * h + g[:, t] * v[:, t]
                outs.append(h)
            return torch.stack(outs, dim=1).to(target_dtype)

        # ---- Chunked parallel scan ----
        # Pad L to multiple of chunk_size
        pad = (-L) % self.chunk_size
        if pad > 0:
            g = F.pad(g, (0, 0, 0, pad))
            v = F.pad(v, (0, 0, 0, pad))
        Lp = L + pad
        n_chunks = Lp // self.chunk_size

        # Reshape to (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)
        g_c = g.view(B, n_chunks, self.chunk_size, D)
        v_c = v.view(B, n_chunks, self.chunk_size, D)

        # ---- Intra-chunk sequential ----
        # h_local[b, c, t] = recurrence within chunk c
        h_local = torch.zeros(B, n_chunks, self.chunk_size, D,
                              device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        h = torch.zeros(B, n_chunks, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        for t in range(self.chunk_size):
            h = (1.0 - g_c[:, :, t]) * h + g_c[:, :, t] * v_c[:, :, t]
            h_local[:, :, t] = h

        # ---- Inter-chunk scan ----
        # chunk_out[c] = h_local[c, -1]  (final state of chunk c)
        chunk_out = h_local[:, :, -1, :]  # (B, n_chunks, D)

        # Compute carry-in state for each chunk:
        # h_in[c] = sum_{c' < c} [prod_{c' < j < c} (1-g_chunk_j)] * chunk_out[c']
        # where g_chunk_j = g of last position in chunk j (approximation)
        g_chunk = g_c[:, :, -1, :]  # (B, n_chunks, D) -- last gate of each chunk

        # log-space cumprod for stability
        # one_minus_g[c] = 1 - g_chunk[c]
        omg = (1.0 - g_chunk).clamp(min=1e-6, max=1.0)  # (B, n_chunks, D)
        log_omg = torch.log(omg)

        # cum_log_omg[c] = sum_{j<=c} log(1-g_j)
        cum_log_omg = torch.cumsum(log_omg, dim=1)  # (B, n_chunks, D)

        # h_carry_in[c] = sum_{c' < c} exp(cum_log_omg[c] - cum_log_omg[c']) * chunk_out[c']
        # We use a sequential scan over chunks only (n_chunks ~ 8 for L=128, chunk=16)
        h_carry = torch.zeros(B, n_chunks, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        carry = torch.zeros(B, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        for c in range(n_chunks):
            h_carry[:, c] = carry
            carry = (1.0 - g_chunk[:, c]) * carry + chunk_out[:, c]

        # ---- Combine ----
        # Full output: h_full[b, c, t] = h_carry[b, c] * prod_{t' <= t}(1-g) + h_local[b, c, t]
        # The h_local already includes the intra-chunk recurrence from zero state.
        # To get the correct output, we need:
        #   h_full[c, t] = h_local[c, t] + h_carry[c] * prod_{t' <= t}(1 - g[c, t'])
        # Compute prod of (1-g) within chunk
        omg_local = (1.0 - g_c).clamp(min=1e-6, max=1.0)  # (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)
        log_omg_local = torch.log(omg_local)
        cum_log_omg_local = torch.cumsum(log_omg_local, dim=2)  # (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)
        prod_local = torch.exp(cum_log_omg_local)  # (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)

        # h_full = h_local + h_carry * prod_local
        h_full = h_local + h_carry.unsqueeze(2) * prod_local

        # Reshape back
        h_full = h_full.view(B, Lp, D)
        if pad > 0:
            h_full = h_full[:, :L, :]
        return h_full.to(target_dtype)

Gated recurrent memory with chunked parallel scan.

g_t = sigmoid(W_g x_t + b_g)
h_t = (1 - g_t) * h_{t-1} + g_t * tanh(W_x x_t + b_x)
y_t = h_t

Vectorized via chunked associative scan: - Intra-chunk: sequential (chunk_size steps) - Inter-chunk: parallel cumprod of (1-g) between chunks

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

Methods

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    # x: (B, L, D)
    target_dtype = x.dtype
    gw = self.gate_proj.weight.to(target_dtype)
    gb = self.gate_proj.bias.to(target_dtype) if self.gate_proj.bias is not None else None
    vw = self.value_proj.weight.to(target_dtype)
    vb = self.value_proj.bias.to(target_dtype) if self.value_proj.bias is not None else None

    g = torch.sigmoid(F.linear(x, gw, gb))   # (B, L, D)
    v = torch.tanh(F.linear(x, vw, vb))       # (B, L, D)
    B, L, D = x.shape

    if _env_flag("GOLDFINCH_DISABLE_RECURRENCE", False):
        # Ablation: return v (no recurrence)
        return v.to(target_dtype)

    if L <= self.chunk_size:
        # Short sequence: simple sequential
        h = torch.zeros(B, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        outs = []
        for t in range(L):
            h = (1.0 - g[:, t]) * h + g[:, t] * v[:, t]
            outs.append(h)
        return torch.stack(outs, dim=1).to(target_dtype)

    # ---- Chunked parallel scan ----
    # Pad L to multiple of chunk_size
    pad = (-L) % self.chunk_size
    if pad > 0:
        g = F.pad(g, (0, 0, 0, pad))
        v = F.pad(v, (0, 0, 0, pad))
    Lp = L + pad
    n_chunks = Lp // self.chunk_size

    # Reshape to (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)
    g_c = g.view(B, n_chunks, self.chunk_size, D)
    v_c = v.view(B, n_chunks, self.chunk_size, D)

    # ---- Intra-chunk sequential ----
    # h_local[b, c, t] = recurrence within chunk c
    h_local = torch.zeros(B, n_chunks, self.chunk_size, D,
                          device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
    h = torch.zeros(B, n_chunks, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
    for t in range(self.chunk_size):
        h = (1.0 - g_c[:, :, t]) * h + g_c[:, :, t] * v_c[:, :, t]
        h_local[:, :, t] = h

    # ---- Inter-chunk scan ----
    # chunk_out[c] = h_local[c, -1]  (final state of chunk c)
    chunk_out = h_local[:, :, -1, :]  # (B, n_chunks, D)

    # Compute carry-in state for each chunk:
    # h_in[c] = sum_{c' < c} [prod_{c' < j < c} (1-g_chunk_j)] * chunk_out[c']
    # where g_chunk_j = g of last position in chunk j (approximation)
    g_chunk = g_c[:, :, -1, :]  # (B, n_chunks, D) -- last gate of each chunk

    # log-space cumprod for stability
    # one_minus_g[c] = 1 - g_chunk[c]
    omg = (1.0 - g_chunk).clamp(min=1e-6, max=1.0)  # (B, n_chunks, D)
    log_omg = torch.log(omg)

    # cum_log_omg[c] = sum_{j<=c} log(1-g_j)
    cum_log_omg = torch.cumsum(log_omg, dim=1)  # (B, n_chunks, D)

    # h_carry_in[c] = sum_{c' < c} exp(cum_log_omg[c] - cum_log_omg[c']) * chunk_out[c']
    # We use a sequential scan over chunks only (n_chunks ~ 8 for L=128, chunk=16)
    h_carry = torch.zeros(B, n_chunks, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
    carry = torch.zeros(B, D, device=x.device, dtype=target_dtype)
    for c in range(n_chunks):
        h_carry[:, c] = carry
        carry = (1.0 - g_chunk[:, c]) * carry + chunk_out[:, c]

    # ---- Combine ----
    # Full output: h_full[b, c, t] = h_carry[b, c] * prod_{t' <= t}(1-g) + h_local[b, c, t]
    # The h_local already includes the intra-chunk recurrence from zero state.
    # To get the correct output, we need:
    #   h_full[c, t] = h_local[c, t] + h_carry[c] * prod_{t' <= t}(1 - g[c, t'])
    # Compute prod of (1-g) within chunk
    omg_local = (1.0 - g_c).clamp(min=1e-6, max=1.0)  # (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)
    log_omg_local = torch.log(omg_local)
    cum_log_omg_local = torch.cumsum(log_omg_local, dim=2)  # (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)
    prod_local = torch.exp(cum_log_omg_local)  # (B, n_chunks, chunk_size, D)

    # h_full = h_local + h_carry * prod_local
    h_full = h_local + h_carry.unsqueeze(2) * prod_local

    # Reshape back
    h_full = h_full.view(B, Lp, D)
    if pad > 0:
        h_full = h_full[:, :L, :]
    return h_full.to(target_dtype)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class GoldFinchAHNBlock (config: Any | None = None,

Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "goldfinch_ahn",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "goldfinch", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True
)
class GoldFinchAHNBlock(BaseAttention):
    """
    GoldFinch-AHN: hybrid sliding-window attention + gated recurrent memory.

    window_size: full window width in tokens (effective radius = window_size // 2).
                 Set to 0 or >= seq_len for full causal context (recommended for GPT-2).
    gate_init_bias: initial bias for per-head attention/memory gate.
                    Higher = more attention. 0.0 = balanced.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        d_model: int | Any | None = None,
        num_heads: int | None = None,
        window_size: int = 64,
        is_causal: bool = False,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        gate_init_bias: float = 1.8,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn

        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank if rank is not None else 64)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        # ---- hidden size ----
        if d_model is None:
            d_model = kwargs.get("d_model") or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or kwargs.get("dim")
            if d_model is None:
                if config is not None:
                    d_model = getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "dim", 768))
                elif source_attn is not None:
                    out_layer = None
                    for attr in ("out_lin", "dense", "c_proj", "o_proj", "out_proj"):
                        if hasattr(source_attn, attr):
                            out_layer = getattr(source_attn, attr)
                            break
                    if out_layer is not None:
                        d_model = getattr(out_layer, "out_features",
                                          getattr(out_layer, "in_features", 768))
                    else:
                        for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin"):
                            q_l = getattr(source_attn, attr, None)
                            if q_l is not None:
                                d_model = getattr(q_l, "in_features",
                                                  getattr(q_l, "out_features", 768))
                                break
                if d_model is None:
                    d_model = 768

        if hasattr(d_model, "hidden_size"):
            d_model = d_model.hidden_size

        # ---- num_heads ----
        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            if num_heads is None:
                if config is not None:
                    num_heads = getattr(config, "num_attention_heads",
                                        getattr(config, "n_heads", 12))
                elif source_attn is not None:
                    num_heads = getattr(source_attn, "num_attention_heads",
                                        getattr(source_attn, "n_heads", 12))
                else:
                    num_heads = 12

        self.dim = int(d_model)
        self.hidden_size = self.dim
        self.num_heads = max(1, int(num_heads))

        # ---- num_kv_heads ----
        if config is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = getattr(
                config, "num_key_value_heads",
                getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", kwargs.get("num_kv_heads", self.num_heads))
            )
        elif source_attn is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = (
                getattr(source_attn, "num_key_value_heads", None)
                or getattr(source_attn, "num_kv_heads", None)
                or kwargs.get("num_kv_heads", self.num_heads)
            )
        else:
            self.num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads", self.num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(int(self.num_kv_heads), self.num_heads))

        # ---- head_dim ----
        _head_dim = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) if config else None
        if _head_dim is None and source_attn is not None:
            _head_dim = getattr(source_attn, "head_dim", None)
        if _head_dim is None:
            _head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads
        self.head_dim = int(_head_dim)
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

        # ---- window (FIX 4: allow full context) ----
        # If GOLDFINCH_FULL_CONTEXT=1, window_radius = inf (full causal)
        full_ctx = _env_flag("GOLDFINCH_FULL_CONTEXT", False)
        if config is not None:
            self.window_size = int(
                getattr(config, "window_size",
                        getattr(comp_config, "window_size",
                                kwargs.get("window_size", window_size))
                        if comp_config is not None else window_size)
            )
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.window_size = int(getattr(comp_config, "window_size",
                                           kwargs.get("window_size", window_size)))
        else:
            self.window_size = int(kwargs.get("window_size", window_size))

        if full_ctx or self.window_size <= 0:
            self.window_radius = None  # sentinel: full context
        else:
            self.window_radius = max(1, self.window_size // 2)

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        model_type = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if config else ""
        is_dec = False
        if config is not None:
            is_dec = bool(getattr(config, "is_decoder", False)
                          or getattr(config, "is_causal", False))
        if any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in (
            "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi", "opt",
            "bloom", "falcon", "olmo", "mpt", "stablelm",
        )):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False) or is_causal:
            is_dec = True
        if source_attn is not None:
            if hasattr(source_attn, "c_attn") or getattr(source_attn, "is_decoder", False):
                is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # ---- LoRA config ----
        if config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(getattr(config, "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))
                if comp_config is not None else kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)))
            self.lora_alpha = float(getattr(config, "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
                if comp_config is not None else kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)))
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)))
            self.lora_alpha = float(getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)))
        else:
            self.lora_rank = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))
            self.lora_alpha = float(kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))

        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0
        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None
        )

        # ---- projections ----
        self.W_q = LoRALinear(self.dim, self.all_head_size, bias=True,
                              lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                              freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.W_k = LoRALinear(self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True,
                              lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                              freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.W_v = LoRALinear(self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True,
                              lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                              freeze_base=self.freeze_base)

        # Gated recurrent memory
        self.delta_mem = GatedRecurrentMemory(self.dim, chunk_size=16)

        # Per-head attention / memory gate (FIX 8: single output, no waste)
        # gate_init_bias can be overridden via env
        _gate_bias = float(os.environ.get("GOLDFINCH_GATE_BIAS", gate_init_bias))
        self.gate = nn.Linear(self.dim, 1)
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.gate.bias.data.fill_(_gate_bias)
            nn.init.zeros_(self.gate.weight)

        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(self.all_head_size, self.dim, bias=True,
                                   lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                                   freeze_base=self.freeze_base)

        dropout_p = 0.1
        if config is not None:
            dropout_p = float(getattr(config, "dropout",
                getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1)))
        elif comp_config is not None:
            dropout_p = float(getattr(comp_config, "dropout", 0.1))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # Single output scale, initialized to preserve pretrained signal
        self.output_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1))

        # Storage accounting
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ---- aliases ----
    @property
    def query(self): return self.W_q
    @property
    def key(self): return self.W_k
    @property
    def value(self): return self.W_v
    @property
    def q_proj(self): return self.W_q
    @property
    def k_proj(self): return self.W_k
    @property
    def v_proj(self): return self.W_v
    @property
    def q_lin(self): return self.W_q
    @property
    def k_lin(self): return self.W_k
    @property
    def v_lin(self): return self.W_v
    @property
    def out_lin(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def dense(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def o_proj(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def c_proj(self): return self.out_proj

    def freeze_base_weights(self):
        for m in (self.W_q, self.W_k, self.W_v, self.out_proj):
            if hasattr(m, "freeze_base_weights"):
                m.freeze_base_weights()
        # FIX 6: freeze delta_mem base weights if freeze_base
        if self.freeze_base:
            for p in self.delta_mem.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self):
        for m in (self.W_q, self.W_k, self.W_v, self.out_proj):
            if hasattr(m, "unfreeze_base_weights"):
                m.unfreeze_base_weights()
        for p in self.delta_mem.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights (unchanged, but with FIX 12 for int masks)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        try:
            for p in src.parameters():
                self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        cls_name = getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__
        has_out = any(hasattr(src, a) for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj"))
        is_nested = False
        if (
            cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention",
                         "XLMRobertaSelfAttention", "ElectraSelfAttention",
                         "DebertaSelfAttention")
            or (hasattr(src, "query") and not has_out)
            or (hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src, "output"))
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted = {"q": (None, None), "k": (None, None),
                     "v": (None, None), "o": (None, None)}

        # GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    "Conv1D" in c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__
                    or "Conv1D" in base_c.__class__.__name__
                    or hasattr(c_attn_mod, "nf") or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (c_w[:split], c_b[:split] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (c_w[split:2*split], c_b[split:2*split] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["v"] = (c_w[2*split:], c_b[2*split:] if c_b is not None else None)

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        "Conv1D" in c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__
                        or "Conv1D" in base_o.__class__.__name__
                        or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf") or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (o_w, bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None)

        # Nested (BERT / RoBERTa)
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                    extracted[key] = (m.weight.data.clone(),
                                      b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                d = src.output.dense
                b = getattr(d, "bias", None)
                extracted["o"] = (d.weight.data.clone(),
                                  b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)

        # Flat
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query", "W_q"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key", "W_k"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value", "W_v"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in candidates.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        m = getattr(src, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[key] = (m.weight.data.clone(),
                                              b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [k for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
                   if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Goldfinch UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [k for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
                         if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning("Goldfinch copy_weights: missing %s; using initialized weights",
                           missing_after)

        target_map = {"q": self.W_q, "k": self.W_k, "v": self.W_v, "o": self.out_proj}

        with torch.no_grad():
            for key, target in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[key]
                if w is None:
                    if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target.weight)
                        if target.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)
                if (w.shape != target.weight.shape
                        and w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()

                out_d = min(target.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                target.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype)
                )

                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    mb = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))
                elif target.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)

                w_norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target.weight.data.float()).item())
                if w_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning("Goldfinch copy_weights: '%s' has near-zero norm (%.2e)",
                                   key, w_norm)

                if self.freeze_base:
                    target.freeze_base_weights()

        # Track compressed storage (FIX 9: include output_scale and gate)
        try:
            for m in (self.W_q, self.W_k, self.W_v, self.out_proj, self.delta_mem, self.gate):
                for p in m.parameters():
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
            self._comp_storage_bytes += self.output_scale.numel() * self.output_scale.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if self.freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    @classmethod
    def from_distilbert_pretrained(cls, old_attn, model_config=None, **kwargs):
        return cls(config=model_config, pretrained_attn=old_attn, **kwargs)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Mask helper (FIX 7: support more shapes)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device):
        if attention_mask is None:
            return None

        def _to_bool(t):
            if t.dtype == torch.bool:
                return t
            if t.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return t > -1e4
            return t > 0

        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask)
            if m.shape == (B, L):
                return m
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            # (B, 1, L) or (B, L, L)
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask)
            if m.shape[1] == 1:
                m = m[:, 0, :]
            else:
                m = m.any(dim=1)
            if m.shape == (B, L):
                return m
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask)
            m = m.any(dim=-2)  # (B, H, L)
            m = m.any(dim=1)   # (B, L)
            if m.shape == (B, L):
                return m

        try:
            m = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)
            if isinstance(m, torch.Tensor) and m.shape == (B, L):
                return m.bool()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("GoldFinchAHN does not support cross-attention.")

        x = hidden_states
        if not x.is_contiguous():
            x = x.contiguous()
        B, L, D = x.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = x.device
        dtype_in = x.dtype

        # ---- QKV ----
        q = self.W_q(x).view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = self.W_k(x).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.W_v(x).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
            if pos_ids is None:
                pos_ids = torch.arange(L, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            try:
                cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(k, seq_len=L)
            except TypeError:
                try:
                    cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(k, pos_ids)
                except Exception:
                    cos, sin = None, None
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        # ---- GQA ----
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---- Sliding-window attention (FIX 2: SDPA fast-path) ----
        # Build additive bool mask
        idx = torch.arange(L, device=device)
        if self.window_radius is None:
            # Full context
            band = torch.ones(L, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)
        else:
            diff = (idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0)).abs()
            band = diff <= self.window_radius

        if self.is_causal:
            causal = idx.unsqueeze(1) >= idx.unsqueeze(0)
            valid_local = causal & band
        else:
            valid_local = band

        valid_mask = self._normalize_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)
        if valid_mask is not None:
            # combine padding mask: (B, 1, 1, L)
            pad_mask = valid_mask[:, None, None, :]
            valid_local_4d = valid_local.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0) & pad_mask
        else:
            valid_local_4d = valid_local.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        # SDPA fast-path: use additive bool mask
        # SDPA requires attn_mask of shape (L, L) or (B, H, L, L) with bool or float
        try:
            # Try SDPA with bool mask (True = keep, False = mask)
            if valid_local_4d.dtype == torch.bool:
                # Expand to (B, H, L, L) for SDPA
                if valid_local_4d.shape[0] == 1 and B > 1:
                    attn_mask = valid_local_4d.expand(B, H, L, L)
                elif valid_local_4d.shape[0] == B and valid_local_4d.shape[1] == 1:
                    attn_mask = valid_local_4d.expand(B, H, L, L)
                else:
                    attn_mask = valid_local_4d
                short_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    q, k, v,
                    attn_mask=attn_mask,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                    is_causal=False,  # already encoded in mask
                )
            else:
                raise RuntimeError("mask not bool")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("Goldfinch SDPA failed (%s); using manual path.", e)
            # Fallback: manual matmul with additive mask
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * (d ** -0.5)
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_local_4d, -1e4)
            # FIX 5: head_mask BEFORE softmax
            if head_mask is not None:
                hm = head_mask
                if hm.dim() == 1:
                    hm = hm.view(1, H, 1, 1)
                elif hm.dim() == 2:
                    hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], H, 1, 1)
                elif hm.dim() == 3:
                    hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
                scores = scores * hm.float()
            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype_in)
            attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
            attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)
            short_out = torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)

        short_out = short_out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)

        # ---- Recurrent memory ----
        long_out = self.delta_mem(x).to(dtype_in)  # (B, L, D)

        # ---- Gated fusion (FIX 8: gate is (B, L, 1)) ----
        gate_w = self.gate.weight.to(dtype_in)
        gate_b = self.gate.bias.to(dtype_in) if self.gate.bias is not None else None
        gate = torch.sigmoid(F.linear(x, gate_w, gate_b)).to(dtype_in)  # (B, L, 1)

        # Ensure long_out matches all_head_size
        if long_out.shape[-1] != self.all_head_size:
            if long_out.shape[-1] < self.all_head_size:
                long_out = F.pad(long_out, (0, self.all_head_size - long_out.shape[-1]))
            else:
                long_out = long_out[..., :self.all_head_size]

        fused = (gate * short_out.to(dtype_in) + (1.0 - gate) * long_out).to(dtype_in)

        if valid_mask is not None:
            fused = fused * valid_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(fused.dtype)

        # ---- Output projection ----
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = fused
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(fused)
            out = (out * self.output_scale.to(out.dtype)).to(out.dtype)

        return out.to(dtype_in), None  # attentions not returned (SDPA doesn't expose)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        def _sz(p):
            if isinstance(p, (nn.Parameter, torch.Tensor)):
                return p.numel() * p.element_size()
            return 0

        out = {}
        for name, m in (
            ("W_q", self.W_q), ("W_k", self.W_k), ("W_v", self.W_v),
            ("out_proj", self.out_proj), ("delta_mem", self.delta_mem),
            ("gate", self.gate),
        ):
            out[name] = sum(_sz(p) for p in m.parameters())
        out["output_scale"] = _sz(self.output_scale)
        out["total"] = sum(out.values())
        return out

GoldFinch-AHN: hybrid sliding-window attention + gated recurrent memory.

window_size: full window width in tokens (effective radius = window_size // 2). Set to 0 or >= seq_len for full causal context (recommended for GPT-2). gate_init_bias: initial bias for per-head attention/memory gate. Higher = more attention. 0.0 = balanced.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

            m.freeze_base_weights()
