def unfreeze_base_weights(self)
Expand source code 
def unfreeze_base_weights(self):
    for m in (self.W_q, self.W_k, self.W_v, self.out_proj):
        if hasattr(m, "unfreeze_base_weights"):
            m.unfreeze_base_weights()
    for p in self.delta_mem.parameters():
        p.requires_grad = True

Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("griffin_ssd")
class GriffinSSDBlock(BaseAttention):
    """Griffin Hybrid Block: local window + SSD linear attention."""

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        window_size: int = 64,
        d_model: int | Any | None = None,
        num_heads: int | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        def _get(k: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
            for obj in (comp_config, config, getattr(config, "compression_config", None)):
                if obj is not None:
                    if isinstance(obj, dict) and k in obj and obj[k] is not None:
                        return obj[k]
                    elif hasattr(obj, k):
                        val = getattr(obj, k)
                        if val is not None:
                            return val
            return kwargs.get(k, default)

        if d_model is None:
            d_model = _get("hidden_size", _get("d_model", _get("n_embd", _get("dim", None))))
        if d_model is None and old_attn is not None:
            d_model = (
                getattr(old_attn, "hidden_size", None)
                or getattr(old_attn, "embed_dim", None)
                or getattr(old_attn, "all_head_size", None)
            )
            if d_model is None:
                for cand in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "c_attn"):
                    m = getattr(old_attn, cand, None)
                    if m is not None and hasattr(m, "in_features"):
                        d_model = m.in_features
                        break
        if d_model is None:
            d_model = kwargs.get("hidden_size", kwargs.get("d_model", kwargs.get("dim", 768)))

        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = (
                _get("num_attention_heads", None) or _get("num_heads", None)
                or _get("n_heads", None) or _get("n_head", None)
            )
        if num_heads is None and old_attn is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_attention_heads", None))
        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_heads", 12))

        self.dim = int(d_model)
        self.num_heads = int(num_heads)

        resolved_head_dim = _get("head_dim", None)
        if resolved_head_dim is None and old_attn is not None:
            resolved_head_dim = getattr(old_attn, "head_dim", None)
            if resolved_head_dim is None:
                for q_cand in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q"):
                    m = getattr(old_attn, q_cand, None)
                    if m is not None and hasattr(m, "out_features"):
                        resolved_head_dim = m.out_features // max(1, self.num_heads)
                        break
        if resolved_head_dim is None:
            resolved_head_dim = self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads)
        self.head_dim = int(resolved_head_dim)

        kv_heads = _get("num_key_value_heads", None)
        if kv_heads is None and old_attn is not None:
            kv_heads = getattr(
                old_attn, "num_key_value_heads",
                getattr(old_attn, "num_kv_heads", getattr(old_attn, "n_kv_heads", None)),
            )
            if (kv_heads is None and hasattr(old_attn, "k_proj")
                    and hasattr(old_attn.k_proj, "out_features") and self.head_dim > 0):
                kv_heads = old_attn.k_proj.out_features // self.head_dim
        self.num_kv_heads = int(kv_heads if kv_heads is not None else self.num_heads)

        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.window_size = int(_get("window_size", kwargs.get("window_size", window_size)))

        model_type = str(_get("model_type", "")).lower()
        is_dec = _get("is_decoder", _get("is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))))
        if (any(model_type.startswith(pfx) for pfx in (
                "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "bloom", "opt", "falcon", "codegen"
            ))
            or (hasattr(config, "architectures") and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
            ))):
            is_dec = True
        if old_attn is not None and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder

        is_nested = False
        if old_attn is not None:
            src_cls = old_attn.__class__.__name__
            if (src_cls in (
                    "BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention", "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention", "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (src_cls.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in src_cls.lower())
                or (hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"))):
                is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        self.lora_rank = int(_get("lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(_get("lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", lora_alpha)))
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.W_q = LoRALinear(self.dim, self.all_head_size, bias=False,
                              lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                              freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.W_k = LoRALinear(self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=False,
                              lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                              freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.W_v = LoRALinear(self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=False,
                              lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                              freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.ssd_proj = LoRALinear(self.dim, self.all_head_size, bias=False,
                                   lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                                   freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(self.all_head_size, self.dim, bias=True,
                                   lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                                   freeze_base=self.freeze_base)

        _decay_init = _env_float("GRIFFIN_DECAY_INIT", 2.0)
        self.decay_param = nn.Parameter(torch.full((self.num_heads, 1, 1), _decay_init))

        _gate_init = _env_float("GRIFFIN_GATE_INIT", 0.0)
        self.fusion_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.full((self.num_heads,), _gate_init))

        self.rotary_emb = getattr(old_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if old_attn is not None else None
        self.comp_config = comp_config

        if old_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(old_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        if old_attn is None:
            return

        extracted = {"q": (None, None), "k": (None, None), "v": (None, None), "o": (None, None)}

        try:
            adapter = UniversalAdapter()
            for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
                w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
            c_w, c_b = _unpack_weight_bias(old_attn.c_attn)
            if c_w is not None:
                if c_w.dim() == 2 and c_w.shape[1] == 3 * self.dim:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                extracted["q"] = (c_w[:split_size, :], c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (c_w[split_size:2*split_size, :], c_b[split_size:2*split_size] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["v"] = (c_w[2*split_size:, :], c_b[2*split_size:] if c_b is not None else None)
            if hasattr(old_attn, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
                o_w, o_b = _unpack_weight_bias(old_attn.c_proj)
                if o_w is not None:
                    if o_w.dim() == 2 and o_w.shape[0] != self.dim and o_w.shape[1] == self.dim:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    extracted["o"] = (o_w, o_b)
        elif hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn.self, "query"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
            s = old_attn.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                extracted[proj_key] = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(s, sub_name, None))
            if hasattr(old_attn, "output") and hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                extracted["o"] = _unpack_weight_bias(old_attn.output.dense)
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
                "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
                "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
                "o": ["out_proj", "out_lin", "o_proj", "dense"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in candidates.items():
                if extracted[proj_key][0] is None:
                    for name in names:
                        if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                            cw, cb = _unpack_weight_bias(getattr(old_attn, name))
                            if cw is not None:
                                extracted[proj_key] = (cw, cb)
                                break

        target_map = {"q": self.W_q, "k": self.W_k, "v": self.W_v, "o": self.out_proj}
        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    continue
                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                      and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and getattr(target_mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                          and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))

            if extracted["q"][0] is not None:
                w_q = extracted["q"][0]
                if w_q.shape == self.ssd_proj.weight.shape:
                    self.ssd_proj.weight.copy_(w_q * 0.5)
                elif w_q.t().contiguous().shape == self.ssd_proj.weight.shape:
                    self.ssd_proj.weight.copy_(w_q.t().contiguous() * 0.5)

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.W_q, self.W_k, self.W_v, self.ssd_proj, self.out_proj]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def _ssd_scan_chunked(
        self,
        q: torch.Tensor,
        k: torch.Tensor,
        v: torch.Tensor,
        decay: torch.Tensor,
        mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        chunk_size: int = 32,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Chunked parallel scan for causal SSD.
        
        Mathematical equivalence:
            y_t = q_t @ state_t
            state_t = decay * state_{t-1} + k_t^T @ v_t
        
        Chunked decomposition:
            y_t = q_t @ state_{chunk_start} * decay^(t - chunk_start) 
                + sum_{j=chunk_start}^{t} decay^(t-j) * (q_t @ k_j^T) @ v_j
        """
        B, H, L, d = q.shape
        target_dtype = q.dtype

        # Symmetric normalization (FIX 7)
        q_n = F.normalize(q.float(), p=2, dim=-1)               # (B, H, L, d)
        k_n = F.normalize(k.float(), p=2, dim=-1) / math.sqrt(d) # (B, H, L, d)
        v_f = v.float()                                          # (B, H, L, d)

        # decay: (1, H, 1, 1)
        decay_f = decay.to(torch.float32).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        # log decay for stable exponentiation
        log_decay = torch.log(decay_f.clamp(min=1e-6, max=1.0 - 1e-6))  # (1, H, 1, 1)

        # ---- Pad to multiple of chunk_size ----
        chunk_size = min(chunk_size, L)
        pad_len = (chunk_size - (L % chunk_size)) % chunk_size
        Lp = L + pad_len
        if pad_len > 0:
            q_n = F.pad(q_n, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            k_n = F.pad(k_n, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))
            v_f = F.pad(v_f, (0, 0, 0, pad_len))

        n_chunks = Lp // chunk_size

        # ---- Reshape to (B, H, n_chunks, chunk_size, d) ----
        q_c = q_n.view(B, H, n_chunks, chunk_size, d)
        k_c = k_n.view(B, H, n_chunks, chunk_size, d)
        v_c = v_f.view(B, H, n_chunks, chunk_size, d)

        # ---- Intra-chunk: parallel attention within each chunk with decay ----
        # Positions within chunk
        pos = torch.arange(chunk_size, device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        # diff[i,j] = i - j (causal: i >= j)
        diff = pos.unsqueeze(0) - pos.unsqueeze(1)  # (C, C)
        causal = diff >= 0  # (C, C) bool

        # D_intra[i,j] = decay^(i-j) if i>=j else 0
        # log_decay shape: (1, H, 1, 1) → need (1, H, 1, 1, 1) for broadcasting
        log_decay_5d = log_decay.view(1, H, 1, 1, 1)
        diff_5d = diff.view(1, 1, 1, chunk_size, chunk_size)  # (1,1,1,C,C)
        exp_arg = (diff_5d * log_decay_5d).clamp(min=-40.0, max=0.0)  # (1,H,1,C,C)
        D_intra = torch.exp(exp_arg) * causal.view(1, 1, 1, chunk_size, chunk_size).float()

        # scores_intra[b,h,c,i,j] = q_c[b,h,c,i] · k_c[b,h,c,j]
        # q_c: (B,H,nc,C,d), k_c^T: (B,H,nc,d,C) → scores: (B,H,nc,C,C)
        scores_intra = torch.matmul(q_c, k_c.transpose(-1, -2))  # (B,H,nc,C,C)
        scores_intra = scores_intra * D_intra  # broadcast (1,H,1,C,C) → (B,H,nc,C,C) ✓

        # y_intra[b,h,c] = scores_intra[b,h,c] @ v_c[b,h,c]
        y_intra = torch.matmul(scores_intra, v_c)  # (B,H,nc,C,d)

        # ---- Inter-chunk: parallel scan over n_chunks ----
        # Each chunk's final state: state_after_chunk[c] = decay^C * state_before + sum of kv in chunk
        # Contribution of chunk c to next chunks: k_v_chunk[c] = sum_{i=0}^{C-1} decay^(C-1-i) * k_c[i]^T @ v_c[i]
        # Actually simpler: state contribution = k_c^T @ v_c (matrix) with proper decay weights
        
        # For chunk c, kv_contrib[c] = sum_{i=0}^{C-1} decay^(C-1-i) * (k_c[i]^T @ v_c[i])
        # decay^j where j = C-1-i
        pos_in_chunk = torch.arange(chunk_size, device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        # decay_weights[i] = decay^(C-1-i)
        decay_in_chunk = torch.exp(
            ((chunk_size - 1 - pos_in_chunk).view(1, 1, 1, chunk_size, 1) * log_decay_5d).clamp(min=-40.0, max=0.0)
        )  # (1,H,1,C,1)

        # k_c: (B,H,nc,C,d), v_c: (B,H,nc,C,d)
        # weighted k: (B,H,nc,C,d) * (1,H,1,C,1) → (B,H,nc,C,d)
        k_weighted = k_c * decay_in_chunk  # (B,H,nc,C,d)
        
        # kv_contrib[c] = k_weighted[c]^T @ v_c[c]
        # (B,H,nc,d,C) @ (B,H,nc,C,d) → (B,H,nc,d,d)
        kv_contrib = torch.matmul(k_weighted.transpose(-1, -2), v_c)  # (B,H,nc,d,d)

        # Now parallel scan over chunks with chunk_decay = decay^chunk_size
        chunk_decay = torch.exp((log_decay_5d.squeeze(-1).squeeze(-1) * chunk_size).clamp(min=-40.0, max=0.0))  # (1,H,1,1)
        chunk_decay = chunk_decay.squeeze(-1).squeeze(-1).view(1, H, 1, 1)  # (1,H,1,1)

        # states_before[c] = state before processing chunk c
        # states_before[0] = 0
        # states_before[c] = chunk_decay * states_before[c-1] + kv_contrib[c-1]
        states_before = torch.zeros(B, H, n_chunks, d, d, device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        running = torch.zeros(B, H, d, d, device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        for c in range(n_chunks):
            states_before[:, :, c] = running
            running = running * chunk_decay + kv_contrib[:, :, c]

        # ---- Inter-chunk output ----
        # y_inter[b,h,c,i] = q_c[b,h,c,i] @ states_before[b,h,c] * decay^(i+1)
        # Actually: q at position i in chunk c sees state_before[c] with decay^(i+1)?
        # Let's be precise: 
        # state_before[c] = sum_{c'<c} decay^(chunk_size * (c-1-c')) * kv_contrib[c']
        # For query at position i in chunk c, the contribution from past chunks is:
        #   q_i @ state_before[c] * decay^(i) — the (i) accounts for positions within current chunk
        # Wait, more carefully:
        # state after processing chunk c-1 is states_before[c]
        # At position i in chunk c, we've had i decay steps since start of chunk
        # state_at_position_i = decay^i * state_before[c] + sum_{j=0}^{i-1} decay^(i-1-j) * k[c,j]^T v[c,j]
        # The first term: query sees decay^i * state_before[c]
        # The second term is captured by y_intra
        
        # query positions: pos (C,)
        # decay^i for i in [0, C-1]
        q_decay = torch.exp((pos.view(1, 1, 1, chunk_size, 1) * log_decay_5d).clamp(min=-40.0, max=0.0))  # (1,H,1,C,1)

        # q_c: (B,H,nc,C,d), states_before: (B,H,nc,d,d)
        # q_c @ states_before: (B,H,nc,C,d)
        q_state = torch.matmul(q_c, states_before)  # (B,H,nc,C,d)
        y_inter = q_state * q_decay  # (1,H,1,C,1) broadcast → (B,H,nc,C,d) ✓

        # ---- Combine ----
        y = y_intra + y_inter  # (B,H,nc,C,d)
        y = y.view(B, H, Lp, d)[:, :, :L, :]

        # Apply mask to query output (FIX 6)
        if mask is not None:
            m = mask.view(B, 1, L, 1).float()
            y = y * m

        return torch.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0).to(target_dtype)
    def _ssd_symmetric(self, q, k, v, decay, mask=None):
        """Bidirectional symmetric SSD for encoders."""
        B, H, L, d = q.shape
        target_dtype = q.dtype

        q_n = F.normalize(q.float(), p=2, dim=-1)
        k_n = F.normalize(k.float(), p=2, dim=-1) / math.sqrt(d)

        decay_f = decay.to(torch.float32).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        log_decay = torch.log(decay_f.clamp(min=1e-6, max=1.0 - 1e-6))

        pos = torch.arange(L, device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        dist = torch.abs(pos.unsqueeze(0) - pos.unsqueeze(1))  # (L, L)
        D_sym = torch.exp((dist.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0) * log_decay).clamp(min=-40.0, max=0.0))

        if mask is not None:
            m = mask.view(B, 1, L, 1).to(D_sym.dtype)
            D_sym = D_sym * m.transpose(-1, -2) * m

        scores = torch.matmul(q_n, k_n.transpose(-1, -2)) * D_sym
        y = torch.matmul(scores, v.float())

        if mask is not None:
            m = mask.view(B, 1, L, 1).to(y.dtype)
            y = y * m

        return torch.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0).to(target_dtype)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            return hidden_states, None

        mask = kwargs.get("mask")
        if attention_mask is None and mask is not None:
            attention_mask = mask

        while isinstance(hidden_states, (tuple, list)):
            hidden_states = hidden_states[0]

        if not hidden_states.is_contiguous():
            hidden_states = hidden_states.contiguous()

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim

        q = self.W_q(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().to(target_dtype)
        k = (self.W_k(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, d)
             .transpose(1, 2).contiguous().to(target_dtype))
        v = (self.W_v(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, d)
             .transpose(1, 2).contiguous().to(target_dtype))
        q_ssd = (self.ssd_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, d)
                 .transpose(1, 2).contiguous().to(target_dtype))

        pos_emb = position_embeddings if position_embeddings is not None else kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
        if pos_emb is not None:
            if isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
                cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            else:
                cos, sin = pos_emb, None
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            if pos_ids is None:
                pos_ids = torch.arange(L, device=hidden_states.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            cos, sin = None, None
            try:
                cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(q, seq_len=L)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(q, pos_ids)
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(q)
                    except Exception:
                        cos, sin = None, None
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            k = repeat_kv(k, repeats)
            v = repeat_kv(v, repeats)

        # Local sliding window
        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()) / math.sqrt(d)
        idx = torch.arange(L, device=hidden_states.device)
        dist = torch.abs(idx.unsqueeze(1) - idx.unsqueeze(0))
        window_mask = dist > self.window_size
        scores = scores.masked_fill(window_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -10000.0)

        if self.is_decoder:
            causal = torch.triu(torch.ones(L, L, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=torch.bool), diagonal=1)
            scores = scores.masked_fill(causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -10000.0)

        mask_bool = None
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                mask_bool = attention_mask.bool()
                pad_mask = (attention_mask == 0).view(B, 1, 1, L)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(pad_mask, -10000.0)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                scores = scores + attention_mask.to(scores.dtype)

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-10000.0, posinf=10000.0, neginf=-10000.0)
        local_attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(target_dtype)
        local_attn = torch.nan_to_num(local_attn, nan=0.0)
        local_out = torch.matmul(local_attn, v).to(target_dtype)

        # Global SSD
        if _env_flag("GRIFFIN_DISABLE_SSD", False):
            global_out = torch.zeros_like(local_out)
        else:
            decay = torch.sigmoid(self.decay_param.to(target_dtype)).view(1, H, 1, 1)
            if self.is_decoder:
                global_out = self._ssd_scan_chunked(q_ssd, k, v, decay, mask_bool, chunk_size=32)
            else:
                global_out = self._ssd_symmetric(q_ssd, k, v, decay, mask_bool)

        # Fusion gate
        gate = torch.sigmoid(self.fusion_gate).view(1, H, 1, 1).to(target_dtype)
        fused = (gate * local_out + (1.0 - gate) * global_out).to(target_dtype)

        fused = fused.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size).to(target_dtype)
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = fused
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(fused).to(target_dtype)

        if out.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            if out.shape[-1] < hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                out = F.pad(out, (0, hidden_states.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
            else:
                out = out[..., : hidden_states.shape[-1]]

        if attention_mask is not None and mask_bool is not None:
            out = out * mask_bool.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)

        return out.to(target_dtype), None

Griffin Hybrid Block: local window + SSD linear attention.

class HeadGate (hidden_dim: int, n_heads: int)
Expand source code 
class HeadGate(nn.Module):
    r"""Learned per-example head importance scalars with masked pooling."""

    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int, n_heads: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 32), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(32, n_heads))
        # Initialize head gate to neutral 1.0 (bias=3.0 -> sigmoid(3.0) ≈ 0.95)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].weight)
        nn.init.constant_(self.net[-1].bias, 3.0)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> torch.Tensor:
        net_dtype = next(self.net.parameters()).dtype
        pooled = _masked_mean(x, valid_mask).to(net_dtype)
        return torch.sigmoid(self.net(pooled)).to(x.dtype)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class HeadRoPE (head_dim: int, max_seq_len: int = 4096, base: float = 10000.0)
Expand source code 
class HeadRoPE(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, head_dim: int, max_seq_len: int = 4096, base: float = 10000.0) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.max_seq_len = max_seq_len
        inv_freq = 1.0 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2).float() / head_dim))
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        self._build_cache(max_seq_len)

    def _build_cache(self, seq_len: int) -> None:
        t = torch.arange(seq_len, device=self.inv_freq.device).float()
        freqs = torch.einsum("i,j->ij", t, self.inv_freq)
        emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)
        self.register_buffer("cos_cached", emb.cos().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(1), persistent=False)
        self.register_buffer("sin_cached", emb.sin().unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(1), persistent=False)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, past_len: int = 0) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, L, d = x.shape
        if past_len + L > self.max_seq_len:
            self._build_cache(past_len + L)
            self.max_seq_len = past_len + L

        cos = self.cos_cached[:, :, past_len : past_len + L, :d]
        sin = self.sin_cached[:, :, past_len : past_len + L, :d]

        x_even = x[..., 0::2]
        x_odd = x[..., 1::2]
        rot_even = x_even * cos[..., 0::2] - x_odd * sin[..., 0::2]
        rot_odd = x_even * sin[..., 0::2] + x_odd * cos[..., 0::2]
        return torch.stack((rot_even, rot_odd), dim=-1).flatten(-2)

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "hymba_transx_attn", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "hymba_transx", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True
)
class HymbaTransXBlock(BaseAttention):
    r"""HymbaTransX: Hybrid Differential Transformer & State Space Model Block."""

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        num_heads: int | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size")
        num_heads = kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
        if hidden_size is None and config is not None:
            hidden_size = getattr(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "dim",
                    getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "n_embd", None)),
                ),
            )
        if hidden_size is None and source_attn is not None:
            for attr in ["hidden_size", "embed_dim", "dim", "d_model"]:
                if hasattr(source_attn, attr) and getattr(source_attn, attr) is not None:
                    hidden_size = getattr(source_attn, attr)
                    break
            if hidden_size is None:
                out_layer = getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "out_lin",
                    getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "dense",
                        getattr(source_attn, "c_proj", getattr(source_attn, "o_proj", None)),
                    ),
                )
                hidden_size = (
                    getattr(out_layer, "out_features", None) if out_layer is not None else None
                )
        hidden_size = int(hidden_size or 768)

        if num_heads is None and config is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                getattr(config, "n_heads", getattr(config, "num_heads", None)),
            )
        if num_heads is None and source_attn is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "num_attention_heads",
                getattr(source_attn, "n_heads", getattr(source_attn, "num_heads", None)),
            )
        num_heads = int(num_heads or (hidden_size // 64 if hidden_size % 64 == 0 else 12))

        self.dim = hidden_size
        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        cfg_kv = (
            getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
            if config
            else None
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv) if cfg_kv is not None else self.num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))

        cfg_head_dim = (
            getattr(config, "head_dim", kwargs.get("head_dim"))
            if config
            else kwargs.get("head_dim")
        )
        if cfg_head_dim is None and source_attn is not None:
            for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin", "W_q1"):
                mod = getattr(source_attn, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    cfg_head_dim = mod.weight.shape[0] // max(1, self.num_heads)
                    break
        self.head_dim = (
            int(cfg_head_dim) if cfg_head_dim is not None else self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads)
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
        self.supports_gqa = True

        # Decoder detection
        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(config, is_decoder, source_attn)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        # HX-FIX-02: RoPE only for models that actually use it
        self.use_rope = _uses_rope(config, source_attn)

        # LoRA config
        self.lora_rank = int(
            kwargs.get(
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(
                    comp_config,
                    "lora_rank",
                    getattr(config, "lora_rank", 16) if config else 16,
                ),
            )
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            kwargs.get(
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(
                    comp_config,
                    "lora_alpha",
                    getattr(config, "lora_alpha", 32.0) if config else 32.0,
                ),
            )
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # RoPE and projections
        self.rope = HeadRoPE(self.head_dim) if self.use_rope else None

        self.W_q1 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_k1 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_q2 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_k2 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_v = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # HX-FIX-06: W_q2 and W_k2 are EXACT copies (no noise)
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.W_q2.weight.data.copy_(self.W_q1.weight.data)
            self.W_k2.weight.data.copy_(self.W_k1.weight.data)

        # HX-FIX-07: diff_lambda init = -2.0 (sigmoid ≈ 0.12)
        self.diff_lambda = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-2.0))

        # HX-FIX-13: SSM branch DISABLED by default for GPT-2 decoder
        self.mamba = None
        use_ssm = not _env_flag("HYMBA_DISABLE_SSM", False)
        if use_ssm and config is not None:
            mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "")).lower()
            # Only enable SSM for models where it makes sense (not GPT-2)
            if not mt.startswith("gpt"):
                try:
                    from neuralops.attentions.simple_ssm import SimpleSSM

                    self.mamba = SimpleSSM(
                        d_model=self.dim, config=config, is_decoder=self.is_decoder
                    )
                except ImportError:
                    logger.warning("HymbaTransX: SimpleSSM not available; SSM branch disabled.")
        if self.mamba is None:
            logger.info("HymbaTransX: SSM branch disabled (GPT-2 or unavailable).")

        # HX-FIX-01: alpha init = 4.0 → sigmoid(4.0) ≈ 0.98 (attention-dominant)
        self.alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(4.0))

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Aliases
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.W_q1

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.W_k1

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.W_v

    @property
    def q_proj(self):
        return self.W_q1

    @property
    def k_proj(self):
        return self.W_k1

    @property
    def v_proj(self):
        return self.W_v

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        if hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = False
        elif (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or cls_name
            in (
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                "ElectraSelfAttention",
                "DebertaSelfAttention",
            )
            or cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention")
            and "distil" not in cls_name.lower()
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
        else:
            self.is_nested_encoder = False

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                cls_c = c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__
                base_cls_c = base_c.__class__.__name__
                if (
                    "Conv1D" in cls_c
                    or "Conv1D" in base_cls_c
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[1] == 3 * self.dim)
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split, :],
                    c_b[:split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split : 2 * split, :],
                    c_b[split : 2 * split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    cls_o = c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__
                    base_cls_o = base_o.__class__.__name__
                    if (
                        "Conv1D" in cls_o
                        or "Conv1D" in base_cls_o
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] != self.dim and o_w.shape[1] == self.dim)
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa / BERT nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                mod = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # 4. UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q, _ = extracted["q"]
            w_k, _ = extracted["k"]
            w_v, _ = extracted["v"]
            w_o, b_o = extracted["o"]

            def _adapt(w, target_shape):
                if w is None:
                    return None
                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.shape[0], -1)
                if w.shape == target_shape:
                    return w
                if w.t().contiguous().shape == target_shape:
                    return w.t().contiguous()
                # Truncate / pad
                out_d, in_d = target_shape
                s_out, s_in = w.shape
                m_out = min(out_d, s_out)
                m_in = min(in_d, s_in)
                padded = w.new_zeros(target_shape)
                padded[:m_out, :m_in] = w[:m_out, :m_in]
                return padded

            # W_q1
            wq = _adapt(w_q, self.W_q1.weight.shape)
            if wq is not None:
                self.W_q1.weight.data.copy_(
                    wq.to(device=self.W_q1.weight.device, dtype=self.W_q1.weight.dtype)
                )

            # W_k1
            wk = _adapt(w_k, self.W_k1.weight.shape)
            if wk is not None:
                self.W_k1.weight.data.copy_(
                    wk.to(device=self.W_k1.weight.device, dtype=self.W_k1.weight.dtype)
                )

            # W_v
            wv = _adapt(w_v, self.W_v.weight.shape)
            if wv is not None:
                self.W_v.weight.data.copy_(
                    wv.to(device=self.W_v.weight.device, dtype=self.W_v.weight.dtype)
                )

            # W_o
            if w_o is not None:
                wo = _adapt(w_o, self.out_proj.weight.shape)
                if wo is not None:
                    self.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(
                        wo.to(
                            device=self.out_proj.weight.device,
                            dtype=self.out_proj.weight.dtype,
                        )
                    )
                if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    m = min(self.out_proj.bias.numel(), b_o.numel())
                    self.out_proj.bias.data.zero_()
                    self.out_proj.bias.data[:m].copy_(
                        b_o[:m].to(
                            device=self.out_proj.bias.device,
                            dtype=self.out_proj.bias.dtype,
                        )
                    )

            # HX-FIX-06: W_q2 and W_k2 = EXACT copies (no noise)
            self.W_q2.weight.data.copy_(self.W_q1.weight.data)
            self.W_k2.weight.data.copy_(self.W_k1.weight.data)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Mask helper
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _prepare_valid_mask(
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
        B: int,
        N_kv: int,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        if attention_mask is None:
            return None
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                return attention_mask
            if attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return attention_mask > -1e4
            return attention_mask > 0
        if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = attention_mask
            elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = attention_mask > -1e4
            else:
                v = attention_mask > 0
            return v.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported attention_mask dim: {attention_mask.dim()}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("HymbaTransXBlock does not support cross-attention.")

        x = hidden_states if hidden_states is not None else kwargs.get("query", kwargs.get("x"))
        if x is None and len(args) > 0:
            x = args[0]
        if x is None:
            raise ValueError("HymbaTransXBlock.forward: no input tensor provided.")

        B, L, D = x.shape
        device = x.device

        # Projections
        q1 = self.W_q1(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k1 = self.W_k1(x).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        q2 = self.W_q2(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k2 = self.W_k2(x).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.W_v(x).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # KV-cache
        past_k1 = past_v = None
        past_k2 = None
        past_len = 0
        if past_key_value is not None:
            if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)):
                if len(past_key_value) >= 3:
                    past_k1, past_v, past_k2 = (
                        past_key_value[0],
                        past_key_value[1],
                        past_key_value[2],
                    )
                elif len(past_key_value) >= 2:
                    past_k1, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
            elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                idx = min(layer_idx, len(past_key_value.key_cache) - 1)
                past_k1 = past_key_value.key_cache[idx]
                past_v = past_key_value.value_cache[idx]

        if past_k1 is not None:
            past_len = past_k1.shape[2]
            k1_cat = torch.cat([past_k1, k1], dim=2)
            v_cat = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=2)
            k2_cat = torch.cat([past_k2, k2], dim=2) if past_k2 is not None else k2
        else:
            k1_cat = k1
            v_cat = v
            k2_cat = k2

        N_kv = k1_cat.shape[2]

        # RoPE (only if use_rope)
        if self.use_rope:
            if position_embeddings is not None:
                cos, sin = position_embeddings
                cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
                sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
                q1, k1_cat = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q1, k1_cat, cos, sin)
                q2, k2_cat = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q2, k2_cat, cos, sin)
            elif self.rope is not None:
                q1 = self.rope(q1, past_len=past_len)
                k1_cat = self.rope(k1_cat, past_len=0)
                q2 = self.rope(q2, past_len=past_len)
                k2_cat = self.rope(k2_cat, past_len=0)

        # GQA expansion
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k1_cat = repeat_kv(k1_cat, self.kv_repeat)
            k2_cat = repeat_kv(k2_cat, self.kv_repeat)
            v_cat = repeat_kv(v_cat, self.kv_repeat)

        # Differential attention
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        scores1 = torch.matmul(q1.float(), k1_cat.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
        scores2 = torch.matmul(q2.float(), k2_cat.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        # Causal mask (offset-aware for cache)
        if self.is_decoder:
            if past_len > 0:
                abs_q = (past_len + torch.arange(L, device=device)).unsqueeze(1)
                abs_k = torch.arange(N_kv, device=device).unsqueeze(0)
                causal = abs_k <= abs_q
            else:
                causal = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, N_kv, device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
            scores1 = scores1.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)
            scores2 = scores2.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)

        # Padding mask
        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if encoder_hidden_states is not None else attention_mask
        valid_mask = self._prepare_valid_mask(eff_mask, B, N_kv, device)
        if valid_mask is not None:
            scores1 = scores1.masked_fill(~valid_mask[:, None, None, :].bool(), -1e4)
            scores2 = scores2.masked_fill(~valid_mask[:, None, None, :].bool(), -1e4)

        scores1 = torch.nan_to_num(scores1, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        scores2 = torch.nan_to_num(scores2, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        attn1 = F.softmax(scores1, dim=-1)
        attn2 = F.softmax(scores2, dim=-1)

        # Learnable lambda (init sigmoid(-2) ≈ 0.12)
        lam = torch.sigmoid(self.diff_lambda)
        diff_attn = F.relu(attn1 - lam * attn2)
        denom = diff_attn.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
        diff_attn = diff_attn / (denom + 1e-6)
        diff_attn = self.dropout(diff_attn).to(v_cat.dtype)

        # head_mask broadcasting
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
            diff_attn = diff_attn * head_mask.to(diff_attn.dtype)

        A_out = (
            torch.matmul(diff_attn, v_cat)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
            .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        )

        # SSM branch (disabled for GPT-2 by default)
        if self.mamba is not None:
            if x.requires_grad:
                from torch.utils.checkpoint import checkpoint

                def _run_mamba(h, m):
                    return self.mamba(h, attention_mask=m)[0]

                if self.is_decoder:
                    M_out = checkpoint(_run_mamba, x, attention_mask, use_reentrant=False)
                else:
                    x_rev = torch.flip(x, dims=[1])
                    mf = checkpoint(_run_mamba, x, attention_mask, use_reentrant=False)
                    mb = checkpoint(_run_mamba, x_rev, attention_mask, use_reentrant=False)
                    M_out = 0.5 * (mf + torch.flip(mb, dims=[1]))
            else:
                if self.is_decoder:
                    mamba_res = self.mamba(x, attention_mask=attention_mask)
                    M_out = mamba_res[0] if isinstance(mamba_res, tuple) else mamba_res
                else:
                    x_rev = torch.flip(x, dims=[1])
                    mamba_fwd = self.mamba(x, attention_mask=attention_mask)
                    mamba_bwd = self.mamba(x_rev, attention_mask=attention_mask)
                    mf = mamba_fwd[0] if isinstance(mamba_fwd, tuple) else mamba_fwd
                    mb = mamba_bwd[0] if isinstance(mamba_bwd, tuple) else mamba_bwd
                    M_out = 0.5 * (mf + torch.flip(mb, dims=[1]))

            # alpha init = 4.0 → sigmoid(4.0) ≈ 0.98 (attention-dominant)
            alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.alpha)
            combined = alpha * A_out + (1.0 - alpha) * M_out
        else:
            # SSM disabled: pure attention
            combined = A_out

        # Output projection
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = combined
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(combined)

        out = torch.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        return out, (diff_attn if output_attentions else None)

class InfoMambaConceptBottleneck (dim: int, bottleneck_dim: int = 256)
Expand source code 
class InfoMambaConceptBottleneck(nn.Module):
    """
    Information Bottleneck with down/up projections.
    SSM executes in the compressed (bottleneck_dim) space.
    """

    def __init__(self, dim: int, bottleneck_dim: int = 256) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.bottleneck_dim = bottleneck_dim
        self.norm_in = nn.LayerNorm(dim)
        self.W_down = nn.Linear(dim, bottleneck_dim, bias=False)
        self.norm_mid = nn.LayerNorm(bottleneck_dim)
        self.W_up = nn.Linear(bottleneck_dim, dim, bias=False)
        self.norm_out = nn.LayerNorm(dim)

    def down(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        h = self.norm_in(x)
        z = self.W_down(h)
        return self.norm_mid(z)

    def up(self, z: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        z = F.gelu(z)
        h = self.W_up(z)
        return self.norm_out(h)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("jamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True)
class Jamba2Block(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(self, config: Any | None = None, d_model: int | None = None, n_heads: int | None = None, n_experts: int = 4, top_k: int = 2, rank: int | None = 64, pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None, old_attn: nn.Module | None = None, comp_config: Any | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.config = config

        d_model = kwargs.get("d_model") or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or kwargs.get("dim") or d_model
        n_heads = kwargs.get("n_heads") or kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads") or n_heads

        if d_model is None and config is not None: d_model = getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 768)))
        if d_model is None and source_attn is not None:
            for attr in ("out_lin", "dense", "c_proj", "o_proj", "out_proj"):
                layer = getattr(source_attn, attr, None)
                if layer is not None and hasattr(layer, "out_features"):
                    d_model = layer.out_features
                    break
        if d_model is None: d_model = 768

        if n_heads is None and config is not None: n_heads = getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12))
        if n_heads is None and source_attn is not None: n_heads = getattr(source_attn, "num_attention_heads", getattr(source_attn, "n_heads", 12))
        if n_heads is None: n_heads = 12

        if config is not None:
            n_experts = int(getattr(config, "num_experts", getattr(config, "n_experts", n_experts)))
            top_k = int(getattr(config, "top_k", top_k))

        self.d_model = int(d_model)
        self.dim = self.d_model
        self.hidden_size = self.d_model
        self.n_heads = int(n_heads)
        self.num_heads = self.n_heads

        num_kv_heads = None
        if config is not None: num_kv_heads = getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        if num_kv_heads is None: num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads", kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads))
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(int(num_kv_heads), self.num_heads))

        self.head_dim = self.d_model // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
        self.supports_gqa = True

        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(config, kwargs.get("is_decoder"), source_attn, kwargs)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        if config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(getattr(config, "lora_rank", getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))))
            self.lora_alpha = float(getattr(config, "lora_alpha", getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))))
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)))
            self.lora_alpha = float(getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)))
        else:
            self.lora_rank = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))
            self.lora_alpha = float(kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.source_attn = source_attn
        self.rotary_emb = getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None

        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(self.d_model, self.all_head_size, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(self.d_model, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(self.d_model, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(self.all_head_size, self.d_model, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)

        self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj = self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin

        self.mamba = SVDMambaSub(self.d_model, config=config, rank=rank, is_decoder=self.is_decoder)
        self.moe = ExpertChoiceMoE(self.d_model, n_experts=n_experts, capacity_factor=1.0)

        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model)
        self.norm3 = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model)

        # FIX 5: Renamed to explicitly include "lora_" so KD script adds them to the Optimizer
        self.lora_mamba_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self.lora_moe_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))

        dropout_p = float(getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1) if config else 0.1))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self._aux_loss_sum: float = 0.0
        self._aux_loss_count: int = 0

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)
        self._return_format = _detect_return_format(source_attn, self.is_nested_encoder)

    @property
    def aux_loss(self) -> float:
        if self._aux_loss_count == 0: return 0.0
        return self._aux_loss_sum / self._aux_loss_count

    def reset_aux_loss(self) -> None:
        self._aux_loss_sum = 0.0
        self._aux_loss_count = 0

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        for mod in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
            if hasattr(mod, "freeze_base_weights"): mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        for mod in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
            if hasattr(mod, "unfreeze_base_weights"): mod.unfreeze_base_weights()

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None: return
        cls_name = getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__
        has_out = any(hasattr(src, a) for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj"))
        if cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (hasattr(src, "query") and not has_out): self.is_nested_encoder = True
        if hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src, "output"): self.is_nested_encoder = True

        extracted = {"q": (None, None), "k": (None, None), "v": (None, None), "o": (None, None)}

        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            cw = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if cw is not None:
                cw = cw.data.clone()
                if (c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or hasattr(c, "nf") or (cw.ndim == 2 and cw.shape[0] < cw.shape[1])): cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                split = cw.size(0) // 3
                cb = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c, "bias", None))
                cb = cb.data.clone() if cb is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (cw[:split], cb[:split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (cw[split : 2 * split], cb[split : 2 * split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["v"] = (cw[2 * split :], cb[2 * split :] if cb is not None else None)

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                o = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
                ow = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
                if ow is not None:
                    ow = ow.data.clone()
                    if (o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or hasattr(o, "nf") or (ow.ndim == 2 and ow.shape[0] < ow.shape[1])): ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                    bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (ow, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                    extracted[key] = (m.weight.data.clone(), b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                d = src.output.dense
                b = getattr(d, "bias", None)
                extracted["o"] = (d.weight.data.clone(), b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in candidates.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        m = getattr(src, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            w_data = m.weight.data.clone()
                            if (m.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or hasattr(m, "nf") or (key == "o" and w_data.ndim == 2 and w_data.shape[0] < w_data.shape[1])): w_data = w_data.t().contiguous()
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[key] = (w_data, b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
                            break

        missing = [k for k, (w, _) in extracted.items() if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None: extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Jamba2 UniversalAdapter fallback: %s", e)

        target_map = {"q": self.q_lin, "k": self.k_lin, "v": self.v_lin, "o": self.out_lin}

        with torch.no_grad():
            for key, target in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[key]
                if w is None:
                    if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target.weight)
                        if target.bias is not None: nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2: w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)
                if w.shape != target.weight.shape and w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape: w = w.t().contiguous()

                out_d = min(target.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                target.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(w[:out_d, :in_d].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype))

                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    mb = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))
                elif target.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)

                if self.freeze_base: target.freeze_base_weights()

        if hasattr(src, "rotary_emb") and src.rotary_emb is not None: self.rotary_emb = src.rotary_emb
        if self.freeze_base: self.freeze_base_weights()

    def _norm_dtype(self, norm: nn.LayerNorm, fallback: torch.dtype) -> torch.dtype:
        return norm.weight.dtype if getattr(norm, "weight", None) is not None else fallback

    def _format_output(self, out: torch.Tensor, attn_weights: torch.Tensor | None, present: Any | None) -> tuple:
        fmt = self._return_format
        if fmt == "bert": return (out, attn_weights) if attn_weights is not None else (out,)
        if fmt == "gpt2": return (out, present, attn_weights) if attn_weights is not None else (out, present)
        return (out, attn_weights, present) if attn_weights is not None else (out, present)

    def _normalize_head_mask(self, head_mask: torch.Tensor, attn: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        hm = head_mask.to(attn.dtype)
        if hm.dim() == 1: hm = hm.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 2:
            if hm.shape[-1] == self.num_heads: hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], self.num_heads, 1, 1)
            else: hm = hm.view(1, hm.shape[-1], 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 3: hm = hm.unsqueeze(2)
        return attn * hm

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        B, L, _ = hidden_states.shape
        layer_idx = int(kwargs.get("layer_idx", 0))
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        past_k, past_v, past_len, past_mamba = _read_cache(past_key_value, layer_idx)

        # ---- Branch 1: Mamba (Pre-LN with FIX 4: Checkpointing) ----
        mamba_out = 0
        present_mamba = None
        if self.mamba is not None:
            n1d = self._norm_dtype(self.norm1, target_dtype)
            x_norm1 = self.norm1(hidden_states.to(n1d)).to(target_dtype)
            
            if self.training:
                from torch.utils.checkpoint import checkpoint
                def _run_mamba(x):
                    return self.mamba(x, attention_mask=attention_mask, past_state=past_mamba)[0]
                m_out = checkpoint(_run_mamba, x_norm1, use_reentrant=False)
            else:
                m_out, present_mamba = self.mamba(
                    x_norm1, attention_mask=attention_mask, past_state=past_mamba
                )
            
            # FIX 5: Use lora_mamba_scale    
            mamba_out = m_out.to(target_dtype) * self.lora_mamba_scale 

        # ---- Branch 2: Attention ----
        q = self.q_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = self.k_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.v_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            cos = cos.narrow(-2, past_len, L) if cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L else cos
            sin = sin.narrow(-2, past_len, L) if sin.shape[-2] >= past_len + L else sin
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
            if pos_ids is None: pos_ids = torch.arange(past_len, past_len + L, device=hidden_states.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            cos = sin = None
            for call in (lambda: self.rotary_emb(k, seq_len=L), lambda: self.rotary_emb(k, pos_ids), lambda: self.rotary_emb(pos_ids)):
                try: cos, sin = call(); break
                except Exception: continue
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        if past_k is not None:
            k = torch.cat([past_k.to(k.dtype), k], dim=-2)
            v = torch.cat([past_v.to(v.dtype), v], dim=-2)

        present_key_value = _write_cache(k, v, present_mamba, past_key_value, layer_idx)

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k_exp = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v_exp = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)
        else:
            k_exp, v_exp = k, v

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder and L > 1)
        use_sdpa = (head_mask is None and not output_attentions and attention_mask is None and past_len == 0)

        attn_weights = None
        if use_sdpa:
            try:
                _qs = q.half() if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
                _ks = k_exp.half() if k_exp.dtype == torch.float32 else k_exp
                _vs = v_exp.half() if v_exp.dtype == torch.float32 else v_exp
                _sdpa_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(_qs, _ks, _vs, is_causal=is_causal, dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0)
                attn_out = _sdpa_out.to(target_dtype).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Jamba2 SDPA path failed: %s", e)
                use_sdpa = False

        if not use_sdpa:
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k_exp.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            if is_causal:
                kv_len = k_exp.shape[-2]
                causal = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, kv_len, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=torch.bool), diagonal=past_len)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)
            if attention_mask is not None:
                if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                    valid = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, k_exp.shape[-2], hidden_states.device)
                    if valid is not None: scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)
                elif attention_mask.dim() == 3: scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
                elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                    if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool: scores = scores.masked_fill(~attention_mask, -1e4)
                    else: scores = scores + attention_mask.float()
            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(v_exp.dtype)
            probs = self.dropout(torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0))
            if head_mask is not None: probs = self._normalize_head_mask(head_mask, probs)
            attn_out = torch.matmul(probs, v_exp).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            attn_weights = probs if output_attentions else None

        attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn_delta = attn_out if self.is_nested_encoder else self.out_lin(attn_out)

        # ---- Branch 3: MoE (Pre-LN with FIX 4: Checkpointing) ----
        moe_out = 0
        if self.moe is not None:
            n3d = self._norm_dtype(self.norm3, target_dtype)
            x_norm3 = self.norm3(hidden_states.to(n3d)).to(target_dtype)
            
            if self.training:
                from torch.utils.checkpoint import checkpoint
                def _run_moe(x):
                    res = self.moe(x)
                    return res[0] if isinstance(res, (tuple, list)) else res
                m_out = checkpoint(_run_moe, x_norm3, use_reentrant=False)
            else:
                m_out = self.moe(x_norm3)
                if isinstance(m_out, (tuple, list)):
                    m_out = m_out[0]
                    
            # FIX 5: Use lora_moe_scale
            moe_out = m_out.to(target_dtype) * self.lora_moe_scale 
            
            if not self.training:
                self._accumulate_aux_loss()

        final_delta = mamba_out + attn_delta + moe_out
        
        return self._format_output(final_delta, attn_weights, present_key_value)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

@property
def aux_loss(self) -> float:

    return self._aux_loss_sum / self._aux_loss_count
