@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("jamba_info", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class JambaInfoBlock(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        per_head_alpha: bool = True,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        hidden_size = self._detect_hidden_size(config, source_attn, kwargs)
        num_heads = self._detect_num_heads(config, source_attn, kwargs, hidden_size)
        num_kv_heads = self._detect_num_kv_heads(config, source_attn, kwargs, num_heads)
        head_dim = self._detect_head_dim(config, source_attn, kwargs, hidden_size, num_heads)

        self.dim = int(hidden_size)
        self.hidden_size = self.dim
        self.num_heads = int(num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads)
        self.head_dim = int(head_dim)
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(config, is_decoder, source_attn)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        self._return_format = _detect_return_format(source_attn, self.is_nested_encoder)

        # Optional rotary_emb (for position_ids fallback)
        self.rotary_emb = getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn else None

        # LoRA
        self.lora_rank = int(
            getattr(config, "lora_rank", None)
            or (getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", None) if comp_config else None)
            or kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            getattr(config, "lora_alpha", None)
            or (getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", None) if comp_config else None)
            or kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # ---------- Projections ----------
        self.W_q1 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_head_size, bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_k1 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_q2 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_head_size, bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_k2 = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_v = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size, self.dim, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Aliases
        self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj = self.W_q1, self.W_k1, self.W_v

        # ---------- Differential Variables ----------
        self.lambda_q1 = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))
        self.lambda_k1 = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))
        self.lambda_q2 = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))
        self.lambda_k2 = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))

        # FIX 2: Per-head λ_init via Diff Transformer paper formula.
        # Formula: λ_init[h] = 0.8 - 0.6 · exp(-0.3 · h)
        head_ids = torch.arange(self.num_heads, dtype=torch.float32)
        lambda_init_values = 0.8 - 0.6 * torch.exp(-0.3 * head_ids)
        self.lambda_init = nn.Parameter(lambda_init_values)

        # FIX 1: Precompute (1 - λ_init) as a buffer for scale factor.
        # Stored as buffer (not parameter) because it's derived from lambda_init.
        # Recomputed in forward to stay in sync if lambda_init changes.
        # (No need to register as buffer — computed on the fly in forward.)

        # FIX 3: RMSNorm on both branches for scale consistency
        self.diff_norm = RMSNorm(self.all_head_size)
        self.ssm_norm = RMSNorm(self.dim)

        # ---------- Alpha ----------
        self.per_head_alpha = bool(per_head_alpha)
        if self.per_head_alpha:
            self.alpha_raw = nn.Parameter(torch.full((self.num_heads,), -2.0))
        else:
            self.alpha_raw = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-2.0))

        # ---------- Bottleneck + SSM ----------
        bottleneck_dim = int(
            getattr(comp_config, "bottleneck_dim", 256) if comp_config else 256
        )
        # Ensure bottleneck_dim is reasonable relative to dim
        bottleneck_dim = max(64, min(bottleneck_dim, self.dim))
        self.bottleneck = InfoMambaConceptBottleneck(self.dim, bottleneck_dim=bottleneck_dim)

        # SSM in bottleneck space
        self.ssm_fwd, self.ssm_bwd = None, None
        if SimpleSSM is not None:
            self.ssm_fwd = self._make_ssm(config, bottleneck_dim)
            if not self.is_decoder:
                self.ssm_bwd = self._make_ssm(config, bottleneck_dim)

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
            )
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    def _make_ssm(self, config: Any | None, d_model: int) -> nn.Module | None:
        if SimpleSSM is None:
            return None
        try:
            return SimpleSSM(d_model=d_model, config=config, is_decoder=self.is_decoder)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("SimpleSSM instantiation failed: %s", e)
            return None

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_hidden_size(config, src, kwargs) -> int:
        for k in ("hidden_size", "d_model", "n_embd", "dim"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("hidden_size", "d_model", "n_embd", "dim"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for attr in ("out_lin", "out_proj", "o_proj", "dense", "c_proj"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "out_features"):
                    return int(mod.out_features)
            for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q1", "c_attn"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "in_features"):
                    return int(mod.in_features)
        return 768

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_num_heads(config, src, kwargs, hidden_size) -> int:
        for k in ("num_heads", "num_attention_heads", "n_heads", "n_head"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("num_attention_heads", "num_heads", "n_heads", "n_head"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for k in ("num_heads", "num_attention_heads", "n_heads"):
                v = getattr(src, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        return max(1, hidden_size // 64)

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_num_kv_heads(config, src, kwargs, num_heads) -> int:
        for k in ("num_key_value_heads", "num_kv_heads"):
            v = kwargs.get(k)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("num_key_value_heads", "num_kv_heads"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            v = getattr(src, "num_key_value_heads", None)
            if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                return v
        return num_heads

    @staticmethod
    def _detect_head_dim(config, src, kwargs, hidden_size, num_heads) -> int:
        v = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
            return v
        if config is not None:
            for k in ("head_dim", "kv_channels", "d_kv"):
                v = getattr(config, k, None)
                if isinstance(v, int) and v > 0:
                    return v
        if src is not None:
            for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin", "W_q1"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight is not None:
                    try:
                        return int(mod.weight.shape[0] // max(1, num_heads))
                    except Exception:
                        pass
        return max(1, hidden_size // max(1, num_heads))

    def _run_ssm(
        self,
        ssm: nn.Module | None,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        if ssm is None:
            return torch.zeros_like(x)
        try:
            out = ssm(x, attention_mask=attention_mask)
        except TypeError:
            try:
                out = ssm(x)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SSM forward failed: %s", e)
                return torch.zeros_like(x)
        return out[0] if isinstance(out, tuple) else out

    def _format_output(
        self,
        out: torch.Tensor,
        attn_weights: torch.Tensor | None,
        present: Any | None,
    ) -> tuple:
        fmt = self._return_format
        if fmt == "bert":
            return (out, attn_weights)
        if fmt == "gpt2":
            return (out, present, attn_weights)
        return (out, attn_weights, present)

    def _normalize_head_mask(
        self, head_mask: torch.Tensor, attn: torch.Tensor
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        hm = head_mask.to(attn.dtype)
        H = self.num_heads
        if hm.dim() == 1:
            hm = hm.view(1, H, 1, 1) if hm.numel() == H else hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 2:
            if hm.shape[-1] == H:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], H, 1, 1)
            elif hm.shape[0] == H:
                hm = hm.t().view(1, H, 1, 1)
            else:
                hm = hm.view(1, hm.shape[-1], 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 3:
            if hm.shape[1] == H:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(2)
            elif hm.shape[2] == H:
                hm = hm.permute(0, 2, 1).unsqueeze(2)
            else:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
        return attn * hm

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = any(hasattr(src, a) for a in ("out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj"))
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            cw = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if cw is not None:
                cw = cw.data.clone()
                if (
                    c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (cw.ndim == 2 and cw.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size)
                ):
                    cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                split = cw.size(0) // 3
                bmod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c, "bias", None))
                cb = bmod.data.clone() if bmod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (cw[:split], cb[:split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (
                    cw[split : 2 * split],
                    cb[split : 2 * split] if cb is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    cw[2 * split :],
                    cb[2 * split :] if cb is not None else None,
                )
            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                o = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
                ow = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
                if ow is not None:
                    ow = ow.data.clone()
                    if (
                        o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (ow.ndim == 2 and ow.shape[0] < ow.shape[1])
                    ):
                        ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                    bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (ow, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)

        # BERT/RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                    extracted[key] = (
                        m.weight.data.clone(),
                        b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                d = src.output.dense
                b = getattr(d, "bias", None)
                extracted["o"] = (
                    d.weight.data.clone(),
                    b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                )

        # Flat
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in candidates.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        m = getattr(src, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            w = m.weight.data.clone()
                            if m.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or (
                                key == "o" and w.ndim == 2 and w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]
                            ):
                                w = w.t().contiguous()
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[key] = (
                                w,
                                b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback: %s", e)

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        def _copy_into(
            target_mod: LoRALinear, w: torch.Tensor | None, b: torch.Tensor | None
        ) -> None:
            if w is None:
                return
            if w.ndim != 2:
                w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)
            if (
                w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
            ):
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            r = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
            c = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
            target_mod.weight.data[:r, :c].copy_(
                w[:r, :c].to(
                    device=target_mod.weight.device, dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype
                )
            )
            if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                n = min(b.numel(), target_mod.bias.numel())
                b_val = b.data if isinstance(b, nn.Parameter) else b
                target_mod.bias.data[:n].copy_(
                    b_val[:n].to(
                        device=target_mod.bias.device, dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype
                    )
                )

        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q, _ = extracted["q"]
            w_k, _ = extracted["k"]
            w_v, _ = extracted["v"]
            w_o, b_o = extracted["o"]

            if w_q is not None:
                _copy_into(self.W_q1, _orient(w_q, self.hidden_size), None)
                _copy_into(self.W_q2, _orient(w_q, self.hidden_size), None)
            if w_k is not None:
                _copy_into(self.W_k1, _orient(w_k, self.hidden_size), None)
                _copy_into(self.W_k2, _orient(w_k, self.hidden_size), None)
            if w_v is not None:
                _copy_into(self.W_v, _orient(w_v, self.hidden_size), None)
            if w_o is not None:
                _copy_into(self.out_proj, _orient(w_o, self.all_head_size), b_o)
            elif self.is_nested_encoder:
                nn.init.eye_(self.out_proj.weight)
                if self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.bias)

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in (
                self.W_q1,
                self.W_k1,
                self.W_q2,
                self.W_k2,
                self.W_v,
                self.out_proj,
            ):
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        position_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        # ---- Input validation ----
        mask = kwargs.get("mask")
        if attention_mask is None and mask is not None:
            attention_mask = mask

        x = hidden_states
        B, L, D = x.shape
        device = x.device

        # ---- Read cache ----
        past_k1, past_k2, past_v, past_len = _read_cache(past_key_value)

        # ---- QKV projections ----
        q1 = self.W_q1(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k1 = (
            self.W_k1(x)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        q2 = self.W_q2(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k2 = (
            self.W_k2(x)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.W_v(x)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # ---- RoPE ----
        position_embeddings = position_embeddings or kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_ids = position_ids or kwargs.get("position_ids")

        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            if cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L:
                cos = cos.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
                sin = sin.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
            q1, k1 = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q1, k1, cos, sin)
            q2, k2 = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q2, k2, cos, sin)
        elif position_ids is not None and self.rotary_emb is not None:
            # FIX 5: fallback via rotary_emb if available
            try:
                cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(x, position_ids=position_ids)
                if cos.shape[-2] >= L:
                    cos = cos[..., :L, :]
                    sin = sin[..., :L, :]
                q1, k1 = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q1, k1, cos, sin)
                q2, k2 = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q2, k2, cos, sin)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("rotary_emb fallback failed: %s", e)

        # ---- KV cache append (before GQA) ----
        if past_k1 is not None:
            # FIX 6: cache is stored in the same dtype as k1
            k1 = torch.cat([past_k1, k1], dim=-2)
            k2 = torch.cat([past_k2, k2], dim=-2)
            v = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=-2)
        present_key_value = (k1, k2, v)

        # ---- GQA expand ----
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k1 = repeat_kv(k1, self.kv_repeat)
            k2 = repeat_kv(k2, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---- Attention scores ----
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        scores1 = torch.matmul(q1.float(), k1.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
        scores2 = torch.matmul(q2.float(), k2.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        # ---- Causal mask ----
        if self.is_decoder and L > 1:
            kv_len = k1.shape[-2]
            causal = torch.tril(
                torch.ones(L, kv_len, device=device, dtype=torch.bool),
                diagonal=past_len,
            )
            scores1 = scores1.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)
            scores2 = scores2.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)

        # ---- Attention mask ----
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                valid = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, k1.shape[-2], device)
                if valid is not None:
                    scores1 = scores1.masked_fill(~valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)
                    scores2 = scores2.masked_fill(~valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
                m3 = attention_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
                if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    m3 = (~attention_mask).unsqueeze(1).float() * -1e4
                scores1 = scores1 + m3
                scores2 = scores2 + m3
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    m4 = (~attention_mask).float() * -1e4
                else:
                    m4 = attention_mask.float()
                scores1 = scores1 + m4
                scores2 = scores2 + m4

        scores1 = torch.nan_to_num(scores1, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        scores2 = torch.nan_to_num(scores2, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        attn1 = F.softmax(scores1, dim=-1).to(q1.dtype)
        attn2 = F.softmax(scores2, dim=-1).to(q2.dtype)

        # ---- Differential Attention (Diff Transformer) ----
        lambda_full = (
            torch.exp(self.lambda_q1 * self.lambda_k1)
            - torch.exp(self.lambda_q2 * self.lambda_k2)
            + self.lambda_init
        ).to(attn1.dtype)
        lambda_full = lambda_full.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1)

        # FIX 1: (1 - λ_init) scaling per Diff Transformer paper
        scale_factor = (1.0 - self.lambda_init).to(attn1.dtype).view(
            1, self.num_heads, 1, 1
        )

        diff_attn = (attn1 - lambda_full * attn2) * scale_factor
        diff_attn = self.dropout(diff_attn).to(v.dtype)

        if head_mask is not None:
            diff_attn = self._normalize_head_mask(head_mask, diff_attn)

        attn_out = (
            torch.matmul(diff_attn, v)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
            .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        )
        attn_out = self.diff_norm(attn_out)

        # ---- Information Bottleneck + SSM ----
        # NOTE: bottleneck operates on x (dual path with attention).
        # To connect bottleneck to attention, replace `x` with `attn_out` below.
        z = self.bottleneck.down(x)

        if self.is_decoder:
            z_ssm = self._run_ssm(self.ssm_fwd, z, attention_mask)
        else:
            if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
                z_rev = torch.flip(z, dims=[1]).contiguous()
                # FIX 4: proper mask reversal — flip along sequence dim
                if attention_mask is not None:
                    # Determine sequence dim: for (B, L) it's dim=1;
                    # for (B, 1, L, L) it's dim=-1; for (B, L, L) it's dim=-1
                    if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                        rev_mask = torch.flip(attention_mask, dims=[1]).contiguous()
                    elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
                        rev_mask = torch.flip(attention_mask, dims=[-1]).contiguous()
                    elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                        rev_mask = torch.flip(attention_mask, dims=[-1]).contiguous()
                    else:
                        rev_mask = attention_mask
                else:
                    rev_mask = None

                ssm_fwd = self._run_ssm(self.ssm_fwd, z, attention_mask)
                ssm_bwd = self._run_ssm(self.ssm_bwd, z_rev, rev_mask)
                z_ssm = 0.5 * (ssm_fwd + torch.flip(ssm_bwd, dims=[1]).contiguous())
            else:
                z_ssm = self._run_ssm(self.ssm_fwd, z, attention_mask)

        ssm_branch = self.bottleneck.up(z_ssm)

        # FIX 3: RMSNorm on SSM branch for scale consistency
        ssm_branch = self.ssm_norm(ssm_branch)

        # ---- Alpha blending ----
        target_dtype = x.dtype

        if self.per_head_alpha:
            # FIX 7: sigmoid in target dtype
            alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.alpha_raw.to(target_dtype)).view(
                1, 1, self.num_heads, 1
            )
            attn_out_h = attn_out.view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            ssm_branch_h = ssm_branch.view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            combined = (1.0 - alpha) * attn_out_h + alpha * ssm_branch_h
            combined = combined.view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        else:
            alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.alpha_raw.to(target_dtype))
            combined = (1.0 - alpha) * attn_out + alpha * ssm_branch

        combined = torch.nan_to_num(combined, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # ---- Output projection ----
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = combined.to(target_dtype)
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(combined).to(target_dtype)

        attn_weights = diff_attn if output_attentions else None
        return self._format_output(out, attn_weights, present_key_value)

Expand source code 
class LoRALinear(nn.Module):
    """Linear projection with frozen base weights and trainable LoRA adapters."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        bias: bool = True,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        freeze_base: bool = True,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        factory_kwargs = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.in_features = in_features
        self.out_features = out_features
        self.lora_rank = lora_rank
        self.lora_alpha = lora_alpha
        self.scaling = (lora_alpha / lora_rank) if lora_rank > 0 else 1.0
        self.freeze_base = freeze_base

        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, in_features), **factory_kwargs))
        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, **factory_kwargs))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((lora_rank, in_features), **factory_kwargs))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty((out_features, lora_rank), **factory_kwargs))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        self.reset_parameters()
        if freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        if self.bias is not None:
            fan_in, _ = nn.init._calculate_fan_in_and_fan_out(self.weight)
            bound = 1 / math.sqrt(fan_in) if fan_in > 0 else 0
            nn.init.uniform_(self.bias, -bound, bound)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_B is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        self.weight.requires_grad = False
        if self.bias is not None:
            self.bias.requires_grad = False
        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            if self.lora_A is not None:
                self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            if self.lora_B is not None:
                self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        target_dtype = x.dtype
        w = self.weight.to(target_dtype) if self.weight.dtype != target_dtype else self.weight
        b = (
            self.bias.to(target_dtype)
            if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != target_dtype)
            else self.bias
        )
        out = F.linear(x, w, b)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_A
            bb = self.lora_B.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_B
            out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.scaling
        return out.to(target_dtype)

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    target_dtype = x.dtype
    w = self.weight.to(target_dtype) if self.weight.dtype != target_dtype else self.weight
    b = (
        self.bias.to(target_dtype)
        if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != target_dtype)
        else self.bias
    )
    out = F.linear(x, w, b)
    if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        a = self.lora_A.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_A
        bb = self.lora_B.to(target_dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != target_dtype else self.lora_B
        out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.scaling
    return out.to(target_dtype)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class Mamba (d_model: int = 768,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
class Mamba(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_model: int = 768, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, 2 * d_model)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.weight)
        if self.out_proj.bias is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.bias)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, **kwargs: Any) -> torch.Tensor:
        x1, x2 = self.in_proj(x).chunk(2, dim=-1)
        y = F.silu(x1) * x2
        return self.out_proj(y)

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Define the computation performed at every call.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("mamba_block")
class MambaBlock(nn.Module):
    """Faithful S6 / Mamba block, production-hardened."""

    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = False

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        d_state: int = 16,
        d_conv: int = 4,
        expand: int = 2,
        dt_rank: int | None = None,
        conv_bias: bool = True,
        bias: bool = False,
        bidirectional: bool = False,
        is_decoder_override: bool | None = None,
        freeze_base: bool = False,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        # Convenience: MambaBlock(768) support
        if isinstance(config, (int, float)) and d_model is None:
            d_model = int(config)
            config = None

        super().__init__()
        self.config = config

        # ---- d_model inference (MB-11) ----
        if d_model is None:
            d_model = _infer_d_model(config, kwargs.get("source_attn"), None, kwargs)
        self.d_model = int(d_model)

        # ---- Dimensions ----
        self.d_state = int(d_state)
        self.d_conv = int(d_conv)
        self.expand = int(expand)
        self.d_inner = self.expand * self.d_model
        self.dt_rank = int(dt_rank or math.ceil(self.d_model / 16))
        self.bidirectional = bool(bidirectional)
        self.freeze_base = bool(freeze_base)

        # ---- Decoder detection (MB-10) ----
        if is_decoder_override is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder_override)
        else:
            _mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if config else ""
            _dec_prefixes = (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
            )
            self.is_decoder = (
                bool(getattr(config, "is_decoder", False) or getattr(config, "causal", False))
                if config
                else False
            ) or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _dec_prefixes)

        # ---- Projections ----
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(self.d_model, 2 * self.d_inner, bias=bias)
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(
            in_channels=self.d_inner,
            out_channels=self.d_inner,
            kernel_size=self.d_conv,
            groups=self.d_inner,
            # FIXED: padding=self.d_conv - 1 causes right-padding, leaking future tokens.
            # Changed to padding=0; manual left-padding is now applied during forward pass.
            padding=0,
            bias=conv_bias,
        )
        self.x_proj = nn.Linear(self.d_inner, self.dt_rank + 2 * self.d_state, bias=False)
        self.dt_proj = nn.Linear(self.dt_rank, self.d_inner, bias=True)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.d_inner, self.d_model, bias=bias)

        # ---- S4D-real initialization of A ----
        A = torch.arange(1, self.d_state + 1, dtype=torch.float32).repeat(self.d_inner, 1)
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(A))
        self.D = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.d_inner))

        # ---- dt initialization ----
        dt_min, dt_max = 0.001, 0.1
        dt_init = torch.exp(
            torch.rand(self.d_inner) * (math.log(dt_max) - math.log(dt_min)) + math.log(dt_min)
        )
        dt_init_inv = dt_init + torch.log(-torch.expm1(-dt_init))
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.dt_proj.bias.copy_(dt_init_inv)
            bound = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.dt_rank)
            nn.init.uniform_(self.dt_proj.weight, -bound, bound)
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.conv1d.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
            if self.conv1d.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(self.conv1d.bias)

        # ---- Bidirectional (backward SSM) ----
        if self.bidirectional:
            self.A_log_bwd = nn.Parameter(torch.log(A.clone() + 0.01 * torch.randn_like(A).abs()))
            self.dt_proj_bwd = nn.Linear(self.dt_rank, self.d_inner, bias=True)
            with torch.no_grad():
                self.dt_proj_bwd.bias.copy_(dt_init_inv)
                nn.init.uniform_(self.dt_proj_bwd.weight, -bound, bound)
            self.x_proj_bwd = nn.Linear(self.d_inner, self.dt_rank + 2 * self.d_state, bias=False)
            self.D_bwd = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.d_inner))

        # ---- Output-format detection (MB-12) ----
        source_attn = kwargs.get("source_attn") or kwargs.get("old_attn")
        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        self._return_format = _detect_return_format(source_attn, False)

        if freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        """MB-04: match the framework's LoRA-freeze convention."""
        for p in self.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = False

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        for p in self.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight distillation (MB-07, MB-08)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: nn.Module) -> None:
        """
        Distill attention weights (W_q, W_k, W_v, W_o) into Mamba's S6
        parameters via low-rank mapping:
            W_v  → in_proj (SSM input path)
            W_k  → x_proj (B branch, via SVD)
            W_q  → x_proj (C branch, via SVD)
            W_o  → out_proj
            dt   → kept at initialization (learnable, input-dependent via x_proj)
        """
        if src is None:
            return

        extracted: dict[str, torch.Tensor | None] = {
            "q": None,
            "k": None,
            "v": None,
            "o": None,
        }

        # --- 1. UniversalAdapter ---
        try:
            from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

            for proj in ("q", "k", "v", "o"):
                try:
                    w, _ = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[proj] = w
                except Exception:
                    pass
        except Exception:
            pass

        # --- 2. GPT-2 fused c_attn / c_proj ---
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None and extracted["q"] is None:
            c = src.c_attn
            base = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            w = getattr(base, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if w is not None:
                w = w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (w.ndim == 2 and w.shape[1] == 3 * self.d_model)
                )
                if is_conv:
                    w = w.t().contiguous()
                split = w.size(0) // 3
                extracted["q"] = w[:split, :]
                extracted["k"] = w[split : 2 * split, :]
                extracted["v"] = w[2 * split :, :]

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None and extracted["o"] is None:
            o = src.c_proj
            base = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
            w = getattr(base, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
            if w is not None:
                w = w.data.clone()
                if w.ndim == 2 and w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]:
                    w = w.t().contiguous()
                extracted["o"] = w

        # --- 3. Flat / nested (BERT / RoBERTa) ---
        named_map = {
            "q": ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"),
            "k": ("k_proj", "k_lin", "key"),
            "v": ("v_proj", "v_lin", "value"),
            "o": ("o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"),
        }
        nested = getattr(src, "self", None)
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj] is not None:
                continue
            # Try nested first
            if nested is not None:
                for n in names:
                    mod = getattr(nested, n, None)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj] = mod.weight.data
                        break
            if extracted[proj] is not None:
                continue
            # Then flat on src
            for n in names:
                mod = getattr(src, n, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj] = mod.weight.data
                    break
            # Nested output.dense for "o"
            if extracted["o"] is None and proj == "o":
                out = getattr(src, "output", None)
                dense = getattr(out, "dense", None) if out is not None else None
                if dense is not None and hasattr(dense, "weight"):
                    extracted["o"] = dense.weight.data

        # Mark nested encoder for later (MB-05)
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        if cls_name in (
            "BertSelfAttention",
            "RobertaSelfAttention",
            "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
        ) or (nested is not None and hasattr(nested, "query")):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True

        # ---- Apply mapping (MB-07, MB-08) ----
        dtype = self.in_proj.weight.dtype
        device = self.in_proj.weight.device

        def _to_t(x: torch.Tensor | None, r: int, c: int) -> torch.Tensor | None:
            """Project W (any shape) to target shape (r, c) via SVD."""
            if x is None:
                return None
            x = x.float()
            if x.ndim != 2:
                return None
            M, N = x.shape
            u, s, vh = torch.linalg.svd(x, full_matrices=False)
            k = min(r, c, s.numel())

            u_r = u[: min(r, M), :k]
            vh_c = vh[:k, : min(c, N)]
            core = (u_r * s[:k].unsqueeze(0)) @ vh_c

            out = torch.zeros(r, c, dtype=torch.float32, device=x.device)
            out[: core.size(0), : core.size(1)] = core

            if r > core.size(0):
                rem_r = r - core.size(0)
                reps_r = (rem_r + core.size(0) - 1) // core.size(0)
                tiled_r = core.repeat(reps_r, 1)[:rem_r, :]
                out[core.size(0) :, : core.size(1)] = tiled_r

            if c > core.size(1):
                rem_c = c - core.size(1)
                reps_c = (rem_c + core.size(1) - 1) // core.size(1)
                tiled_c = out[:, : core.size(1)].repeat(1, reps_c)[:, :rem_c]
                out[:, core.size(1) :] = tiled_c

            return out.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q = extracted["q"]
            w_k = extracted["k"]
            w_v = extracted["v"]
            w_o = extracted["o"]

            # ---------- in_proj: (2*d_inner, d_model) ----------
            # x_ssm path = rows [0 : d_inner]
            # z     path = rows [d_inner : 2*d_inner]
            if w_v is not None:
                w_v_p = _to_t(w_v, self.d_inner, self.d_model)
                if w_v_p is not None:
                    # x_ssm: full projection from W_v
                    self.in_proj.weight.data[: self.d_inner, : self.d_model].copy_(w_v_p)
                    # z: scaled copy so the gate starts balanced
                    self.in_proj.weight.data[self.d_inner :, : self.d_model].copy_(0.5 * w_v_p)

            # ---------- x_proj: (dt_rank + 2*d_state, d_inner) ----------
            # rows [0 : dt_rank]                     → dt  (keep init)
            # rows [dt_rank : dt_rank + d_state]     → B   (from W_k)
            # rows [dt_rank + d_state : ...]         → C   (from W_q)
            # MB-01: DO NOT zero the whole x_proj; only reset B/C rows.
            with torch.no_grad():
                b_start = self.dt_rank
                b_end = self.dt_rank + self.d_state
                c_start = b_end
                c_end = c_start + self.d_state

                if w_k is not None:
                    b_mat = _to_t(w_k, self.d_state, self.d_inner)
                    if b_mat is not None:
                        self.x_proj.weight.data[b_start:b_end, :].copy_(b_mat)
                if w_q is not None:
                    c_mat = _to_t(w_q, self.d_state, self.d_inner)
                    if c_mat is not None:
                        self.x_proj.weight.data[c_start:c_end, :].copy_(c_mat)

            # ---------- out_proj: (d_model, d_inner) ----------
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o_p = _to_t(w_o, self.d_model, self.d_inner)
                if w_o_p is not None:
                    self.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(w_o_p)
                    if self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                        self.out_proj.bias.data.zero_()

            # ---------- conv1d: identity-at-init passthrough ----------
            self.conv1d.weight.data.zero_()
            if self.conv1d.weight.dim() == 3:
                # shape (d_inner, 1, d_conv)
                self.conv1d.weight.data[:, 0, -1] = 1.0
            if self.conv1d.bias is not None:
                self.conv1d.bias.data.zero_()

        # ---- Sanity check ----
        for name, mod in (
            ("in_proj", self.in_proj),
            ("x_proj", self.x_proj),
            ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
        ):
            n = float(torch.linalg.norm(mod.weight.data.float()).item())
            if n < 1e-6:
                logger.warning("MambaBlock: '%s' near-zero norm (%.2e)", name, n)

        if self.freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # State helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _read_state(
        self, past_state: Any
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None, int]:
        """Returns (h, conv_state, dt_state, past_len)."""
        if past_state is None:
            return None, None, None, 0
        if isinstance(past_state, (tuple, list)):
            if len(past_state) >= 3:
                h = past_state[0]
                conv_state = past_state[1]
                dt_state = past_state[2] if len(past_state) > 2 else None
                past_len = h.shape[-1] if h is not None else 0
                # past_len from conv_state if available
                if conv_state is not None:
                    past_len = conv_state.shape[-1] - (self.d_conv - 1)
                    past_len = max(0, past_len)
                return h, conv_state, dt_state, past_len
            elif len(past_state) == 2:
                return past_state[0], past_state[1], None, 0
            elif len(past_state) == 1:
                return past_state[0], None, None, 0
        return None, None, None, 0

    def _recurrent_scan(
        self,
        u: torch.Tensor,  # (B, d_inner, L)
        delta: torch.Tensor,  # (B, d_inner, L)
        A: torch.Tensor,  # (d_inner, d_state)
        B_ssm: torch.Tensor,  # (B, d_state, L)
        C_ssm: torch.Tensor,  # (B, d_state, L)
        D: torch.Tensor,  # (d_inner,)
        state: torch.Tensor | None,  # (B, d_inner, d_state)
        chunk_size: int = 32,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Recurrent selective scan with chunked activation checkpointing and bmm.

        Fix 1: Split L into chunks of size chunk_size and checkpoint each chunk
               during training to bound autograd memory to O(chunk_size).
        Fix 2: Use torch.bmm instead of torch.einsum inside the loop for cuBLAS
               acceleration and shared autograd backward kernels.
        """
        B, d_inner, L = u.shape
        d_state = A.shape[1]

        target_dtype = u.dtype
        h = (
            state.to(target_dtype)
            if state is not None
            else torch.zeros(B, d_inner, d_state, device=u.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        )

        delta = F.softplus(delta).to(target_dtype)
        delta_a = delta.unsqueeze(-1) * A.view(1, d_inner, 1, d_state).to(target_dtype)
        delta_a = torch.exp(delta_a).to(target_dtype)
        delta_b_u = (
            delta.unsqueeze(-1)
            * B_ssm.permute(0, 2, 1).unsqueeze(1).to(target_dtype)
            * u.unsqueeze(-1)
        ).to(target_dtype)
        C_in = C_ssm.to(target_dtype)

        def _scan_chunk(
            h_init: torch.Tensor,
            da_c: torch.Tensor,
            dbu_c: torch.Tensor,
            c_c: torch.Tensor,
        ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
            L_c = da_c.shape[2]
            ys_c = []
            curr_h = h_init
            for i in range(L_c):
                curr_h = da_c[:, :, i, :] * curr_h + dbu_c[:, :, i, :]
                c_t = (
                    c_c[:, :, i].contiguous().unsqueeze(-1)
                )  # (B, d_state, 1) contiguous for cuBLAS
                y_t = torch.bmm(curr_h, c_t).squeeze(-1)  # (B, d_inner)
                ys_c.append(y_t)
            return torch.stack(ys_c, dim=-1), curr_h

        # Chunked scan with activation checkpointing during training
        ys_all = []
        import torch.utils.checkpoint as cp

        use_cp = self.training and torch.is_grad_enabled()

        for s in range(0, L, chunk_size):
            e = min(s + chunk_size, L)
            da_slice = delta_a[:, :, s:e, :]
            dbu_slice = delta_b_u[:, :, s:e, :]
            c_slice = C_in[:, :, s:e]

            if use_cp:
                y_chunk, h = cp.checkpoint(
                    _scan_chunk,
                    h,
                    da_slice,
                    dbu_slice,
                    c_slice,
                    use_reentrant=False,
                )
            else:
                y_chunk, h = _scan_chunk(
                    h,
                    da_slice,
                    dbu_slice,
                    c_slice,
                )
            ys_all.append(y_chunk)

        y = torch.cat(ys_all, dim=-1)  # (B, d_inner, L)
        y = y + u * D.view(1, d_inner, 1).to(target_dtype)
        return y, h

    def _parallel_scan(
        self,
        u: torch.Tensor,
        delta: torch.Tensor,
        A: torch.Tensor,
        B_ssm: torch.Tensor,
        C_ssm: torch.Tensor,
        D: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Parallel scan dispatch with 3-tier fallback:
          Path 1: Official CUDA kernel via selective_scan (Linux only, when compiled)
          Path 2: Triton selective_scan (if mamba_ssm Triton ops available)
          Path 3: Fast chunked parallel scan (framework.core.selective_scan, O(1) ops per chunk)
          Path 4: Chunked recurrent scan with activation checkpointing (failsafe fallback)
        """
        # Path 1: Official CUDA kernel
        if HAS_MAMBA_CUDA and selective_scan is not None:
            try:
                return selective_scan(
                    u=u,
                    delta=delta,
                    A=A,
                    B=B_ssm,
                    C=C_ssm,
                    D=D,
                    delta_softplus=True,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("CUDA selective_scan failed: %s", e)

        # Path 2: Triton kernel
        if HAS_TRITON_MAMBA and triton_selective_scan is not None:
            try:
                y = triton_selective_scan(
                    u.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
                    delta.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
                    A,
                    B_ssm.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
                    C_ssm.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
                    D,
                    delta_softplus=True,
                )
                return y.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Triton selective_scan failed: %s", e)

        # Path 3: Fast chunked parallel scan (avoids token-by-token Python loop)
        if selective_scan is not None:
            try:
                return selective_scan(
                    u=u,
                    delta=delta,
                    A=A,
                    B=B_ssm,
                    C=C_ssm,
                    D=D,
                    delta_softplus=True,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Parallel selective_scan fallback failed: %s", e)

        # Path 4: Failsafe sequential / chunked recurrent fallback
        y, _ = self._recurrent_scan(u, delta, A, B_ssm, C_ssm, D, None)
        return y

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward (MB-02, MB-03, MB-09, MB-12)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        past_state: Any | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool = False,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        if kwargs.get("encoder_hidden_states") is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("MambaBlock does not support cross-attention")

        # Backward-compat: accept past_key_value as an alias for past_state
        if past_state is None and past_key_value is not None:
            past_state = past_key_value

        B, L, _ = hidden_states.shape
        device = hidden_states.device
        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        # ---- Read state ----
        h_prev, conv_prev, _, past_len = self._read_state(past_state)

        # ---- 1. Input projection ----
        xz = self.in_proj(hidden_states)  # (B, L, 2*d_inner)
        x_ssm, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)  # each (B, L, d_inner)

        # ---- 2. Depthwise conv1d with streaming state ----
        if conv_prev is not None:
            # conv_prev: (B, d_inner, d_conv - 1)
            # Concatenate along time axis
            x_cat = torch.cat(
                [conv_prev.transpose(1, 2), x_ssm], dim=1
            )  # (B, L + d_conv - 1, d_inner)
        else:
            # FIXED: Explicit left-padding to prevent future token leakage
            x_cat = F.pad(x_ssm.transpose(1, 2), (self.d_conv - 1, 0)).transpose(1, 2)

        x_conv_full = self.conv1d(x_cat.transpose(1, 2))  # (B, d_inner, L)
        # Take only the L last positions (strictly causal)
        x_conv = x_conv_full[..., -L:].transpose(1, 2)  # (B, L, d_inner)

        # New conv state: last (d_conv - 1) tokens of input
        if self.d_conv - 1 <= L:
            conv_next = x_cat[:, -(self.d_conv - 1) :, :].transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        else:
            # pad with zero if sequence shorter than kernel
            pad = self.d_conv - 1 - L
            conv_next = (
                torch.cat(
                    [
                        torch.zeros(B, pad, self.d_inner, device=device, dtype=x_cat.dtype),
                        x_cat,
                    ],
                    dim=1,
                )
                .transpose(1, 2)
                .contiguous()
            )

        x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

        # ---- 3. Selective parameters ----
        x_dbl = self.x_proj(x_ssm)  # (B, L, dt_rank + 2*d_state)
        dt, B_ssm, C_ssm = x_dbl.split([self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state], dim=-1)
        dt = self.dt_proj(dt)  # (B, L, d_inner)

        A = -torch.exp(self.A_log)  # (d_inner, d_state)

        # ---- 4. Selective scan ----
        u_t = x_ssm.transpose(1, 2)  # (B, d_inner, L)
        dt_t = dt.transpose(1, 2)
        B_t = B_ssm.transpose(1, 2)
        C_t = C_ssm.transpose(1, 2)

        if h_prev is None:
            y = self._parallel_scan(u_t, dt_t, A, B_t, C_t, self.D)
            h_next = None
        else:
            y, h_next = self._recurrent_scan(u_t, dt_t, A, B_t, C_t, self.D, h_prev)

        y = y.transpose(1, 2)  # (B, L, d_inner)

        # ---- 5. Bidirectional (encoder only) ----
        if self.bidirectional and not self.is_decoder:
            x_rev = torch.flip(x_ssm, dims=[1])
            x_dbl_rev = self.x_proj_bwd(x_rev)
            dt_b, B_b, C_b = x_dbl_rev.split([self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state], dim=-1)
            dt_b = self.dt_proj_bwd(dt_b)
            A_b = -torch.exp(self.A_log_bwd)
            y_b = self._parallel_scan(
                torch.flip(x_ssm, dims=[1]).transpose(1, 2),
                dt_b.transpose(1, 2),
                A_b,
                B_b.transpose(1, 2),
                C_b.transpose(1, 2),
                self.D_bwd,
            )
            y_b = torch.flip(y_b.transpose(1, 2), dims=[1])
            y = 0.5 * (y + y_b)

        # ---- 6. Gating ----
        y = y.to(target_dtype) * F.silu(z.to(target_dtype))

        # ---- 7. Output projection ----
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            # Nested encoder: outer layer will project. Skip out_proj.
            out = y.to(target_dtype)
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(y).to(target_dtype)

        # ---- 8. attention_mask (MB-03, MB-09) ----
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                out = out * attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
                # (B, Lq, Lk) — take the last column (query's validity) or diagonal
                m = (
                    attention_mask.diagonal(dim1=-2, dim2=-1)
                    if attention_mask.size(-1) == L
                    else attention_mask[..., -1]
                )
                out = out * m.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                # (B, 1, Lq, Lk) — same reduction
                m = attention_mask.squeeze(1)
                m = m.diagonal(dim1=-2, dim2=-1) if m.size(-1) == L else m[..., -1]
                out = out * m.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)

        # ---- 9. Output tuple (MB-12) ----
        present_state = (h_next, conv_next)
        attn_weights = None

        fmt = self._return_format
        if fmt in ("bert", "gpt2"):
            return (out, attn_weights)
        # llama
        return (out, attn_weights, present_state)

Faithful S6 / Mamba block, production-hardened.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def copy_weights(self, src: nn.Module) ‑> None

def copy_weights(self, src: nn.Module) -> None:
    """
    Distill attention weights (W_q, W_k, W_v, W_o) into Mamba's S6
    parameters via low-rank mapping:
        W_v  → in_proj (SSM input path)
        W_k  → x_proj (B branch, via SVD)
        W_q  → x_proj (C branch, via SVD)
        W_o  → out_proj
        dt   → kept at initialization (learnable, input-dependent via x_proj)
    """
    if src is None:
        return

    extracted: dict[str, torch.Tensor | None] = {
        "q": None,
        "k": None,
        "v": None,
        "o": None,
    }

    # --- 1. UniversalAdapter ---
    try:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        for proj in ("q", "k", "v", "o"):
            try:
                w, _ = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = w
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    # --- 2. GPT-2 fused c_attn / c_proj ---
    if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None and extracted["q"] is None:
        c = src.c_attn
        base = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
        w = getattr(base, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
        if w is not None:
            w = w.data.clone()
            is_conv = (
                c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                or base.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                or (w.ndim == 2 and w.shape[1] == 3 * self.d_model)
            )
            if is_conv:
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            split = w.size(0) // 3
            extracted["q"] = w[:split, :]
            extracted["k"] = w[split : 2 * split, :]
            extracted["v"] = w[2 * split :, :]

    if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None and extracted["o"] is None:
        o = src.c_proj
        base = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
        w = getattr(base, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
        if w is not None:
            w = w.data.clone()
            if w.ndim == 2 and w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]:
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            extracted["o"] = w

    # --- 3. Flat / nested (BERT / RoBERTa) ---
    named_map = {
        "q": ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"),
        "k": ("k_proj", "k_lin", "key"),
        "v": ("v_proj", "v_lin", "value"),
        "o": ("o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"),
    }
    nested = getattr(src, "self", None)
    for proj, names in named_map.items():
        if extracted[proj] is not None:
            continue
        # Try nested first
        if nested is not None:
            for n in names:
                mod = getattr(nested, n, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj] = mod.weight.data
                    break
        if extracted[proj] is not None:
            continue
        # Then flat on src
        for n in names:
            mod = getattr(src, n, None)
            if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                extracted[proj] = mod.weight.data
                break
        # Nested output.dense for "o"
        if extracted["o"] is None and proj == "o":
            out = getattr(src, "output", None)
            dense = getattr(out, "dense", None) if out is not None else None
            if dense is not None and hasattr(dense, "weight"):
                extracted["o"] = dense.weight.data

    # Mark nested encoder for later (MB-05)
    cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
    if cls_name in (
        "BertSelfAttention",
        "RobertaSelfAttention",
        "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
    ) or (nested is not None and hasattr(nested, "query")):
        self.is_nested_encoder = True

    # ---- Apply mapping (MB-07, MB-08) ----
    dtype = self.in_proj.weight.dtype
    device = self.in_proj.weight.device

    def _to_t(x: torch.Tensor | None, r: int, c: int) -> torch.Tensor | None:
        """Project W (any shape) to target shape (r, c) via SVD."""
        if x is None:
            return None
        x = x.float()
        if x.ndim != 2:
            return None
        M, N = x.shape
        u, s, vh = torch.linalg.svd(x, full_matrices=False)
        k = min(r, c, s.numel())

        u_r = u[: min(r, M), :k]
        vh_c = vh[:k, : min(c, N)]
        core = (u_r * s[:k].unsqueeze(0)) @ vh_c

        out = torch.zeros(r, c, dtype=torch.float32, device=x.device)
        out[: core.size(0), : core.size(1)] = core

        if r > core.size(0):
            rem_r = r - core.size(0)
            reps_r = (rem_r + core.size(0) - 1) // core.size(0)
            tiled_r = core.repeat(reps_r, 1)[:rem_r, :]
            out[core.size(0) :, : core.size(1)] = tiled_r

        if c > core.size(1):
            rem_c = c - core.size(1)
            reps_c = (rem_c + core.size(1) - 1) // core.size(1)
            tiled_c = out[:, : core.size(1)].repeat(1, reps_c)[:, :rem_c]
            out[:, core.size(1) :] = tiled_c

        return out.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)

    with torch.no_grad():
        w_q = extracted["q"]
        w_k = extracted["k"]
        w_v = extracted["v"]
        w_o = extracted["o"]

        # ---------- in_proj: (2*d_inner, d_model) ----------
        # x_ssm path = rows [0 : d_inner]
        # z     path = rows [d_inner : 2*d_inner]
        if w_v is not None:
            w_v_p = _to_t(w_v, self.d_inner, self.d_model)
            if w_v_p is not None:
                # x_ssm: full projection from W_v
                self.in_proj.weight.data[: self.d_inner, : self.d_model].copy_(w_v_p)
                # z: scaled copy so the gate starts balanced
                self.in_proj.weight.data[self.d_inner :, : self.d_model].copy_(0.5 * w_v_p)

        # ---------- x_proj: (dt_rank + 2*d_state, d_inner) ----------
        # rows [0 : dt_rank]                     → dt  (keep init)
        # rows [dt_rank : dt_rank + d_state]     → B   (from W_k)
        # rows [dt_rank + d_state : ...]         → C   (from W_q)
        # MB-01: DO NOT zero the whole x_proj; only reset B/C rows.
        with torch.no_grad():
            b_start = self.dt_rank
            b_end = self.dt_rank + self.d_state
            c_start = b_end
            c_end = c_start + self.d_state

            if w_k is not None:
                b_mat = _to_t(w_k, self.d_state, self.d_inner)
                if b_mat is not None:
                    self.x_proj.weight.data[b_start:b_end, :].copy_(b_mat)
            if w_q is not None:
                c_mat = _to_t(w_q, self.d_state, self.d_inner)
                if c_mat is not None:
                    self.x_proj.weight.data[c_start:c_end, :].copy_(c_mat)

        # ---------- out_proj: (d_model, d_inner) ----------
        if w_o is not None:
            w_o_p = _to_t(w_o, self.d_model, self.d_inner)
            if w_o_p is not None:
                self.out_proj.weight.data.copy_(w_o_p)
                if self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.out_proj.bias.data.zero_()

        # ---------- conv1d: identity-at-init passthrough ----------
        self.conv1d.weight.data.zero_()
        if self.conv1d.weight.dim() == 3:
            # shape (d_inner, 1, d_conv)
            self.conv1d.weight.data[:, 0, -1] = 1.0
        if self.conv1d.bias is not None:
            self.conv1d.bias.data.zero_()

    # ---- Sanity check ----
    for name, mod in (
        ("in_proj", self.in_proj),
        ("x_proj", self.x_proj),
        ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
    ):
        n = float(torch.linalg.norm(mod.weight.data.float()).item())
        if n < 1e-6:
            logger.warning("MambaBlock: '%s' near-zero norm (%.2e)", name, n)

    if self.freeze_base:
        self.freeze_base_weights()

Distill attention weights (W_q, W_k, W_v, W_o) into Mamba's S6 parameters via low-rank mapping: W_v → in_proj (SSM input path) W_k → x_proj (B branch, via SVD) W_q → x_proj (C branch, via SVD) W_o → out_proj dt → kept at initialization (learnable, input-dependent via x_proj)

def forward(self,
hidden_states: torch.Tensor,

past_key_value: Any | None = None,

Expand source code 
def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    *args: Any,
    past_state: Any | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool = False,
    **kwargs: Any,
):
    if kwargs.get("encoder_hidden_states") is not None:
        raise NotImplementedError("MambaBlock does not support cross-attention")

    # Backward-compat: accept past_key_value as an alias for past_state
    if past_state is None and past_key_value is not None:
        past_state = past_key_value

    B, L, _ = hidden_states.shape
    device = hidden_states.device
    target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype

    # ---- Read state ----
    h_prev, conv_prev, _, past_len = self._read_state(past_state)

    # ---- 1. Input projection ----
    xz = self.in_proj(hidden_states)  # (B, L, 2*d_inner)
    x_ssm, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)  # each (B, L, d_inner)

    # ---- 2. Depthwise conv1d with streaming state ----
    if conv_prev is not None:
        # conv_prev: (B, d_inner, d_conv - 1)
        # Concatenate along time axis
        x_cat = torch.cat(
            [conv_prev.transpose(1, 2), x_ssm], dim=1
        )  # (B, L + d_conv - 1, d_inner)
    else:
        # FIXED: Explicit left-padding to prevent future token leakage
        x_cat = F.pad(x_ssm.transpose(1, 2), (self.d_conv - 1, 0)).transpose(1, 2)

    x_conv_full = self.conv1d(x_cat.transpose(1, 2))  # (B, d_inner, L)
    # Take only the L last positions (strictly causal)
    x_conv = x_conv_full[..., -L:].transpose(1, 2)  # (B, L, d_inner)

    # New conv state: last (d_conv - 1) tokens of input
    if self.d_conv - 1 <= L:
        conv_next = x_cat[:, -(self.d_conv - 1) :, :].transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
    else:
        # pad with zero if sequence shorter than kernel
        pad = self.d_conv - 1 - L
        conv_next = (
            torch.cat(
                [
                    torch.zeros(B, pad, self.d_inner, device=device, dtype=x_cat.dtype),
                    x_cat,
                ],
                dim=1,
            )
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

    x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

    # ---- 3. Selective parameters ----
    x_dbl = self.x_proj(x_ssm)  # (B, L, dt_rank + 2*d_state)
    dt, B_ssm, C_ssm = x_dbl.split([self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state], dim=-1)
    dt = self.dt_proj(dt)  # (B, L, d_inner)

    A = -torch.exp(self.A_log)  # (d_inner, d_state)

    # ---- 4. Selective scan ----
    u_t = x_ssm.transpose(1, 2)  # (B, d_inner, L)
    dt_t = dt.transpose(1, 2)
    B_t = B_ssm.transpose(1, 2)
    C_t = C_ssm.transpose(1, 2)

    if h_prev is None:
        y = self._parallel_scan(u_t, dt_t, A, B_t, C_t, self.D)
        h_next = None
    else:
        y, h_next = self._recurrent_scan(u_t, dt_t, A, B_t, C_t, self.D, h_prev)

    y = y.transpose(1, 2)  # (B, L, d_inner)

    # ---- 5. Bidirectional (encoder only) ----
    if self.bidirectional and not self.is_decoder:
        x_rev = torch.flip(x_ssm, dims=[1])
        x_dbl_rev = self.x_proj_bwd(x_rev)
        dt_b, B_b, C_b = x_dbl_rev.split([self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state], dim=-1)
        dt_b = self.dt_proj_bwd(dt_b)
        A_b = -torch.exp(self.A_log_bwd)
        y_b = self._parallel_scan(
            torch.flip(x_ssm, dims=[1]).transpose(1, 2),
            dt_b.transpose(1, 2),
            A_b,
            B_b.transpose(1, 2),
            C_b.transpose(1, 2),
            self.D_bwd,
        )
        y_b = torch.flip(y_b.transpose(1, 2), dims=[1])
        y = 0.5 * (y + y_b)

    # ---- 6. Gating ----
    y = y.to(target_dtype) * F.silu(z.to(target_dtype))

    # ---- 7. Output projection ----
    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        # Nested encoder: outer layer will project. Skip out_proj.
        out = y.to(target_dtype)
    else:
        out = self.out_proj(y).to(target_dtype)

    # ---- 8. attention_mask (MB-03, MB-09) ----
    if attention_mask is not None:
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            out = out * attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            # (B, Lq, Lk) — take the last column (query's validity) or diagonal
            m = (
                attention_mask.diagonal(dim1=-2, dim2=-1)
                if attention_mask.size(-1) == L
                else attention_mask[..., -1]
            )
            out = out * m.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            # (B, 1, Lq, Lk) — same reduction
            m = attention_mask.squeeze(1)
            m = m.diagonal(dim1=-2, dim2=-1) if m.size(-1) == L else m[..., -1]
            out = out * m.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)

    # ---- 9. Output tuple (MB-12) ----
    present_state = (h_next, conv_next)
    attn_weights = None

    fmt = self._return_format
    if fmt in ("bert", "gpt2"):
        return (out, attn_weights)
    # llama
    return (out, attn_weights, present_state)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
    """MB-04: match the framework's LoRA-freeze convention."""
    for p in self.parameters():
        p.requires_grad = False
