@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("mamba_svd_flash", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class MambaSVDFlash(BaseAttention):
    """
    Hybrid SSM + MHA with per-chunk SVD and learnable per-head gating.

    State cache layout (for streaming):
        past_key_value = {
            "ssm_h": (B, D),           SSM hidden state h_{L-1}
            "attn_k": (B, H, L, Dh),   attention keys
            "attn_v": (B, H, L, Dh),   attention values
            "seq_len": int,
        }
    Or tuple form: (ssm_h, attn_k, attn_v, seq_len).
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 16,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        hidden_size: int | None = None,
        num_heads: int | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        bottleneck_dim: int = 64,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn

        # ---- d_model ----
        dim = (
            kwargs.get("d_model")
            or kwargs.get("hidden_size")
            or kwargs.get("dim")
            or d_model
            or hidden_size
        )
        if dim is None:
            if config is not None:
                if isinstance(config, int):
                    dim = config
                elif isinstance(config, dict):
                    dim = config.get("hidden_size", config.get("dim", 128))
                else:
                    dim = getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "dim", 128))
            elif source_attn is not None:
                cand = getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "hidden_size",
                    getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "embed_dim",
                        getattr(source_attn, "all_head_size", None),
                    ),
                )
                if cand is None and hasattr(source_attn, "q_proj"):
                    q = getattr(source_attn, "q_proj", None)
                    if q is not None and hasattr(q, "in_features"):
                        cand = q.in_features
                dim = cand or 128

        config_obj = None if isinstance(config, int) else config
        super().__init__(config_obj, rank if rank is not None else 16)

        self.d_model = int(dim or 128)
        self.dim = self.d_model
        self.hidden_size = self.d_model
        self.rank = max(1, int(rank if rank is not None else 16))  # MSF-FIX-02

        # ---- LoRA config ----
        if config_obj is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(
                getattr(
                    config_obj,
                    "lora_rank",
                    getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
                )
            )
            self.lora_alpha = float(
                getattr(
                    config_obj,
                    "lora_alpha",
                    getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
                )
            )
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)))
            self.lora_alpha = float(
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
            )
        else:
            self.lora_rank = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))
            self.lora_alpha = float(kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        model_type = (
            str(getattr(config_obj, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            if config_obj is not None
            else ""
        )
        is_dec = False
        if config_obj is not None:
            is_dec = bool(
                getattr(config_obj, "is_decoder", False) or getattr(config_obj, "is_causal", False)
            )
        if any(
            model_type.startswith(p)
            for p in (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        if source_attn is not None:
            if hasattr(source_attn, "c_attn") or getattr(source_attn, "is_decoder", False):
                is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # ---- heads / GQA ----
        n_heads = num_heads or kwargs.get("n_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
        if n_heads is None:
            if config_obj is not None:
                n_heads = getattr(
                    config_obj,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(config_obj, "n_heads", 12),
                )
            elif source_attn is not None:
                n_heads = getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(source_attn, "num_heads", 12),
                )
            else:
                n_heads = 12
        self.num_heads = int(n_heads)
        if self.d_model % self.num_heads != 0:
            logger.warning("MambaSVDFlash: d_model %% num_heads != 0; forcing num_heads=1")
            self.num_heads = 1
        self.head_dim = self.d_model // max(1, self.num_heads)

        cfg_kv = None
        if config_obj is not None:
            cfg_kv = getattr(
                config_obj,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                getattr(config_obj, "num_kv_heads", None),
            )
        if cfg_kv is None:
            cfg_kv = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads", self.num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(int(cfg_kv), self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # ---- SSM sub-module (MSF-FIX-10) ----
        bd = int(
            getattr(comp_config, "bottleneck_dim", bottleneck_dim)
            if comp_config
            else bottleneck_dim
        )
        self.ssm_fwd = SVDS6StateSpace(self.d_model, rank=self.rank, bottleneck_dim=bd)

        if not self.is_decoder:
            self.ssm_bwd = SVDS6StateSpace(self.d_model, rank=self.rank, bottleneck_dim=bd)
            # MSF-FIX-13: per-head mix
            self.bidirectional_mix = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads))
        else:
            self.ssm_bwd = None
            self.bidirectional_mix = None

        # ---- Attention projections ----
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.d_model,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.d_model,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.d_model,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.d_model,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # ---- Gating (MSF-FIX-12: LoRALinear, per-head output) ----
        self.gating = LoRALinear(
            self.d_model,
            self.num_heads,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        with torch.no_grad():
            nn.init.zeros_(self.gating.weight)
            if self.gating.bias is not None:
                nn.init.constant_(self.gating.bias, -2.0)

        # ---- Dropout ----
        dropout_p = 0.1
        if config_obj is not None:
            dropout_p = float(
                getattr(
                    config_obj,
                    "dropout",
                    getattr(config_obj, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
                )
            )
        elif comp_config is not None:
            dropout_p = float(getattr(comp_config, "dropout", 0.1))
        else:
            dropout_p = float(kwargs.get("dropout", 0.1))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self.source_attn = source_attn
        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None
        )

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ---- aliases ----
    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def freeze_base_weights(self):
        # MSF-FIX-09: freeze SSM projections too
        for m in (
            self.q_proj,
            self.k_proj,
            self.v_proj,
            self.out_proj,
            self.gating,
            self.ssm_fwd.down,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_u,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_v,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_sigma,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_b,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_c,
        ):
            for p in m.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False
        if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
            for p in self.ssm_bwd.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = False
        # Keep LoRA adapters trainable
        for m in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj, self.gating):
            for name, p in m.named_parameters():
                if "lora_" in name:
                    p.requires_grad = True

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self):
        for m in (
            self.q_proj,
            self.k_proj,
            self.v_proj,
            self.out_proj,
            self.gating,
            self.ssm_fwd.down,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_u,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_v,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_sigma,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_b,
            self.ssm_fwd.up_c,
        ):
            for p in m.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = True
        if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
            for p in self.ssm_bwd.parameters():
                p.requires_grad = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        if old_attn is None:
            return

        cls_name = old_attn.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(old_attn, a) for a in ("out_proj", "out_lin", "c_proj", "dense", "o_proj")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(old_attn, "self")
                and hasattr(old_attn.self, "query")
                and hasattr(old_attn, "output")
                and hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn") and old_attn.c_attn is not None:
            c = old_attn.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            cw = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if cw is not None:
                cw = cw.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (cw.ndim == 2 and cw.shape[0] < cw.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                split = cw.size(0) // 3
                bmod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c, "bias", None))
                cb = bmod.data.clone() if bmod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (cw[:split], cb[:split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (
                    cw[split : 2 * split],
                    cb[split : 2 * split] if cb is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    cw[2 * split :],
                    cb[2 * split :] if cb is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(old_attn, "c_proj") and old_attn.c_proj is not None:
                o = old_attn.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
                ow = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
                if ow is not None:
                    ow = ow.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (ow.ndim == 2 and ow.shape[0] < ow.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                    bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (ow, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)

        # Nested (BERT/RoBERTa)
        elif hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn.self, "query"):
            s = old_attn.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                    extracted[key] = (m.weight.data, b.data if b is not None else None)
            if hasattr(old_attn, "output") and hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                d = old_attn.output.dense
                b = getattr(d, "bias", None)
                extracted["o"] = (d.weight.data, b.data if b is not None else None)

        # Flat
        else:
            cand = {
                "q": ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"),
                "k": ("k_proj", "k_lin", "key"),
                "v": ("v_proj", "v_lin", "value"),
                "o": ("out_proj", "out_lin", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in cand.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(old_attn, n):
                        m = getattr(old_attn, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[key] = (
                                m.weight.data,
                                b.data if b is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning("MambaSVDFlash: missing projections %s from %s", missing_after, cls_name)

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for key, target in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[key]
                if w is None:
                    if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target.weight)
                        if target.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)
                if (
                    w.shape != target.weight.shape
                    and w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape
                ):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()

                out_d = min(target.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                target.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype)
                )
                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    mb = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))
                elif target.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)

                if self.freeze_base:
                    target.freeze_base_weights()

            # MSF-FIX-17: initialise SSM projections from W_q, W_k using SVD
            w_q, _ = extracted["q"]
            w_k, _ = extracted["k"]
            w_v, _ = extracted["v"]

            self._init_ssm_from_attention(w_k, w_q, w_v)

        if hasattr(old_attn, "rotary_emb") and old_attn.rotary_emb is not None:
            self.rotary_emb = old_attn.rotary_emb

        if self.freeze_base:
            self.freeze_base_weights()

    def _init_ssm_from_attention(
        self,
        w_k: torch.Tensor | None,
        w_q: torch.Tensor | None,
        w_v: torch.Tensor | None,
    ):
        """
        MSF-FIX-17: use SVD of W_k, W_q to seed the low-rank SSM bases.
        We do NOT train these directly; we compute a least-squares fit of the
        bottleneck outputs to approximate U/V/Sigma/B/C from these weights.
        Simpler approach: leave SSM at random init but scale to match.
        """
        # Practical approach: fit the up_b / up_c linear layers so that
        # B(x) ≈ W_k @ x and C(x) ≈ W_q @ x  (best-effort, through the bottleneck)
        try:
            if w_k is None and w_q is None:
                return

            D = self.d_model
            target_b = w_k if w_k is not None else w_q
            target_c = w_q if w_q is not None else w_k

            if target_b is None or target_c is None:
                return

            # Ensure shapes match (D, D)
            def _fit(mat):
                if mat is None:
                    return
                if mat.ndim != 2:
                    return
                if mat.shape != (D, D):
                    return
                mat_f = mat.float()
                # SVD to get top-r singular values
                try:
                    U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(mat_f, full_matrices=False)
                except Exception:
                    return
                r = min(self.rank, S.numel())
                # Project through the bottleneck using the top-r singular directions
                # Store in up_b / up_c (approximation; leaves low-rank heads)
                with torch.no_grad():
                    if self.ssm_fwd.up_b.weight.shape == (
                        D,
                        self.ssm_fwd.bottleneck_dim,
                    ):
                        # up_b is (D, bottleneck_dim); use Vh rows as bottleneck features
                        Vh_r = Vh[:r, :]  # (r, D)
                        # down projection: (bd, D)  -- we set down.weight rows to Vh_r
                        self.ssm_fwd.down.weight.data[:r, :].copy_(
                            Vh_r.to(
                                self.ssm_fwd.down.weight.device,
                                self.ssm_fwd.down.weight.dtype,
                            )
                        )

        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("SSM weight init from attention failed: %s", e)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Mask helper (MSF-FIX-04)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_valid_mask(
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
        B: int,
        L: int,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        if attention_mask is None:
            return torch.ones(B, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

        def _to_bool(t):
            if t.dtype == torch.bool:
                return t
            if t.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return t > -1e4
            return t > 0

        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask)
            if m.shape == (B, L):
                return m
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask)
            if m.shape[0] == 1:
                return m[0]
            if m.shape[-1] == L:
                return m[:, -1, :] if m.shape[1] == L else m[0, :, :]
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            m = _to_bool(attention_mask)
            m = m.any(dim=-2)
            if m.dim() == 3:
                m = m[:, 0, :]
            if m.shape == (B, L):
                return m

        try:
            m = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)
            if isinstance(m, torch.Tensor) and m.shape == (B, L):
                return m.bool()
        except Exception:
            pass

        return torch.ones(B, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Scan (MSF-FIX-06, MSF-FIX-07, MSF-FIX-08)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _ssm_scan(
        self,
        U: torch.Tensor,  # (B, L, D, r)
        Sigma: torch.Tensor,  # (B, L, r)
        V: torch.Tensor,  # (B, L, r, D)
        B_seq: torch.Tensor,  # (B, L, D)
        C_seq: torch.Tensor,  # (B, L, D)
        x: torch.Tensor,  # (B, L, D)
        mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,  # (B, L) bool
        h0: torch.Tensor | None = None,  # (B, D)
        reverse: bool = False,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Sequential scan with a chunked loop.
        Returns (outputs, h_final).
        - For forward pass, h_final corresponds to step L-1.
        - For reverse pass, the caller flips before/after.
        """
        B, L, D = x.shape
        Sigma.shape[-1]
        device = x.device

        # MSF-FIX-08: preallocate output tensor (no list)
        H = torch.zeros(B, L, D, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        h = torch.zeros(B, D, device=device, dtype=torch.float32) if h0 is None else h0.float()

        US = U.float() * Sigma.float().unsqueeze(-2)  # (B, L, D, r)
        V_f = V.float()
        bx = B_seq.float() * x.float()

        steps = range(L - 1, -1, -1) if reverse else range(L)

        for t in steps:
            # V_f[:, t] is shape (B, r, D), h is (B, D)
            v_t = torch.einsum("brd,bd->br", V_f[:, t], h)  # (B, r)
            h_trans = torch.einsum("bdr,br->bd", US[:, t], v_t)  # (B, D)
            h_next = h_trans + bx[:, t]
            if mask is not None:
                m_t = mask[:, t].unsqueeze(-1).to(h_next.dtype)
                h = torch.where(m_t.bool(), h_next, h)
            else:
                h = h_next
            h = torch.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            H[:, t] = h

        out = C_seq.float() * H
        return out.to(x.dtype), h.to(x.dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        position_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("MambaSVDFlash does not support cross-attention.")

        # MSF-FIX-16: bounded unpack
        if isinstance(hidden_states, (tuple, list)) and hidden_states:
            hidden_states = hidden_states[0]
        if isinstance(attention_mask, (tuple, list)) and attention_mask:
            attention_mask = attention_mask[0]
        if not hidden_states.is_contiguous():
            hidden_states = hidden_states.contiguous()

        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        dtype_in = hidden_states.dtype
        device = hidden_states.device

        # MSF-FIX-15: read cache
        past_h = None
        past_attn_k = None
        past_attn_v = None
        past_len = 0
        if past_key_value is not None:
            if isinstance(past_key_value, dict):
                past_h = past_key_value.get("ssm_h", None)
                past_attn_k = past_key_value.get("attn_k", None)
                past_attn_v = past_key_value.get("attn_v", None)
                past_len = int(past_key_value.get("seq_len", 0))
            elif isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)):
                if len(past_key_value) >= 3:
                    past_h = past_key_value[0]
                    past_attn_k = past_key_value[1]
                    past_attn_v = past_key_value[2]
                    past_len = (
                        int(past_key_value[3])
                        if len(past_key_value) > 3
                        else (past_attn_k.shape[-2] if past_attn_k is not None else 0)
                    )

        mask_bool = self._normalize_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, device)

        # ---------------- 1. SSM path ----------------
        U_f, Sig_f, V_f, B_f, C_f = self.ssm_fwd(hidden_states)

        if self.is_decoder:
            ssm_out, ssm_h_next = self._ssm_scan(
                U_f,
                Sig_f,
                V_f,
                B_f,
                C_f,
                hidden_states,
                mask=mask_bool,
                h0=past_h,
                reverse=False,
            )
        else:
            # Forward direction
            ssm_fwd, _ = self._ssm_scan(
                U_f,
                Sig_f,
                V_f,
                B_f,
                C_f,
                hidden_states,
                mask=mask_bool,
                h0=None,
                reverse=False,
            )
            # Backward direction (only meaningful for encoder)
            if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
                U_b, Sig_b, V_b, B_b, C_b = self.ssm_bwd(hidden_states)
                x_rev = torch.flip(hidden_states, dims=[1])
                mask_rev = torch.flip(mask_bool, dims=[1]) if mask_bool is not None else None
                ssm_bwd_rev, _ = self._ssm_scan(
                    U_b,
                    Sig_b,
                    V_b,
                    B_b,
                    C_b,
                    x_rev,
                    mask=mask_rev,
                    h0=None,
                    reverse=False,
                )
                ssm_bwd = torch.flip(ssm_bwd_rev, dims=[1])
                # MSF-FIX-13: per-head mix
                w = torch.sigmoid(self.bidirectional_mix).view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1)
                ssm_fwd_h = ssm_fwd.view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
                ssm_bwd_h = ssm_bwd.view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
                ssm_out = (
                    (w * ssm_fwd_h + (1 - w) * ssm_bwd_h).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, D)
                )
                ssm_h_next = None
            else:
                ssm_out = ssm_fwd
                ssm_h_next = None

        # ---------------- 2. Attention path ----------------
        Q = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        K = (
            self.k_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        V_a = (
            self.v_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # MSF-FIX-18: RoPE with offset
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_ids is None:
            position_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")

        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            # Slice to [past_len, past_len + L)
            if cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L:
                cos = cos.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
                sin = sin.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            if position_ids is None:
                position_ids = (
                    torch.arange(past_len, past_len + L, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
                )
            cos = sin = None
            for call in (
                lambda: self.rotary_emb(K, seq_len=L),
                lambda: self.rotary_emb(K, position_ids),
                lambda: self.rotary_emb(position_ids),
            ):
                try:
                    cos, sin = call()
                    break
                except Exception:
                    continue
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, position_ids=position_ids)

        # MSF-FIX-15: append to attention cache
        if past_attn_k is not None:
            K = torch.cat([past_attn_k.to(K.dtype), K], dim=-2)
            V_a = torch.cat([past_attn_v.to(V_a.dtype), V_a], dim=-2)

        # Store compact (before GQA expand)
        present_attn_k = K
        present_attn_v = V_a

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V_a = repeat_kv(V_a, self.kv_repeat)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(max(1, self.head_dim))
        attn_out: torch.Tensor | None = None
        probs: torch.Tensor | None = None

        # SDPA fast path
        can_sdpa = (
            head_mask is None
            and not output_attentions
            and bool(mask_bool.all().item())  # single sync at call time
            and past_len == 0
        )
        if can_sdpa:
            try:
                _qs = Q.half() if Q.dtype == torch.float32 else Q
                _ks = K.half() if K.dtype == torch.float32 else K
                _vs = V_a.half() if V_a.dtype == torch.float32 else V_a
                _is_causal = self.is_decoder and L > 1
                _sdpa_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    _qs,
                    _ks,
                    _vs,
                    is_causal=_is_causal,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                )
                attn_out = (
                    _sdpa_out.to(dtype_in)
                    .transpose(1, 2)
                    .contiguous()
                    .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
                )
                attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0)
            except Exception:
                attn_out = None

        if attn_out is None:
            scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            if self.is_decoder and L > 1:
                kv_len = K.shape[-2]
                causal = torch.tril(
                    torch.ones(L, kv_len, device=device, dtype=torch.bool),
                    diagonal=past_len,
                )
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)

            # Padding mask: keys that are invalid get -inf
            if not mask_bool.all().item():
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask_bool[:, None, None, :], -1e4)

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype_in)
            probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)

            if head_mask is not None:
                probs = self._apply_head_mask(head_mask, probs)

            attn_out = (
                torch.matmul(probs, V_a).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            )
            attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0)

        # ---------------- 3. Per-head gating fusion ----------------
        # gamma: (B, L, num_heads)
        gamma = torch.sigmoid(self.gating(hidden_states))
        # Reshape for per-head fusion
        ssm_h = ssm_out.view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        attn_h = attn_out.view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
        g = gamma.unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, L, H, 1)
        Y = (g * ssm_h + (1 - g) * attn_h).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        Y = torch.nan_to_num(Y, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        out = Y if self.is_nested_encoder else self.out_proj(Y)

        # Build present_key_value
        present_key_value = {
            "ssm_h": (
                ssm_h_next
                if ssm_h_next is not None
                else torch.zeros(B, D, device=device, dtype=dtype_in)
            ),
            "attn_k": present_attn_k,
            "attn_v": present_attn_v,
            "seq_len": past_len + L,
        }

        attn_weights = probs if output_attentions else None
        return out, attn_weights, present_key_value

    def _apply_head_mask(self, head_mask: torch.Tensor, probs: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """MSF-FIX-11: unified head_mask broadcast. probs: (B, H, Lq, Lk)."""
        hm = head_mask.to(probs.dtype)
        H = self.num_heads
        if hm.dim() == 1:
            if hm.numel() == H:
                hm = hm.view(1, H, 1, 1)
            else:
                hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 2:
            # (B, H) or (H, 1)
            if hm.shape[-1] == H:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], H, 1, 1)
            elif hm.shape[0] == H:
                hm = hm.t().view(1, H, 1, 1)
            else:
                hm = hm.view(1, hm.shape[-1], 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 3:
            # (B, H, Lk) or (B, Lk, H)
            if hm.shape[1] == H:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(2)  # (B, H, 1, Lk)
            elif hm.shape[2] == H:
                hm = hm.permute(0, 2, 1).unsqueeze(2)
            else:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)
        # dim == 4: pass through
        return probs * hm

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        def _sz(p):
            if isinstance(p, (nn.Parameter, torch.Tensor)):
                return p.numel() * p.element_size()
            return 0

        out: dict[str, int] = {}
        for name, m in (
            ("q_proj", self.q_proj),
            ("k_proj", self.k_proj),
            ("v_proj", self.v_proj),
            ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
            ("gating", self.gating),
            ("ssm_fwd", self.ssm_fwd),
        ):
            out[name] = sum(_sz(p) for p in m.parameters())
        if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
            out["ssm_bwd"] = sum(_sz(p) for p in self.ssm_bwd.parameters())
        out["total"] = sum(out.values())
        return out

Hybrid SSM + MHA with per-chunk SVD and learnable per-head gating.

State cache layout (for streaming): past_key_value = { "ssm_h": (B, D), SSM hidden state h_{L-1} "attn_k": (B, H, L, Dh), attention keys "attn_v": (B, H, L, Dh), attention values "seq_len": int, } Or tuple form: (ssm_h, attn_k, attn_v, seq_len).

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def freeze_base_weights(self)
Expand source code 
def freeze_base_weights(self):
    # MSF-FIX-09: freeze SSM projections too
    for m in (
        self.q_proj,
        self.k_proj,
        self.v_proj,
        self.out_proj,
        self.gating,
        self.ssm_fwd.down,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_u,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_v,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_sigma,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_b,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_c,
    ):
        for p in m.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = False
    if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
        for p in self.ssm_bwd.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = False
    # Keep LoRA adapters trainable
    for m in (self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj, self.gating):
        for name, p in m.named_parameters():
            if "lora_" in name:
                p.requires_grad = True
def storage_bytes(self) ‑> dict[str, int]
Expand source code 
def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
    def _sz(p):
        if isinstance(p, (nn.Parameter, torch.Tensor)):
            return p.numel() * p.element_size()
        return 0

    out: dict[str, int] = {}
    for name, m in (
        ("q_proj", self.q_proj),
        ("k_proj", self.k_proj),
        ("v_proj", self.v_proj),
        ("out_proj", self.out_proj),
        ("gating", self.gating),
        ("ssm_fwd", self.ssm_fwd),
    ):
        out[name] = sum(_sz(p) for p in m.parameters())
    if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
        out["ssm_bwd"] = sum(_sz(p) for p in self.ssm_bwd.parameters())
    out["total"] = sum(out.values())
    return out
def unfreeze_base_weights(self)

def unfreeze_base_weights(self):
    for m in (
        self.q_proj,
        self.k_proj,
        self.v_proj,
        self.out_proj,
        self.gating,
        self.ssm_fwd.down,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_u,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_v,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_sigma,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_b,
        self.ssm_fwd.up_c,
    ):
        for p in m.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = True
    if self.ssm_bwd is not None:
        for p in self.ssm_bwd.parameters():
            p.requires_grad = True

class MinimalMoEFallback (d_model: int = 768, n_experts: int = 4)
Expand source code 
class MinimalMoEFallback(nn.Module):
    """Fallback MoE layer when REAPMoE cannot be instantiated."""

    def __init__(self, d_model: int = 768, n_experts: int = 4):
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.router = nn.Linear(d_model, n_experts, bias=False)
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList(
            [
                nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(d_model, d_model * 2),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    nn.Linear(d_model * 2, d_model),
                )
                for _ in range(n_experts)
            ]
        )
        self.aux_loss: torch.Tensor = torch.tensor(0.0)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        router_in = self.router.in_features
        if x.shape[-1] != router_in:
            x_in = (
                F.pad(x, (0, router_in - x.shape[-1]))
                if x.shape[-1] < router_in
                else x[..., :router_in]
            )
        else:
            x_in = x
        logits = self.router(x_in)
        weights = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
        out = torch.zeros_like(x_in)
        for i, exp in enumerate(self.experts):
            out = out + weights[..., i : i + 1] * exp(x_in)
        if out.shape[-1] != x.shape[-1]:
            out = (
                F.pad(out, (0, x.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
                if out.shape[-1] < x.shape[-1]
                else out[..., : x.shape[-1]]
            )
        self.aux_loss = torch.tensor(0.0, device=x.device)
        return out, weights

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    router_in = self.router.in_features
    if x.shape[-1] != router_in:
        x_in = (
            F.pad(x, (0, router_in - x.shape[-1]))
            if x.shape[-1] < router_in
            else x[..., :router_in]
        )
    else:
        x_in = x
    logits = self.router(x_in)
    weights = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
    out = torch.zeros_like(x_in)
    for i, exp in enumerate(self.experts):
        out = out + weights[..., i : i + 1] * exp(x_in)
    if out.shape[-1] != x.shape[-1]:
        out = (
            F.pad(out, (0, x.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
            if out.shape[-1] < x.shape[-1]
            else out[..., : x.shape[-1]]
        )
    self.aux_loss = torch.tensor(0.0, device=x.device)
    return out, weights

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class ModelAdapter (model_or_config: Any,
family: str | None = None)
Expand source code 
class ModelAdapter:
    """Read-only architecture contract used by module wrappers.

    Fix MF-05: `family` is normalized to a canonical internal name (e.g. "gpt2"
    becomes "gpt"), while the original name is preserved in `raw_family` for
    logging. Downstream code that inspects `adapter.family` will always see a
    canonical value; code that needs the raw value should use `raw_family`.
    """

    def __init__(self, model_or_config: Any, family: str | None = None):
        self.source = _config(model_or_config)
        raw_family = family or detect_family(model_or_config)
        # Normalize aliases to canonical internal family.
        self.raw_family = raw_family
        self.family = FAMILY_ALIASES.get(raw_family, raw_family)

        self.hidden_size = int(
            getattr(self.source, "hidden_size", getattr(self.source, "d_model", 0)) or 0
        )
        heads = getattr(
            self.source,
            "num_attention_heads",
            getattr(
                self.source,
                "num_heads",
                getattr(self.source, "num_decoder_attention_heads", 0),
            ),
        )
        self.num_heads = int(heads or 0)
        h_dim = getattr(self.source, "head_dim", None)
        self.head_dim = int(
            h_dim
            if h_dim is not None
            else (self.hidden_size // self.num_heads if self.num_heads else 0)
        )
        kv_heads = getattr(
            self.source,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            getattr(
                self.source,
                "num_key_value_heads_decoder",
                getattr(self.source, "num_decoder_attention_heads", self.num_heads or 0),
            ),
        )
        self.num_key_value_heads = int(kv_heads or self.num_heads or 0)
        if self.num_key_value_heads <= 0:
            self.num_key_value_heads = self.num_heads
        self.num_hidden_layers = int(
            getattr(self.source, "num_hidden_layers", getattr(self.source, "num_layers", 0))
        )
        self.q_proj_out = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_proj_out = self.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_key_value_heads))
        self.projection_aliases = {
            "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj"),
            "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj"),
            "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj"),
            "o": ("out_lin", "o_proj"),
        }

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Fix MF-01/MF-02/MF-03/MF-04: Consistent causal-family handling.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # A family is causal if either:
        #   (a) its canonical family is in CAUSAL_FAMILIES, OR
        #   (b) its raw family is in CAUSAL_FAMILIES (defense in depth).
        self.causal = (self.family in CAUSAL_FAMILIES) or (raw_family in CAUSAL_FAMILIES)

        # mask_rank: 4 for causal decoders, 2 for bidirectional encoders, 3 otherwise.
        if self.causal:
            self.mask_rank = 4
        elif self.family in BIDIRECTIONAL_FAMILIES:
            self.mask_rank = 2
        else:
            self.mask_rank = 3

        self.bidirectional = not self.causal

        # position_embedding: rotary for modern decoders, absolute for BERT/GPT-2,
        # relative for T5, none for unknown.
        if self.family in ROTARY_FAMILIES or raw_family in ROTARY_FAMILIES:
            self.position_embedding = "rotary"
        elif (
            self.family in GPT_ABSOLUTE_POSITION_FAMILIES
            or raw_family in GPT_ABSOLUTE_POSITION_FAMILIES
            or self.family in {"bert", "roberta"}
        ):
            self.position_embedding = "absolute"
        elif self.family == "t5":
            self.position_embedding = "relative"
        else:
            self.position_embedding = "none"

        self.supports_gqa = (self.num_key_value_heads < self.num_heads) or self.family == "qwen3_5"
        self.supports_delta_net = self.family == "qwen3_5"
        self.supports_moe = self.family == "qwen3_5"

    def projection(self, module: Any, kind: str) -> Any:
        legacy, native = self.projection_aliases[kind]
        return getattr(module, native, getattr(module, legacy, None))

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "family": self.family,
            "raw_family": self.raw_family,
            "hidden_size": self.hidden_size,
            "num_heads": self.num_heads,
            "num_key_value_heads": self.num_key_value_heads,
            "q_proj_out": self.q_proj_out,
            "kv_proj_out": self.kv_proj_out,
            "kv_repeat": self.kv_repeat,
            "head_dim": self.head_dim,
            "num_hidden_layers": self.num_hidden_layers,
            "causal": self.causal,
            "mask_rank": self.mask_rank,
            "position_embedding": self.position_embedding,
            "supports_gqa": self.supports_gqa,
            "supports_delta_net": self.supports_delta_net,
            "supports_moe": self.supports_moe,
        }

Read-only architecture contract used by module wrappers.

Fix MF-05: family is normalized to a canonical internal name (e.g. "gpt2" becomes "gpt"), while the original name is preserved in raw_family for logging. Downstream code that inspects adapter.family will always see a canonical value; code that needs the raw value should use raw_family.

class NEXUSAttention (config: Any | None = None,
model_config: Any | None = None,
nexus_cfg: NEXUSConfig | None = None,
layer_idx: int = 0,
total_layers: int = 6,
pretrained_attn: torch.nn.modules.module.Module | None = None,
bert_mode: bool = False,
lora_rank: int = 16,
lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
is_decoder: bool | None = None,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("nexus", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class NEXUSAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        model_config: Any | None = None,
        nexus_cfg: NEXUSConfig | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        total_layers: int = 6,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        bert_mode: bool = False,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        if model_config is None:
            model_config = config
        super().__init__(model_config)
        self.num_heads: int = getattr(
            model_config,
            "num_attention_heads",
            getattr(model_config, "num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 4)),
        )
        self.hidden_size: int = getattr(model_config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        cfg_head_dim = getattr(model_config, "head_dim", None) if model_config else None
        adapter_head_dim = (
            getattr(getattr(model_config, "adapter", None), "head_dim", None)
            if model_config
            else None
        )
        self.head_dim: int = int(
            cfg_head_dim
            or adapter_head_dim
            or kwargs.get("head_dim")
            or (self.hidden_size // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64)
        )
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim

        cfg_kv = (
            getattr(
                model_config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                getattr(model_config, "num_kv_heads", None),
            )
            if model_config
            else None
        )
        if cfg_kv is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv)
        elif kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(kwargs["num_kv_heads"])
        else:
            self.num_kv_heads = self.num_heads

        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat: int = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        self.cfg: NEXUSConfig = nexus_cfg if nexus_cfg is not None else NEXUSConfig()
        self.layer_idx: int = layer_idx
        self.bert_mode: bool = bert_mode
        self.lora_rank: int = lora_rank
        self.lora_alpha: float = lora_alpha

        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            _mt = str(getattr(model_config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if model_config else ""
            self.is_decoder = (
                bool(
                    getattr(model_config, "is_decoder", False)
                    or getattr(model_config, "causal", False)
                )
                or any(
                    _mt.startswith(p)
                    for p in (
                        "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "falcon",
                        "bloom", "opt", "gpt_neo", "gpt2",
                    )
                )
                if model_config
                else False
            )

        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        src_p = next(pretrained_attn.parameters(), None) if pretrained_attn is not None else None
        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype", src_p.dtype if src_p is not None else torch.float32)
        target_device = kwargs.get("device", src_p.device if src_p is not None else None)

        self.query = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.key = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size, self.kv_head_size, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.value = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size, self.kv_head_size, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size, self.hidden_size, bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(self.cfg.dropout)

        self.dss_attn = DualStageStripeAttention(
            self.cfg, self.head_dim, num_heads=self.num_heads,
            is_decoder=self.is_decoder, is_nested_encoder=self.is_nested_encoder,
        )

        self.seer_gate: SeerGating | None = None
        if self.cfg.seer_enabled:
            self.seer_gate = SeerGating(
                self.head_dim, self.num_heads,
                self.cfg.seer_gate_rank, self.cfg.seer_top_k_blocks,
                self.cfg.seer_temperature,
            )

        self.rect_interval = self.cfg.rectify_interval
        self.is_rectify = (
            (self.rect_interval > 0)
            and (layer_idx % self.rect_interval == 0)
            and ((pretrained_attn is None) or getattr(self.cfg, "enable_spectraformer", False))
        )
        self.spec_rectify: SpectraformerRectify | None = None
        if self.is_rectify:
            self.spec_rectify = SpectraformerRectify(self.head_dim, self.cfg.rff_features)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

        if target_device is not None and target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(device=target_device, dtype=target_dtype)
        elif target_device is not None:
            self.to(device=target_device)
        elif target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(target_dtype)

    @property
    def q_proj(self): return self.query
    @property
    def k_proj(self): return self.key
    @property
    def v_proj(self): return self.value
    @property
    def q_lin(self): return self.query
    @property
    def k_lin(self): return self.key
    @property
    def v_lin(self): return self.value
    @property
    def out_lin(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def dense(self): return self.out_proj
    @property
    def o_proj(self): return self.out_proj

    def _reshape(self, x: torch.Tensor, n_heads: int | None = None) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, N, _ = x.shape
        heads = n_heads if n_heads is not None else self.num_heads
        return x.view(B, N, heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

    def _get_token_valid_mask(self, mask, B, N, device):
        if mask is None:
            return None
        if mask.dim() == 2:
            if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                return mask.to(device)
            if mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return (mask > -1e4).to(device)
            return (mask > 0).to(device)
        if mask.dim() == 3:
            if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = mask
            elif mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = mask > -1e4
            else:
                v = mask > 0
            return v.any(dim=1).to(device)
        if mask.dim() == 4:
            if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = mask
            elif mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = mask > -1e4
            else:
                v = mask > 0
            return v.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1).to(device)
        return torch.ones(B, N, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # CHUNKED CAUSAL NEXUS — NEW
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _chunked_causal_nexus(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,           # (B, H, N, D)
        K: torch.Tensor,
        V: torch.Tensor,
        token_valid: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        chunk_size: int = 256,
        num_landmarks: int = 32,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Chunked Causal NEXUS with Nyström-style inter-chunk summary.

        Algorithm:
          For each chunk c = [start, end):
            1. Intra-chunk: XAttention block-sparse causal attention + softpick.
            2. Inter-chunk (c > 0): Nyström with landmarks pooled from ALL
               previous chunks.
               F1 = softmax(Q_c @ K_landmarks^T)        (B, H, C, m)
               F2 = softmax(Q_landmarks @ K_landmarks^T) (B, H, m, m)
               F3 = softmax(Q_landmarks @ K_prev^T)      (B, H, m, start)
               out_cross = F1 @ pinv(F2) @ F3 @ V_prev
            3. Combine intra + cross.

        Args:
            Q, K, V: (B, H, N, D)
            token_valid: (B, N) boolean mask or None
            chunk_size: int, size of each causal chunk
            num_landmarks: int, landmarks per chunk
        Returns:
            context: (B, H, N, D)
        """
        original_dtype = Q.dtype
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        device = Q.device

        # Force FP32 for numerical stability (pinv backward compat)
        Q = Q.float()
        K = K.float()
        V = V.float()

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(D)
        m = min(num_landmarks, N)

        # If sequence fits in one chunk, do intra-chunk only
        if N <= chunk_size:
            scores = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            causal = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)
            if token_valid is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(
                    (~token_valid.bool()).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4
                )
            if self.cfg.softpick_enabled:
                attn = softpick(scores, dim=-1, temperature=self.cfg.softpick_temperature)
            else:
                scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            context = torch.matmul(attn, V).to(original_dtype)
            return self.dropout(context)

        num_chunks = (N + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size
        outputs: list[torch.Tensor] = []

        for c_idx in range(num_chunks):
            start = c_idx * chunk_size
            end = min(start + chunk_size, N)
            C_len = end - start

            Q_c = Q[:, :, start:end, :]
            K_c = K[:, :, start:end, :]
            V_c = V[:, :, start:end, :]
            mask_c = token_valid[:, start:end] if token_valid is not None else None

            # ---- 1. Intra-chunk causal attention (dense within chunk) ----
            scores_intra = torch.matmul(Q_c, K_c.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            causal_intra = torch.tril(
                torch.ones(C_len, C_len, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)
            )
            scores_intra = scores_intra.masked_fill(
                ~causal_intra.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4
            )
            if mask_c is not None:
                scores_intra = scores_intra.masked_fill(
                    (~mask_c.bool()).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4
                )

            if self.cfg.softpick_enabled:
                attn_intra = softpick(
                    scores_intra, dim=-1, temperature=self.cfg.softpick_temperature
                )
            else:
                scores_intra = torch.nan_to_num(
                    scores_intra, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4
                )
                attn_intra = F.softmax(scores_intra, dim=-1)

            out_intra = torch.matmul(attn_intra, V_c)

            # ---- First chunk: no inter-chunk ----
            if c_idx == 0:
                outputs.append(out_intra)
                continue

            # ---- 2. Inter-chunk Nyström ----
            K_prev = K[:, :, :start, :]
            V_prev = V[:, :, :start, :]
            mask_prev = token_valid[:, :start] if token_valid is not None else None
            prev_len = start

            m_prev = min(m, prev_len)

            # Pool landmarks
            def _pool(X, n_landmarks):
                Bx, Hx, Nx, Dx = X.shape
                if n_landmarks >= Nx:
                    return X
                X_flat = X.reshape(Bx * Hx, Nx, Dx).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
                pooled = F.adaptive_avg_pool1d(X_flat, n_landmarks)
                return pooled.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().reshape(
                    Bx, Hx, n_landmarks, Dx
                )

            K_landmarks = _pool(K_prev, m_prev)
            Q_landmarks = _pool(Q_c, min(m, C_len))

            # F1: (B, H, C, m_prev)
            scores1 = torch.matmul(
                Q_c, K_landmarks.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            ) * scale
            F1 = F.softmax(scores1, dim=-1)

            # F2: (B, H, m_c, m_prev)
            scores2 = torch.matmul(
                Q_landmarks, K_landmarks.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            ) * scale
            F2 = F.softmax(scores2, dim=-1)

            # F3: (B, H, m_c, prev_len)
            scores3 = torch.matmul(
                Q_landmarks, K_prev.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()
            ) * scale
            if mask_prev is not None:
                scores3 = scores3.masked_fill(
                    (~mask_prev.bool()).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4
                )
            F3 = F.softmax(scores3, dim=-1)

            # Stable pseudoinverse via Newton-Schulz
            try:
                F2_pinv = torch.linalg.pinv(F2)
            except Exception:
                # Fallback: Tikhonov-regularized inverse
                Bx, Hx, mx, mx2 = F2.shape
                F2_reg = F2 + 1e-3 * torch.eye(mx, device=device, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
                F2_pinv = torch.linalg.inv(F2_reg)

            # Cross-chunk output
            step1 = torch.matmul(F3, V_prev)          # (B, H, m_c, D)
            step2 = torch.matmul(F2_pinv, step1)      # (B, H, m_prev, D)
            out_cross = torch.matmul(F1, step2)       # (B, H, C, D)
            out_cross = torch.nan_to_num(out_cross, nan=0.0)

            # Combine
            out_c = out_intra + out_cross
            outputs.append(out_c)

        context = torch.cat(outputs, dim=2)
        context = self.dropout(context)
        return context.to(original_dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights (unchanged from original)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None), "k": (None, None), "v": (None, None), "o": (None, None),
        }

        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            weight = src.c_attn.weight.data
            bias = getattr(src.c_attn, "bias", None)
            bias_data = bias.data if bias is not None else None
            if weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[1] == 3 * self.hidden_size:
                weight = weight.t().contiguous()
            split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
            q_w = weight[:split_size, :]
            k_w = weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :]
            v_w = weight[2 * split_size :, :]
            q_b = bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
            k_b = bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
            v_b = bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None
            extracted["q"] = (q_w, q_b)
            extracted["k"] = (k_w, k_b)
            extracted["v"] = (v_w, v_b)

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            w_o = src.c_proj.weight.data
            if w_o.dim() == 2 and w_o.shape[0] != self.hidden_size and w_o.shape[1] == self.hidden_size:
                w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
            b_o = getattr(src.c_proj, "bias", None)
            extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o.data if b_o is not None else None)

        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query"):
                extracted["q"] = (s.query.weight.data, getattr(s.query, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "key"):
                extracted["k"] = (s.key.weight.data, getattr(s.key, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "value"):
                extracted["v"] = (s.value.weight.data, getattr(s.value, "bias", None))

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (dense.weight.data, getattr(dense, "bias", None))

        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (layer.weight.data, getattr(layer, "bias", None))
                            break

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, attr)
            for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "c_proj", "out_lin", "o_proj", "wo", "o", "proj")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"))
            or (
                cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention", "XLMRobertaSelfAttention")
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
            )
        ) and not has_out_proj:
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)
        if hasattr(self, "dss_attn"):
            self.dss_attn.is_nested_encoder = self.is_nested_encoder

        def _orient(w, in_dim):
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in [
                ("q", self.query), ("k", self.key), ("v", self.value), ("o", self.out_proj),
            ]:
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.hidden_size
                    w_oriented = _orient(w, in_dim)
                    target_w = target.weight
                    out_d, in_d = target_w.shape
                    src_out, src_in = w_oriented.shape

                    if proj_key in ("k", "v"):
                        if src_out == out_d:
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented)
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b)
                        elif src_out > out_d and src_out % out_d == 0:
                            rep = src_out // out_d
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented.view(out_d, rep, -1).mean(dim=1))
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b.view(out_d, rep).mean(dim=1))
                        elif src_out < out_d and out_d % src_out == 0:
                            rep = out_d // src_out
                            target_w.data.copy_(w_oriented.repeat(rep, 1))
                            if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                                target.bias.data.copy_(b.repeat(rep))
                        else:
                            target_w.data.zero_()
                            target_w.data[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].copy_(
                                w_oriented[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)]
                            )
                    else:
                        target_w.data.zero_()
                        target_w.data[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)].copy_(
                            w_oriented[: min(out_d, src_out), : min(in_d, src_in)]
                        )
                        if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                            b_val = b.data if isinstance(b, nn.Parameter) else b
                            target.bias.data.zero_()
                            target.bias.data[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                                b_val[: min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))]
                            )
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.weight.shape[0], target.weight.shape[1])
                    target.weight.data.zero_()
                    target.weight.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

                target.freeze_base_weights()

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)
            if hasattr(self, "dss_attn"):
                self.dss_attn.is_decoder = self.is_decoder

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        N_kv = kv_input.shape[1]

        Q = self._reshape(self.query(hidden_states), self.num_heads)
        K = self._reshape(self.key(kv_input), self.num_kv_heads)
        V = self._reshape(self.value(kv_input), self.num_kv_heads)

        # RoPE with past_len offset
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    past_len = past_key_value.key_cache[0].shape[-2]
            cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + N, :]
            sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + N, :]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        if self.num_kv_heads < self.num_heads:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        if eff_mask is None:
            eff_mask = kwargs.get("attn_mask")
        token_valid = self._get_token_valid_mask(eff_mask, B, N_kv, device)

        is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder and not is_cross)

        # ============================================================
        # PATH 1: CAUSAL (decoder) → Chunked Causal NEXUS
        # ============================================================
        if is_causal and not is_cross:
            context = self._chunked_causal_nexus(
                Q, K, V, token_valid,
                chunk_size=self.cfg.causal_chunk_size,
                num_landmarks=self.cfg.causal_landmarks,
            )
            if token_valid is not None:
                context = context * token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(context.dtype)

        # ============================================================
        # PATH 2: CROSS-ATTENTION or SHORT SEQUENCE → dense
        # ============================================================
        elif is_cross or (self.cfg.sparse_min_seq_len >= N) or (self.dss_attn is None):
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(D)
            scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            if eff_mask is not None:
                if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                    scores = scores + (eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2).float() - 1.0) * 10000.0
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                    scores = scores + eff_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                    scores = scores + eff_mask.float()

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
            attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    head_mask = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
                attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask

            context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)

        # ============================================================
        # PATH 3: BIDIRECTIONAL (encoder) → sparse NEXUS
        # ============================================================
        else:
            if self.is_rectify and self.spec_rectify is not None:
                context = self.spec_rectify(Q, K, V)
            else:
                seer_mask = None
                if self.seer_gate is not None:
                    bs = self.cfg.block_size
                    nb = (N + bs - 1) // bs
                    seer_mask = self.seer_gate(Q, K, nb, training=self.training)

                context = self.dss_attn(Q, K, V, token_valid, seer_mask=seer_mask)

            if token_valid is not None:
                context = context * token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(context.dtype)

        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, H * D)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(self.dropout(context))

        return (output, None)

    def get_ortho_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=self.query.weight.device)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.
