Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("orion", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class ORIONAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        model_config: Any | None = None,
        orion_cfg: ORIONConfig | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: Any | None = None,
        old_attn: Any | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        bert_mode: bool = False,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        cfg = config or model_config
        source_attn = (
            pretrained_attn
            if pretrained_attn is not None
            else (old_attn if old_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn"))
        )
        try:
            super().__init__(cfg, rank)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(cfg)

        if orion_cfg is None:
            orion_cfg = ORIONConfig()
            if rank is not None:
                orion_cfg.svd_max_rank = max(rank, orion_cfg.svd_min_rank)

        hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size")
        num_heads = kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_heads"))
        num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", kwargs.get("num_kv_heads"))

        if hidden_size is None or num_heads is None:
            if cfg is not None:
                if hidden_size is None:
                    hidden_size = _cfg_get(
                        cfg,
                        "hidden_size",
                        _cfg_get(cfg, "d_model", _cfg_get(cfg, "dim", 768)),
                    )
                if num_heads is None:
                    num_heads = _cfg_get(cfg, "num_attention_heads", _cfg_get(cfg, "num_heads", 12))
                if num_kv_heads is None:
                    num_kv_heads = _cfg_get(
                        cfg,
                        "num_key_value_heads",
                        _cfg_get(cfg, "num_kv_heads", num_heads),
                    )
            elif source_attn is not None:
                if hidden_size is None:
                    out_layer = getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "out_lin",
                        getattr(
                            source_attn,
                            "dense",
                            getattr(
                                source_attn,
                                "c_proj",
                                getattr(source_attn, "o_proj", None),
                            ),
                        ),
                    )
                    hidden_size = (
                        getattr(out_layer, "out_features", 768) if out_layer is not None else 768
                    )
                if num_heads is None:
                    num_heads = getattr(
                        source_attn,
                        "num_attention_heads",
                        getattr(source_attn, "n_heads", 12),
                    )
                if num_kv_heads is None:
                    num_kv_heads = getattr(source_attn, "num_key_value_heads", num_heads)
            else:
                hidden_size = 768 if hidden_size is None else hidden_size
                num_heads = 12 if num_heads is None else num_heads
                num_kv_heads = num_heads if num_kv_heads is None else num_kv_heads

        if num_kv_heads is None:
            num_kv_heads = num_heads

        self.num_heads = max(1, int(num_heads))
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, int(num_kv_heads))
        self.hidden_size = int(hidden_size)
        if self.hidden_size % self.num_heads != 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"hidden_size {self.hidden_size} must be divisible by num_heads {self.num_heads}"
            )
        self.head_dim = self.hidden_size // self.num_heads
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True
        self.cfg = orion_cfg
        self.bert_mode = bert_mode

        # Allocate kernel vs full heads
        if self.num_heads <= 1:
            self.num_kernel_heads = 1
            self.num_full_heads = 0
        else:
            self.num_full_heads = max(1, self.num_heads // 3)
            self.num_kernel_heads = self.num_heads - self.num_full_heads

        # Decoder detection
        model_type = str(getattr(cfg, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if cfg else ""
        is_dec = (
            bool(
                getattr(
                    cfg,
                    "is_decoder",
                    getattr(cfg, "is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", False)),
                )
            )
            if cfg
            else False
        )
        if (
            any(
                model_type.startswith(p)
                for p in ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "falcon", "bloom", "opt")
            )
            or hasattr(cfg, "architectures")
            and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(cfg, "architectures", []) or [])
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # LoRA config
        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                cfg,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if cfg
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                cfg,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if cfg
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and source_attn is not None:
            p = next(source_attn.parameters(), None)
            if p is not None:
                target_dtype = p.dtype

        # Projections
        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            energy=orion_cfg.svd_energy,
            min_rank=orion_cfg.svd_min_rank,
            max_rank=orion_cfg.svd_max_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            hidden_size,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            energy=orion_cfg.svd_energy,
            min_rank=orion_cfg.svd_min_rank,
            max_rank=orion_cfg.svd_max_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            hidden_size,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            energy=orion_cfg.svd_energy,
            min_rank=orion_cfg.svd_min_rank,
            max_rank=orion_cfg.svd_max_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            energy=orion_cfg.svd_energy,
            min_rank=orion_cfg.svd_min_rank,
            max_rank=orion_cfg.svd_max_rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        rff_init = torch.randn(
            max(1, self.num_kernel_heads), self.head_dim, orion_cfg.rff_features
        ) * (1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim))
        self.register_buffer("rff_proj", rff_init, persistent=False)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(orion_cfg.dropout)

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

        if target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(target_dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def query(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def dense(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def out_lin(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self) -> FactorisedLinear:
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # Flat modules (DistilBERT, Qwen, etc.)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                "ORIONAttention.copy_weights: missing projections %s from %s",
                missing_after,
                src.__class__.__name__,
            )
            return

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        # Identity for nested encoders
                        target_mod.down.weight.data.zero_()
                        target_mod.up.weight.data.zero_()
                        r = min(target_mod.rank, self.hidden_size, self.all_head_size)
                        target_mod.down.weight.data[:r, :r].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                        target_mod.up.weight.data[:r, :r].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                        if target_mod.up.bias is not None:
                            target_mod.up.bias.data.zero_()
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

                # Orientation
                expected = (target_mod.out_features, target_mod.in_features)
                if w.shape != expected and w.t().contiguous().shape == expected:
                    w = w.t().contiguous()

                target_mod.decompose_from_weight(
                    w[: target_mod.out_features, : target_mod.in_features],
                    b[: target_mod.out_features] if b is not None else None,
                )

                norm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "ORIONAttention: %s has near-zero Frobenius norm (%.2e)",
                        proj_key,
                        norm,
                    )

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # OR-FIX-03: _kernel_linear_attention with token_valid
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _kernel_linear_attention(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,
        K: torch.Tensor,
        V: torch.Tensor,
        token_valid: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        FAVOR+ random feature linear attention.

        Implements the unbiased feature map from Choromanski et al. (2020):
            φ(x) = exp(ω·x - ||x||²/2) / sqrt(m),   ω ~ N(0, I_d)

        The `-||x||²/2` term is essential for an unbiased estimate of exp(q·k).
        The scaling 1/sqrt(d) is applied to x once (not to ω, not twice).
        """
        B, Hk, N, D = Q.shape
        if Hk == 0:
            return torch.zeros(B, 0, N, D, device=Q.device, dtype=Q.dtype)

        F_dim = self.cfg.rff_features
        orig_dtype = Q.dtype
        Q, K, V = Q.float(), K.float(), V.float()
        proj = self.rff_proj[:Hk].to(Q.device).float()  # (Hk, D, m); ω ~ N(0, I_d)

        # Scale x ONCE by 1/sqrt(d). This is the "x" entering the FAVOR+ formula.
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        Q_scaled = Q * scale
        K_scaled = K * scale

        # Project onto random features:  (B, Hk, N, m)
        Q_proj = torch.einsum("bhnd,hdf->bhnf", Q_scaled, proj)
        K_proj = torch.einsum("bhnd,hdf->bhnf", K_scaled, proj)

        # Subtract ||x||²/2 (per query/key) → unbiased FAVOR+ feature map
        Q_norm_sq = (Q_scaled**2).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) / 2.0  # (B, Hk, N, 1)
        K_norm_sq = (K_scaled**2).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) / 2.0  # (B, Hk, N, 1)

        # Numerical safety: subtract global max before exp (log-sum-exp style)
        # Mathematically cancels out in num/denom, so accuracy is unchanged.
        max_val = max(float(Q_proj.detach().amax().item()), float(K_proj.detach().amax().item()))
        max_val = max(max_val, 0.0)  # only subtract if positive

        Q_feat = torch.exp(Q_proj - Q_norm_sq - max_val) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
        K_feat = torch.exp(K_proj - K_norm_sq - max_val) / math.sqrt(F_dim)

        # Padding mask on K and V
        if token_valid is not None:
            mask_kv = token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).to(K_feat.dtype)
            K_feat = K_feat * mask_kv
            V = V * mask_kv

        M = Q_feat.shape[-1]
        if self.is_decoder:
            chunk_size = min(64, N)
            num_chunks = (N + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size
            S = torch.zeros(B, Hk, M, D, dtype=torch.float32, device=Q.device)
            Z = torch.zeros(B, Hk, M, dtype=torch.float32, device=Q.device)
            ctx_list = []

            for c in range(num_chunks):
                start = c * chunk_size
                end = min(start + chunk_size, N)
                L = end - start

                q_c = Q_feat[:, :, start:end]
                k_c = K_feat[:, :, start:end]
                v_c = V[:, :, start:end]

                causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, L, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.float32))
                intra_attn = torch.matmul(q_c, k_c.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()) * causal_mask
                intra_out = torch.matmul(intra_attn, v_c)
                inter_out = torch.matmul(q_c, S)

                intra_den = intra_attn.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                inter_den = torch.matmul(q_c, Z.unsqueeze(-1))

                den = (intra_den + inter_den).clamp(min=1e-4)
                ctx_list.append((intra_out + inter_out) / den)

                S = S + torch.matmul(k_c.transpose(-1, -2).contiguous(), v_c)
                Z = Z + k_c.sum(dim=2)

            ctx = torch.cat(ctx_list, dim=2)
        else:
            KV = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhnd->bhfd", K_feat, V)
            K_sum = K_feat.sum(dim=2)
            denom = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhf->bhn", Q_feat, K_sum).unsqueeze(-1).clamp(min=1e-4)
            ctx = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhfd->bhnd", Q_feat, KV) / denom
        return ctx.to(orig_dtype)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # OR-FIX-02: _full_softpick_attention rewritten
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _full_softpick_attention(
        self,
        Q: torch.Tensor,
        K: torch.Tensor,
        V: torch.Tensor,
        token_valid: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Full softmax attention for the "full" heads.

        Returns a tensor of shape (B, Hf, N, D). No internal out_proj is
        applied here; the caller handles out_proj after concatenating all
        head groups.
        """
        B, Hf, N, D = Q.shape
        if Hf == 0:
            return torch.zeros(B, 0, N, D, device=Q.device, dtype=Q.dtype)

        scale = self.head_dim**-0.5
        is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder) and N > 1

        # Build additive attention bias for padding
        attn_bias = None
        if token_valid is not None:
            valid_bias = ((1.0 - token_valid.float()) * -1e4).view(B, 1, 1, N)
            attn_bias = valid_bias.to(Q.dtype)

        # Try SDPA
        try:
            q_sdp = Q.half() if Q.dtype == torch.float32 else Q
            k_sdp = K.half() if K.dtype == torch.float32 else K
            v_sdp = V.half() if V.dtype == torch.float32 else V
            bias_sdp = None
            if attn_bias is not None:
                bias_sdp = attn_bias.to(q_sdp.dtype)

            out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                q_sdp,
                k_sdp,
                v_sdp,
                attn_mask=bias_sdp,
                dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                is_causal=is_causal and bias_sdp is None,
            )
            out = out.to(Q.dtype)
            return torch.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Fallback: manual temperature softmax
        scores = torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        if is_causal:
            causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=Q.device, dtype=torch.bool))
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)

        if token_valid is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2).bool(), -1e4)

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn_w = temperature_softmax(scores, dim=-1, temperature=self.cfg.softpick_temperature).to(
            V.dtype
        )
        attn_w = self.dropout(attn_w)
        return torch.matmul(attn_w, V)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if hidden_states.dim() == 4:
            B, H, L, Dh = hidden_states.shape
            hidden_states = hidden_states.view(B, L, H * Dh)
        elif not hidden_states.is_contiguous():
            hidden_states = hidden_states.contiguous()

        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        Hk = self.num_kernel_heads
        Hf = self.num_full_heads
        device = hidden_states.device

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        N_kv = kv_input.shape[1]

        Q_all = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        K_all = (
            self.k_proj(kv_input)
            .view(B, N_kv, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        V_all = (
            self.v_proj(kv_input)
            .view(B, N_kv, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # RoPE
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            Q_all, K_all = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q_all, K_all, cos, sin)

        # GQA expansion
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K_all = repeat_kv(K_all, self.kv_repeat)
            V_all = repeat_kv(V_all, self.kv_repeat)

        # Token valid mask
        token_valid = None
        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        if eff_mask is not None:
            token_valid = get_token_valid_mask(eff_mask, B, N_kv, device)

        ctx_list = []

        # OR-FIX-04: pass token_valid to both branches
        if Hk > 0:
            Q_kernel = Q_all[:, :Hk]
            K_kernel = K_all[:, :Hk]
            V_kernel = V_all[:, :Hk]
            ctx_kernel = self._kernel_linear_attention(Q_kernel, K_kernel, V_kernel, token_valid)
            ctx_list.append(ctx_kernel)

        if Hf > 0:
            Q_full = Q_all[:, Hk:]
            K_full = K_all[:, Hk:]
            V_full = V_all[:, Hk:]
            ctx_full = self._full_softpick_attention(Q_full, K_full, V_full, token_valid)
            ctx_list.append(ctx_full)

        if len(ctx_list) == 1:
            context = ctx_list[0]
        else:
            context = torch.cat(ctx_list, dim=1)

        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # head_mask
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                hm = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), -1, 1, 1)
            else:
                hm = head_mask
            context = context * hm.to(context.dtype)

        # Reshape to (B, N, H*D)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        # Output projection (skipped for nested encoders)
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)
        output = torch.nan_to_num(output, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        return output, None

    def get_ortho_loss(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.zeros((), device=self.out_proj.up.weight.device)

class PHANTOMAttention (config: Any | None = None,

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("phantom")
class PHANTOMAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 16,
        phantom_cfg: PHANTOMConfig | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: Any | None = None,
        bert_mode: bool = False,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank or 16)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)

        cur_rank = rank or getattr(
            comp_config,
            "cur_rank",
            getattr(config, "cur_rank", getattr(config, "svd_rank", 16)),
        )
        if phantom_cfg is None:
            phantom_cfg = PHANTOMConfig(cur_rank=cur_rank)
        elif rank is not None:
            phantom_cfg.cur_rank = rank
        self.cfg: PHANTOMConfig = phantom_cfg

        model_config = config or kwargs.get("model_config")
        self.hidden_size: int = getattr(model_config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        self.dim: int = self.hidden_size
        self.num_heads: int = getattr(
            model_config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads: int = getattr(
            model_config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
        )
        self.head_dim: int = getattr(
            model_config,
            "head_dim",
            kwargs.get("head_dim", self.dim // self.num_heads),
        )
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa: bool = True

        is_dec = getattr(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False) or getattr(config, "causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        
        # Robust detection like in NASC
        m_type = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "")).lower() if config else ""
        if any(m_type.startswith(p) for p in ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi", "opt")):
            is_dec = True

        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and source_attn is not None:
            target_dtype = next(source_attn.parameters(), torch.tensor([])).dtype
            if not isinstance(target_dtype, torch.dtype):
                target_dtype = torch.float32

        # Projections
        self.q_proj = FactorisedLinear_CUR(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            cur_rank=self.cfg.cur_rank,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.k_proj = FactorisedLinear_CUR(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            cur_rank=self.cfg.cur_rank,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.v_proj = FactorisedLinear_CUR(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            cur_rank=self.cfg.cur_rank,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.out_proj = FactorisedLinear_CUR(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            cur_rank=self.cfg.cur_rank,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        self.query = self.q_proj
        self.key = self.k_proj
        self.value = self.v_proj

        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(self.cfg.dropout)

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

        if target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(target_dtype)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                f"PHANTOMAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )
            return

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        target_mod.copy_cur(torch.eye(self.all_head_size, self.dim), None)
                        continue
                    else:
                        logger.warning(f"Missing required weights for {proj_key}")
                        return

                if w.shape != (target_mod.out_features, target_mod.in_features):
                    if w.t().contiguous().shape == (
                        target_mod.out_features,
                        target_mod.in_features,
                    ):
                        w = w.t().contiguous()
                    elif (
                        w.shape[0] < target_mod.out_features
                        and target_mod.out_features % w.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.out_features // w.shape[0]
                        w = w.repeat(repeats, 1)
                    else:
                        full_w = torch.zeros(
                            (target_mod.out_features, target_mod.in_features),
                            dtype=w.dtype,
                            device=w.device,
                        )
                        min_0 = min(target_mod.out_features, w.shape[0])
                        min_1 = min(target_mod.in_features, w.shape[1])
                        full_w[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])
                        w = full_w

                target_mod.copy_cur(w, b)

                fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(f"Weight copy notice for {proj_key}: norm {fnorm:.6f} < 1e-4")

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, N, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, D = self.num_heads, self.head_dim

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, H, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        K = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        V = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        # Apply RoPE if provided
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin)

        # Repeat KV for GQA
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            K = repeat_kv(K, repeats)
            V = repeat_kv(V, repeats)

        token_valid = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N, hidden_states.device)
        is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)

        context = lsh_attention(
            Q,
            K,
            V,
            rounds=self.cfg.lsh_rounds,
            bucket_size=self.cfg.lsh_bucket_size,
            softpick_temp=(self.cfg.softpick_temperature if self.cfg.softpick_enabled else 1.0),
            dropout=self.dropout if self.cfg.softpick_enabled else None,
            token_valid=token_valid,
            is_causal=is_causal,
        )

        # head_mask handling
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                context = context * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(context.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                context = context * head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(
                    context.dtype
                )
            elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                context = context * head_mask.to(context.dtype)

        context = context.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, N, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return context, None

        output = self.out_proj(context)
        return output, None

class RMSNorm (dim: int, eps: float = 1e-06)
Expand source code 
class RMSNorm(nn.Module):
    """RMSNorm for stabilizing Differential Attention Outputs."""

    def __init__(self, dim: int, eps: float = 1e-6):
        super().__init__()
        self.eps = eps
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(dim))

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        norm = torch.rsqrt(x.pow(2).mean(-1, keepdim=True) + self.eps)
        return x * norm * self.weight

RMSNorm for stabilizing Differential Attention Outputs.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

Methods

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    norm = torch.rsqrt(x.pow(2).mean(-1, keepdim=True) + self.eps)
    return x * norm * self.weight

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class SAMBAM3Block (config: Any | None = None,

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("samba_m3", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class SAMBAM3Block(nn.Module):
    r"""SAMBA-M3: Local Sliding-Window Attention + Spectral Mamba."""

    supports_gqa: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        n_heads: int | None = None,
        window: int = 128,
        rank: int | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if isinstance(config, int):
            d_model = config
            config = None

        # ---- d_model / n_heads ----
        if d_model is None:
            if config is not None:
                d_model = _cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", _cfg_get(config, "d_model", 768))
            elif pretrained_attn is not None:
                for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"):
                    m = getattr(pretrained_attn, attr, None)
                    if m is not None and hasattr(m, "in_features"):
                        d_model = m.in_features
                        break
                if d_model is None:
                    d_model = getattr(pretrained_attn, "hidden_size", 768)
            else:
                d_model = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)

        if n_heads is None:
            n_heads = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(config, "n_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)),
            )

        window = int(_cfg_get(config, "window_size", window))

        self.d_model: int = int(d_model)
        self.n_heads: int = int(n_heads)

        if self.n_heads <= 0 or self.d_model % self.n_heads != 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"d_model ({self.d_model}) must be divisible by n_heads ({self.n_heads})"
            )

        self.head_dim: int = self.d_model // self.n_heads
        self.window: int = window
        self.lora_rank: int = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", lora_rank)))
        self.lora_alpha: float = float(
            kwargs.get("lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))
        )

        cfg_kv = _cfg_get(config, "num_key_value_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        self.num_kv_heads: int = int(cfg_kv) if cfg_kv is not None else self.n_heads
        self.num_key_value_groups: int = max(1, self.n_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        self.q_head_size: int = self.n_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # ---- SM3-FIX-08: decoder detection (override wins) ----
        if is_decoder is not None:
            is_dec = bool(is_decoder)
        else:
            model_type = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "")).lower()
            is_dec = bool(
                _cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", False)
                or _cfg_get(config, "is_causal", False)
                or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)
            )
            decoder_prefixes = (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
            if any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in decoder_prefixes):
                is_dec = True
            arch = getattr(config, "architectures", None) if config is not None else None
            if arch and any("GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a) for a in arch):
                is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal: bool = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        # ---- Projections ----
        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.d_model,
            self.q_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.d_model,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.d_model,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.q_head_size,
            self.d_model,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        # ---- Mamba branch ----
        self.mamba = SpectralMamba(
            self.d_model, config=config, is_decoder=self.is_decoder, **kwargs
        )

        # ---- SM3-FIX-04: alpha always fp32, cast in forward ----
        self.alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0, dtype=torch.float32))
        self.gate_fn = nn.Sigmoid()

        dropout_p = float(
            _cfg_get(config, "dropout", _cfg_get(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    # ---- aliases ----
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def q_proj(self):
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def k_proj(self):
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def v_proj(self):
        return self.v_lin

    def get_ortho_loss(self) -> float:
        return 0.0

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        for m in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
            m.freeze_base_weights()

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        for m in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
            m.unfreeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight transfer (SM3-FIX-09)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q = b_q = w_k = b_k = w_v = b_v = w_o = b_o = None

            src_cls_name = getattr(pretrained_attn, "__class__", type(pretrained_attn)).__name__
            if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
                hasattr(pretrained_attn, "query")
                and not hasattr(pretrained_attn, "dense")
                and not hasattr(pretrained_attn, "out_proj")
            ):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True

            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
                src = pretrained_attn.self
                if hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense"):
                    w_o = pretrained_attn.output.dense.weight
                    b_o = pretrained_attn.output.dense.bias
            else:
                src = pretrained_attn

            # --- GPT-2 fused ---
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                c_attn_w = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
                c_attn_b = src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None
                p_cls = getattr(src.c_attn, "__class__", type(src.c_attn)).__name__
                if (
                    "Conv1D" in p_cls
                    or hasattr(src.c_attn, "nf")
                    or (c_attn_w.shape[0] == self.d_model and c_attn_w.shape[1] != self.d_model)
                ):
                    c_attn_w = c_attn_w.t().contiguous()
                q_dim = self.q_head_size
                k_dim = self.kv_head_size
                v_dim = self.kv_head_size
                w_q = c_attn_w[:q_dim, :]
                w_k = c_attn_w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :]
                w_v = c_attn_w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :]
                if c_attn_b is not None:
                    b_q = c_attn_b[:q_dim]
                    b_k = c_attn_b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim]
                    b_v = c_attn_b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim]
                out_w = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
                p_proj_cls = getattr(src.c_proj, "__class__", type(src.c_proj)).__name__
                if (
                    "Conv1D" in p_proj_cls
                    or hasattr(src.c_proj, "nf")
                    or (out_w.shape[0] == self.d_model and out_w.shape[1] != self.d_model)
                ):
                    out_w = out_w.t().contiguous()
                w_o = out_w
                b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

            # --- BERT/RoBERTa nested ---
            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None
                if w_o is None:
                    for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, attr):
                            proj = getattr(src, attr)
                            w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                            b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                            break

            # --- Qwen / LLaMA ---
            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
                b_q = (
                    src.q_proj.bias.detach()
                    if getattr(src.q_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                    else None
                )
                w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
                b_k = (
                    src.k_proj.bias.detach()
                    if getattr(src.k_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                    else None
                )
                w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
                b_v = (
                    src.v_proj.bias.detach()
                    if getattr(src.v_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                    else None
                )
                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                    b_o = (
                        src.o_proj.bias.detach()
                        if getattr(src.o_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                        else None
                    )

            # --- Flat (DistilBERT) ---
            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

            # --- Fallback ---
            if w_q is None:
                try:
                    adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                    for proj, target in (
                        ("q", self.q_lin),
                        ("k", self.k_lin),
                        ("v", self.v_lin),
                        ("o", self.out_lin),
                    ):
                        w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                        if w is None:
                            continue
                        if proj == "q":
                            w_q, b_q = w, b
                        elif proj == "k":
                            w_k, b_k = w, b
                        elif proj == "v":
                            w_v, b_v = w, b
                        else:
                            w_o, b_o = w, b
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

            # --- Copy into targets (SM3-FIX-09: cast + freeze) ---
            for target_mod, w_src, b_src, name in (
                (self.q_lin, w_q, b_q, "q_lin"),
                (self.k_lin, w_k, b_k, "k_lin"),
                (self.v_lin, w_v, b_v, "v_lin"),
                (self.out_lin, w_o, b_o, "out_lin"),
            ):
                if w_src is None:
                    continue
                tw = target_mod.weight
                w_cast = w_src.to(dtype=tw.dtype, device=tw.device)
                # orient if transposed
                if w_cast.shape != tw.shape and w_cast.t().contiguous().shape == tw.shape:
                    w_cast = w_cast.t().contiguous()
                min_r = min(tw.shape[0], w_cast.shape[0])
                min_c = min(tw.shape[1], w_cast.shape[1])
                tw.data[:min_r, :min_c].copy_(w_cast[:min_r, :min_c])
                if b_src is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    tb = target_mod.bias
                    b_cast = b_src.to(dtype=tb.dtype, device=tb.device)
                    mb = min(tb.numel(), b_cast.numel())
                    tb.data[:mb].copy_(b_cast[:mb])
                fnorm = float(torch.linalg.norm(tw.data.float()))
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning("SAMBAM3Block: %s norm too small (%.2e)", name, fnorm)
                target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def _chunked_sliding_window_attention(
        self,
        q: torch.Tensor,
        k: torch.Tensor,
        v: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_len: int = 0,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, L, d = q.shape
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(d)
        chunk = 64
        outputs = []

        for start in range(0, L, chunk):
            end = min(start + chunk, L)
            q_c = q[:, :, start:end]
            C_q = end - start

            # Absolute query positions (offset by past_len)
            q_abs_start = past_len + start
            q_abs_end = past_len + end

            # Key range: absolute positions in [max(0, q_abs_start - window), q_abs_end + window) for bidir
            if self.is_decoder:
                k_abs_start = max(0, q_abs_start - self.window)
                k_abs_end = q_abs_end
            else:
                k_abs_start = max(0, q_abs_start - self.window)
                k_abs_end = min(past_len + L, q_abs_end + self.window)

            # Slice keys in the *cache coordinates* (0..past_len+L)
            k_c = k[:, :, k_abs_start:k_abs_end]
            v_c = v[:, :, k_abs_start:k_abs_end]

            scores = torch.matmul(q_c.float(), k_c.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous().float()) * scale

            q_idx = torch.arange(q_abs_start, q_abs_end, device=q.device).view(1, 1, C_q, 1)
            k_idx = torch.arange(k_abs_start, k_abs_end, device=q.device).view(1, 1, 1, -1)
            dist = (q_idx - k_idx).abs()
            valid = dist <= self.window
            if self.is_decoder:
                valid = valid & (k_idx <= q_idx)

            scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid, -1e4)

            # SM3-FIX-07: head_mask dim==3 handled uniformly
            if attention_mask is not None:
                if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                    # (B, N_total)
                    pad = (attention_mask[:, k_abs_start:k_abs_end] == 0).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                    scores = scores.masked_fill(pad, -1e4)
                elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
                    scores = scores + attention_mask[:, start:end, k_abs_start:k_abs_end].unsqueeze(
                        1
                    ).to(scores.dtype)
                elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                    scores = scores + attention_mask[:, :, start:end, k_abs_start:k_abs_end].to(
                        scores.dtype
                    )

            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
            probs = self.dropout(probs.to(v_c.dtype))

            if head_mask is not None:
                hm = head_mask
                if hm.dim() == 1:
                    hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif hm.dim() == 2:
                    hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
                elif hm.dim() == 3:
                    # (B, H, Lk) — align to current key slice and unsqueeze
                    hm = hm[:, :, k_abs_start:k_abs_end].unsqueeze(2)
                hm = hm.to(probs.dtype)
                probs = probs * hm

            out_c = torch.matmul(probs, v_c)
            outputs.append(out_c)

        return torch.cat(outputs, dim=2)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("SAMBAM3Block does not support cross-attention")

        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        # ---- SM3-FIX-05: read cache ----
        past_k = past_v = None
        past_len = 0
        if past_key_value is not None:
            if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 2:
                past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
                past_len = past_k.shape[-2]
            elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                past_k = past_key_value.key_cache[0]
                past_v = past_key_value.value_cache[0]
                past_len = past_k.shape[-2]

        # ---- QKV ----
        q = (
            self.q_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.n_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k = (
            self.k_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.v_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # ---- SM3-FIX-06: RoPE with offset ----
        pos_emb = position_embeddings or kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, tuple) and len(pos_emb) == 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb
            if cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L:
                cos = cos.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
                sin = sin.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # ---- Append to KV cache ----
        if past_k is not None:
            k = torch.cat([past_k.to(k.dtype), k], dim=-2)
            v = torch.cat([past_v.to(v.dtype), v], dim=-2)

        present_key_value = (k, v)

        # ---- GQA expand ----
        if self.num_kv_heads != self.n_heads:
            k_exp = repeat_kv(k, self.num_key_value_groups)
            v_exp = repeat_kv(v, self.num_key_value_groups)
        else:
            k_exp, v_exp = k, v

        # ---- Attention ----
        attn_out = self._chunked_sliding_window_attention(
            q,
            k_exp,
            v_exp,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            head_mask=head_mask,
            past_len=past_len,
        )
        attn_out = attn_out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.q_head_size)
        attn_out = attn_out.to(target_dtype)

        # ---- Mamba branch ----
        mamba_out, _ = self.mamba(hidden_states, attention_mask=attention_mask, **kwargs)
        mamba_out = mamba_out.to(target_dtype)

        # ---- Fusion (SM3-FIX-04: alpha cast) ----
        alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.alpha).to(target_dtype)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = alpha * attn_out + (1.0 - alpha) * mamba_out
        else:
            attn_proj = self.out_lin(attn_out).to(target_dtype)
            out = alpha * attn_proj + (1.0 - alpha) * mamba_out

        out = torch.nan_to_num(out.to(target_dtype), nan=0.0)

        # ---- SM3-FIX-11: no silent pad; assert shape ----
        if out.shape[-1] != D:
            raise RuntimeError(f"SAMBAM3Block: output dim {out.shape[-1]} != expected {D}")

        return out, (present_key_value if output_attentions is False else (out, present_key_value))

def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
    with torch.no_grad():
        w_q = b_q = w_k = b_k = w_v = b_v = w_o = b_o = None

        src_cls_name = getattr(pretrained_attn, "__class__", type(pretrained_attn)).__name__
        if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
            hasattr(pretrained_attn, "query")
            and not hasattr(pretrained_attn, "dense")
            and not hasattr(pretrained_attn, "out_proj")
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True

        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
            src = pretrained_attn.self
            if hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense"):
                w_o = pretrained_attn.output.dense.weight
                b_o = pretrained_attn.output.dense.bias
        else:
            src = pretrained_attn

        # --- GPT-2 fused ---
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
            c_attn_w = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
            c_attn_b = src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None
            p_cls = getattr(src.c_attn, "__class__", type(src.c_attn)).__name__
            if (
                "Conv1D" in p_cls
                or hasattr(src.c_attn, "nf")
                or (c_attn_w.shape[0] == self.d_model and c_attn_w.shape[1] != self.d_model)
            ):
                c_attn_w = c_attn_w.t().contiguous()
            q_dim = self.q_head_size
            k_dim = self.kv_head_size
            v_dim = self.kv_head_size
            w_q = c_attn_w[:q_dim, :]
            w_k = c_attn_w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :]
            w_v = c_attn_w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :]
            if c_attn_b is not None:
                b_q = c_attn_b[:q_dim]
                b_k = c_attn_b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim]
                b_v = c_attn_b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim]
            out_w = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
            p_proj_cls = getattr(src.c_proj, "__class__", type(src.c_proj)).__name__
            if (
                "Conv1D" in p_proj_cls
                or hasattr(src.c_proj, "nf")
                or (out_w.shape[0] == self.d_model and out_w.shape[1] != self.d_model)
            ):
                out_w = out_w.t().contiguous()
            w_o = out_w
            b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

        # --- BERT/RoBERTa nested ---
        elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
            w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
            b_q = src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
            b_k = src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
            b_v = src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None
            if w_o is None:
                for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                    if hasattr(src, attr):
                        proj = getattr(src, attr)
                        w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                        b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                        break

        # --- Qwen / LLaMA ---
        elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
            w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
            b_q = (
                src.q_proj.bias.detach()
                if getattr(src.q_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                else None
            )
            w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
            b_k = (
                src.k_proj.bias.detach()
                if getattr(src.k_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                else None
            )
            w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
            b_v = (
                src.v_proj.bias.detach()
                if getattr(src.v_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                else None
            )
            if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                b_o = (
                    src.o_proj.bias.detach()
                    if getattr(src.o_proj, "bias", None) is not None
                    else None
                )

        # --- Flat (DistilBERT) ---
        elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
            w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
            b_q = src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
            b_k = src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
            b_v = src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None
            if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

        # --- Fallback ---
        if w_q is None:
            try:
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for proj, target in (
                    ("q", self.q_lin),
                    ("k", self.k_lin),
                    ("v", self.v_lin),
                    ("o", self.out_lin),
                ):
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                    if w is None:
                        continue
                    if proj == "q":
                        w_q, b_q = w, b
                    elif proj == "k":
                        w_k, b_k = w, b
                    elif proj == "v":
                        w_v, b_v = w, b
                    else:
                        w_o, b_o = w, b
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        # --- Copy into targets (SM3-FIX-09: cast + freeze) ---
        for target_mod, w_src, b_src, name in (
            (self.q_lin, w_q, b_q, "q_lin"),
            (self.k_lin, w_k, b_k, "k_lin"),
            (self.v_lin, w_v, b_v, "v_lin"),
            (self.out_lin, w_o, b_o, "out_lin"),
        ):
            if w_src is None:
                continue
            tw = target_mod.weight
            w_cast = w_src.to(dtype=tw.dtype, device=tw.device)
            # orient if transposed
            if w_cast.shape != tw.shape and w_cast.t().contiguous().shape == tw.shape:
                w_cast = w_cast.t().contiguous()
            min_r = min(tw.shape[0], w_cast.shape[0])
            min_c = min(tw.shape[1], w_cast.shape[1])
            tw.data[:min_r, :min_c].copy_(w_cast[:min_r, :min_c])
            if b_src is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                tb = target_mod.bias
                b_cast = b_src.to(dtype=tb.dtype, device=tb.device)
                mb = min(tb.numel(), b_cast.numel())
                tb.data[:mb].copy_(b_cast[:mb])
            fnorm = float(torch.linalg.norm(tw.data.float()))
            if fnorm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning("SAMBAM3Block: %s norm too small (%.2e)", name, fnorm)
            target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

Expand source code 
def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    *args: Any,
    **kwargs: Any,
):
    if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
        raise NotImplementedError("SAMBAM3Block does not support cross-attention")

    B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
    target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype

    # ---- SM3-FIX-05: read cache ----
    past_k = past_v = None
    past_len = 0
    if past_key_value is not None:
        if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 2:
            past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
            past_len = past_k.shape[-2]
        elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
            past_k = past_key_value.key_cache[0]
            past_v = past_key_value.value_cache[0]
            past_len = past_k.shape[-2]

    # ---- QKV ----
    q = (
        self.q_lin(hidden_states)
        .view(B, L, self.n_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )
    k = (
        self.k_lin(hidden_states)
        .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )
    v = (
        self.v_lin(hidden_states)
        .view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )

    # ---- SM3-FIX-06: RoPE with offset ----
    pos_emb = position_embeddings or kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
    if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, tuple) and len(pos_emb) == 2:
        cos, sin = pos_emb
        if cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L:
            cos = cos.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
            sin = sin.narrow(-2, past_len, L)
        q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

    # ---- Append to KV cache ----
    if past_k is not None:
        k = torch.cat([past_k.to(k.dtype), k], dim=-2)
        v = torch.cat([past_v.to(v.dtype), v], dim=-2)

    present_key_value = (k, v)

    # ---- GQA expand ----
    if self.num_kv_heads != self.n_heads:
        k_exp = repeat_kv(k, self.num_key_value_groups)
        v_exp = repeat_kv(v, self.num_key_value_groups)
    else:
        k_exp, v_exp = k, v

    # ---- Attention ----
    attn_out = self._chunked_sliding_window_attention(
        q,
        k_exp,
        v_exp,
        attention_mask=attention_mask,
        head_mask=head_mask,
        past_len=past_len,
    )
    attn_out = attn_out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.q_head_size)
    attn_out = attn_out.to(target_dtype)

    # ---- Mamba branch ----
    mamba_out, _ = self.mamba(hidden_states, attention_mask=attention_mask, **kwargs)
    mamba_out = mamba_out.to(target_dtype)

    # ---- Fusion (SM3-FIX-04: alpha cast) ----
    alpha = torch.sigmoid(self.alpha).to(target_dtype)

    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        out = alpha * attn_out + (1.0 - alpha) * mamba_out
    else:
        attn_proj = self.out_lin(attn_out).to(target_dtype)
        out = alpha * attn_proj + (1.0 - alpha) * mamba_out

    out = torch.nan_to_num(out.to(target_dtype), nan=0.0)

    # ---- SM3-FIX-11: no silent pad; assert shape ----
    if out.shape[-1] != D:
        raise RuntimeError(f"SAMBAM3Block: output dim {out.shape[-1]} != expected {D}")

    return out, (present_key_value if output_attentions is False else (out, present_key_value))

class SVDMambaSub (d_model: int = 768,
config: Any | None = None,
