is_decoder: bool = False,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
class SVDMambaSub(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(self, d_model: int = 768, config: Any | None = None, rank: int | None = None, is_decoder: bool = False, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.ssm = SimpleSSM(d_model=d_model, config=config, is_decoder=is_decoder)
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        
        nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.bias)

        self._ssm_supports_state = self._ssm_accepts_past_state()

    def _ssm_accepts_past_state(self) -> bool:
        import inspect
        try:
            sig = inspect.signature(self.ssm.forward)
            return "past_state" in sig.parameters or "state" in sig.parameters
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return False

    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None, past_state: Any | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, Any | None]:
        x_proj = self.in_proj(hidden_states)
        ssm_result: Any = None
        present_state: Any | None = None

        if self._ssm_supports_state:
            try:
                ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask, past_state=past_state)
            except TypeError:
                try:
                    ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask, state=past_state)
                except Exception:
                    ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask)
        else:
            ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask)

        if isinstance(ssm_result, tuple):
            ssm_out = ssm_result[0]
            if len(ssm_result) > 1: present_state = ssm_result[1]
        else:
            ssm_out = ssm_result

        out = self.out_proj(ssm_out)
        return out, present_state

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

class SVDS6StateSpace (d_model: int,
rank: int = 16,
bottleneck_dim: int = 64,
dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
device: torch.device | None = None)
Expand source code 
class SVDS6StateSpace(nn.Module):
    """
    Per-token low-rank SVD state transition.

        U_t, Σ_t, V_t, B_t, C_t = Bottleneck(x_t)
        h_t = U_t · diag(Σ_t) · V_t · h_{t-1} + B_t · x_t
        y_t = C_t · h_t

    Contractive by construction (MSF-FIX-03):
      ||U_t||_2 ≤ 1, ||V_t||_2 ≤ 1, Σ_t ∈ (0, 1)   ⇒  ||UΣV||_2 ≤ 1.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        d_model: int,
        rank: int = 16,
        bottleneck_dim: int = 64,
        dtype: torch.dtype | None = None,
        device: torch.device | None = None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.rank = max(1, int(rank))
        self.bottleneck_dim = max(self.rank, int(bottleneck_dim))

        fk = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}

        # MSF-FIX-10: bottleneck to keep params small
        self.down = nn.Linear(d_model, self.bottleneck_dim, bias=False, **fk)

        # Outputs from the bottleneck:
        #   - U:   d_model * rank         (per-token basis for columns)
        #   - V:   rank * d_model         (per-token basis for rows)
        #   - sigma: rank                 (per-token singular values)
        #   - B:   d_model                (input gate)
        #   - C:   d_model                (output gate)
        self.up_u = nn.Linear(self.bottleneck_dim, d_model * self.rank, bias=False, **fk)
        self.up_v = nn.Linear(self.bottleneck_dim, self.rank * d_model, bias=False, **fk)
        self.up_sigma = nn.Linear(self.bottleneck_dim, self.rank, bias=True, **fk)
        self.up_b = nn.Linear(self.bottleneck_dim, d_model, bias=True, **fk)
        self.up_c = nn.Linear(self.bottleneck_dim, d_model, bias=True, **fk)

        # Sigma bias: initial sigmoid ≈ 0.85 (stable contraction)
        with torch.no_grad():
            nn.init.zeros_(self.up_sigma.weight)
            nn.init.constant_(self.up_sigma.bias, 1.75)  # sigmoid(1.75) ≈ 0.85

        # C bias: identity-like start
        with torch.no_grad():
            if self.up_c.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(self.up_c.bias)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor):
        B, L, D = x.shape
        r = self.rank

        z = self.down(x)  # (B, L, bottleneck)

        U = self.up_u(z).view(B, L, D, r)
        V = self.up_v(z).view(B, L, r, D)
        sigma = torch.sigmoid(self.up_sigma(z))  # (B, L, r) ∈ (0,1)
        B_seq = self.up_b(z)  # (B, L, D)
        C_seq = self.up_c(z)  # (B, L, D)

        # MSF-FIX-03: enforce unit spectral norms on U, V
        U = F.normalize(U, p=2, dim=-2, eps=1e-8)
        V = F.normalize(V, p=2, dim=-2, eps=1e-8)

        return U, sigma, V, B_seq, C_seq

Per-token low-rank SVD state transition.

U_t, Σ_t, V_t, B_t, C_t = Bottleneck(x_t)
h_t = U_t · diag(Σ_t) · V_t · h_{t-1} + B_t · x_t
y_t = C_t · h_t

Contractive by construction (MSF-FIX-03): ||U_t||_2 ≤ 1, ||V_t||_2 ≤ 1, Σ_t ∈ (0, 1) ⇒ ||UΣV||_2 ≤ 1.

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("samba_moe", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class SambaMoEBlock(BaseAttention):
    r"""SambaMoE: SAMBA-M3 + REAP-MoE Hybrid Architecture with pretrained weight copying."""

    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        n_heads: int | None = None,
        n_experts: int = 8,
        window: int = 128,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        hidden_size = kwargs.get("d_model") or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or d_model
        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            cand = getattr(
                pretrained_attn,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(
                    pretrained_attn,
                    "embed_dim",
                    getattr(pretrained_attn, "all_head_size", None),
                ),
            )
            if (
                cand is None
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "q_proj")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.q_proj, "in_features")
            ):
                cand = pretrained_attn.q_proj.in_features
            elif (
                cand is None
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "q_lin")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.q_lin, "in_features")
            ):
                cand = pretrained_attn.q_lin.in_features
            if cand is not None:
                hidden_size = cand
        if hidden_size is None and config is not None:
            hidden_size = _cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", _cfg_get(config, "d_model", None))
        hidden_size = int(hidden_size or 768)

        if config is not None:
            n_heads = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(config, "n_heads", n_heads or 12),
            )
            n_experts = _cfg_get(config, "num_experts", _cfg_get(config, "n_experts", n_experts))
            window = _cfg_get(config, "window_size", window)
        else:
            n_heads = n_heads or 12

        self.d_model = hidden_size
        self.n_heads = int(n_heads)
        self.lora_rank = kwargs.get("lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", lora_rank))
        self.lora_alpha = kwargs.get("lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))

        self.swa_mamba = SAMBAM3Block(
            config=config,
            d_model=self.d_model,
            n_heads=self.n_heads,
            window=window,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            is_decoder=is_decoder,
            **kwargs,
        )

        if REAPMoE is not None:
            try:
                self.moe = REAPMoE(self.d_model, n_experts=n_experts)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("REAPMoE construction failed: %s, using MinimalMoEFallback", e)
                self.moe = MinimalMoEFallback(self.d_model, n_experts=min(4, n_experts))
        else:
            self.moe = MinimalMoEFallback(self.d_model, n_experts=min(4, n_experts))

        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model)
        self.aux_loss: torch.Tensor = torch.tensor(0.0)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    @property
    def is_nested_encoder(self) -> bool:
        return getattr(self.swa_mamba, "is_nested_encoder", False)

    @is_nested_encoder.setter
    def is_nested_encoder(self, val: bool) -> None:
        if hasattr(self, "swa_mamba"):
            self.swa_mamba.is_nested_encoder = val

    @property
    def query(self) -> nn.Module:
        return getattr(self.swa_mamba, "q_lin", None)

    @property
    def key(self) -> nn.Module:
        return getattr(self.swa_mamba, "k_lin", None)

    @property
    def value(self) -> nn.Module:
        return getattr(self.swa_mamba, "v_lin", None)

    def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
        """Propagate weight transfer to internal SAMBA-M3 and SSM components."""
        src_cls_name = getattr(pretrained_attn, "__class__", type(pretrained_attn)).__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(pretrained_attn, attr)
            for attr in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        if (
            src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention")
            or (src_cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
            or (hasattr(pretrained_attn, "query") and not has_out_proj)
            or (hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output"))
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
            if hasattr(self, "swa_mamba"):
                self.swa_mamba.is_nested_encoder = True

        if hasattr(self.swa_mamba, "copy_weights"):
            self.swa_mamba.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None] | torch.Tensor:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("SambaMoEBlock does not support cross-attention")

        x, attn_w = self.swa_mamba(
            hidden_states,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            head_mask=head_mask,
            encoder_hidden_states=encoder_hidden_states,
            encoder_attention_mask=encoder_attention_mask,
            past_key_value=past_key_value,
            output_attentions=output_attentions,
            output_hidden_states=output_hidden_states,
            return_dict=return_dict,
            **kwargs,
        )

        router_in = getattr(self.moe, "d_model", self.d_model)
        if hasattr(self.moe, "router") and hasattr(self.moe.router, "in_features"):
            router_in = self.moe.router.in_features

        if x.shape[-1] != router_in:
            x_moe = (
                F.pad(x, (0, router_in - x.shape[-1]))
                if x.shape[-1] < router_in
                else x[..., :router_in]
            )
        else:
            x_moe = x

        moe_out, _ = self.moe(x_moe)
        self.aux_loss = getattr(self.moe, "aux_loss", torch.tensor(0.0, device=x.device))

        if moe_out.shape[-1] != x.shape[-1]:
            moe_out = (
                F.pad(moe_out, (0, x.shape[-1] - moe_out.shape[-1]))
                if moe_out.shape[-1] < x.shape[-1]
                else moe_out[..., : x.shape[-1]]
            )

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        x = x.to(target_dtype)
        moe_out = moe_out.to(target_dtype)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            # Nested encoder: bypass outer LayerNorm (self.norm) and double residual
            out = x + moe_out
        else:
            out = self.norm(x + moe_out).to(target_dtype)

        if out.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            out = (
                F.pad(out, (0, hidden_states.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
                if out.shape[-1] < hidden_states.shape[-1]
                else out[..., : hidden_states.shape[-1]]
            )
        return (out.to(target_dtype), attn_w)

class SeerGating (head_dim: int,
num_heads: int,
gate_rank: int,

temperature: float = 1.0,
**kwargs)
Expand source code 
class SeerGating(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        head_dim: int,
        num_heads: int,
        gate_rank: int,
        top_k: int,
        temperature: float = 1.0,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.num_heads = num_heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.top_k = top_k
        self.temperature = temperature

        self.q_pool = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(head_dim, gate_rank),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(gate_rank, gate_rank),
        )
        self.k_pool = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(head_dim, gate_rank),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(gate_rank, gate_rank),
        )

    def forward(
        self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, n_blocks: int, training: bool = True
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        gr = self.q_pool[0].out_features

        Q_pooled = Q.mean(dim=2)
        K_pooled = K.mean(dim=2)

        q_gate = self.q_pool(Q_pooled).view(B, H, 1, gr)
        k_gate = self.k_pool(K_pooled).view(B, H, gr, 1)

        gate_score = torch.sigmoid(
            torch.matmul(q_gate, k_gate).squeeze(-1).squeeze(-1) / self.temperature
        )

        # FIXED: Gradient breakage due to .long() and .item() in python loop.
        # Implemented a fully vectorized Soft Straight-Through Estimator (STE) mask
        # so gate_score receives dense gradients while maintaining hard sparsity in forward pass.
        k_float = gate_score * n_blocks
        
        idx = torch.arange(n_blocks, device=Q.device).view(1, 1, 1, n_blocks)
        k_float_exp = k_float.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
        
        # Soft mask for differentiability
        temperature = 0.1
        soft_mask = torch.sigmoid((k_float_exp - idx) / temperature)
        
        # Hard mask for strict sparsity (k_float rounded)
        k_int = torch.clamp(k_float.round(), min=1, max=n_blocks).long().unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
        hard_mask = (idx < k_int).float()
        
        # STE Trick: Forward is hard, backward is soft
        ste_mask = hard_mask + soft_mask - soft_mask.detach()
        
        mask = torch.zeros(B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks, device=Q.device, dtype=ste_mask.dtype)
        # Apply STE mask across all queries (dim 2) for the selected key blocks (dim 3)
        mask = mask + ste_mask
        
        # Ensure diagonal and first block are always kept
        diag_idx = torch.arange(n_blocks, device=Q.device)
        mask[:, :, diag_idx, diag_idx] = 1.0
        mask[:, :, :, 0] = 1.0
        
        return mask

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def forward(
    self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, n_blocks: int, training: bool = True
) -> torch.Tensor:
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    gr = self.q_pool[0].out_features

    Q_pooled = Q.mean(dim=2)
    K_pooled = K.mean(dim=2)

    q_gate = self.q_pool(Q_pooled).view(B, H, 1, gr)
    k_gate = self.k_pool(K_pooled).view(B, H, gr, 1)

    gate_score = torch.sigmoid(
        torch.matmul(q_gate, k_gate).squeeze(-1).squeeze(-1) / self.temperature
    )

    # FIXED: Gradient breakage due to .long() and .item() in python loop.
    # Implemented a fully vectorized Soft Straight-Through Estimator (STE) mask
    # so gate_score receives dense gradients while maintaining hard sparsity in forward pass.
    k_float = gate_score * n_blocks
    
    idx = torch.arange(n_blocks, device=Q.device).view(1, 1, 1, n_blocks)
    k_float_exp = k_float.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
    
    # Soft mask for differentiability
    temperature = 0.1
    soft_mask = torch.sigmoid((k_float_exp - idx) / temperature)
    
    # Hard mask for strict sparsity (k_float rounded)
    k_int = torch.clamp(k_float.round(), min=1, max=n_blocks).long().unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
    hard_mask = (idx < k_int).float()
    
    # STE Trick: Forward is hard, backward is soft
    ste_mask = hard_mask + soft_mask - soft_mask.detach()
    
    mask = torch.zeros(B, H, n_blocks, n_blocks, device=Q.device, dtype=ste_mask.dtype)
    # Apply STE mask across all queries (dim 2) for the selected key blocks (dim 3)
    mask = mask + ste_mask
    
    # Ensure diagonal and first block are always kept
    diag_idx = torch.arange(n_blocks, device=Q.device)
    mask[:, :, diag_idx, diag_idx] = 1.0
    mask[:, :, :, 0] = 1.0
    
    return mask

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class SelectiveMamba (config: Any | None = None,
rank: int | None = None,
d_model: int | Any | None = None,
dim: int | None = None,
d_state: int = 16,
d_conv: int = 4,
expand: int = 1,
lora_rank: int = 16,
lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
is_decoder: bool | None = None,
pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("selective_mamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "upi_mamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True
)  # DEPRECATED alias
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "upi_stuffed_mamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True
)  # DEPRECATED alias
class SelectiveMamba(BaseAttention):
    r"""
    Selective Mamba SSM block.

    Implements a Mamba-style selective state space model with
    input-dependent B, C, and dt parameters:
        h_t = Ā_t · h_{t-1} + B̄_t · x_t
        y_t = C_t · h_t + D · x_t
    where Ā_t = exp(dt_t · A).

    The "selectivity" comes from x_proj producing B_t and C_t
    from the input.

    NOTE: The previous name "UPI-Stuffed" has been removed
    because none of the claimed features (Uncertainty Penalty,
    Interpolation, Stuffing) were actually implemented.

    Registered as "upi_mamba" (legacy alias) and
    "selective_mamba" (canonical).
    """

    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        d_model: int | Any | None = None,
        dim: int | None = None,
        d_state: int = 16,
        d_conv: int = 4,
        expand: int = 1,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        src_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("old_attn")

        if dim is None and d_model is None:
            if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
                dim = _cfg_get(
                    config,
                    "hidden_size",
                    _cfg_get(config, "dim", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)),
                )
            else:
                dim = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
        else:
            dim = dim or d_model

        if hasattr(dim, "hidden_size"):
            dim = dim.hidden_size

        self.dim: int = int(dim)
        self.d_state: int = d_state
        self.d_conv: int = d_conv
        self.expand: int = expand
        self.inner_dim: int = self.dim * expand
        self.lora_rank = kwargs.get("lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", lora_rank))
        self.lora_alpha = kwargs.get("lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))

        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder: bool = is_decoder
        elif config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            self.is_decoder = bool(getattr(config, "is_decoder", False))
        else:
            self.is_decoder = False

        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        self.in_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.inner_dim * 2,
            bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(
            self.inner_dim,
            self.inner_dim,
            kernel_size=d_conv,
            groups=self.inner_dim,
            padding=0,
        )
        # A_log init: log([1, 2, ..., d_state]) repeated.
        # In our scan, we compute A = +exp(A_log) (positive),
        # then log_decay = -dt * A (negative), giving decay < 1.
        # This is mathematically equivalent to Mamba's
        # A = -exp(A_log), decay = exp(dt * A).
        A_init = torch.arange(1, d_state + 1, dtype=torch.float32).repeat(self.inner_dim, 1)
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(A_init))

        # Trainable skip connection D matching inner_dim
        self.D = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.inner_dim))

        # Mamba paper: dt_rank = ceil(d_model / 16), not d_inner
        self.dt_rank = math.ceil(self.dim / 16)

        # x_proj maps inner_dim → dt_rank + 2*d_state
        self.x_proj = nn.Linear(
            self.inner_dim,
            self.dt_rank + 2 * self.d_state,
            bias=False,
        )
        self.x_proj.weight.requires_grad = True

        # dt_proj maps dt_rank → inner_dim
        self.dt_proj = nn.Linear(self.dt_rank, self.inner_dim, bias=True)

        # Initialize dt_proj bias (Mamba paper Eq. 12)
        dt_min, dt_max = 0.001, 0.1
        dt_init = torch.exp(
            torch.rand(self.inner_dim) * (math.log(dt_max) - math.log(dt_min)) + math.log(dt_min)
        )
        dt_init_inv = dt_init + torch.log(-torch.expm1(-dt_init))
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.dt_proj.bias.copy_(dt_init_inv)
            # Weight init: uniform(-1/sqrt(dt_rank), 1/sqrt(dt_rank))
            bound = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.dt_rank)
            nn.init.uniform_(self.dt_proj.weight, -bound, bound)
        self.dt_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
        self.dt_proj.bias.requires_grad = True

        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.inner_dim,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        if src_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src_attn)

    @property
    def query(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def key(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def value(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        """Robust weight copying from DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2 Conv1D, or Qwen/LLaMA."""
        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q, _b_q = None, None
            w_k, _b_k = None, None
            w_v, _b_v = None, None
            w_o, b_o = None, None

            src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
            if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
                hasattr(old_attn, "query")
                and not hasattr(old_attn, "dense")
                and not hasattr(old_attn, "out_proj")
            ):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True

            if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
                src = old_attn.self
                if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                    w_o = old_attn.output.dense.weight.detach()
                    b_o = (
                        old_attn.output.dense.bias.detach()
                        if old_attn.output.dense.bias is not None
                        else None
                    )
            else:
                src = old_attn

            # Pattern 1: GPT-2 Fused c_attn
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                c_attn_w = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
                (src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None)
                if c_attn_w.dim() == 2 and (
                    c_attn_w.shape[0] == self.dim and c_attn_w.shape[1] != self.dim
                ):
                    c_attn_w = c_attn_w.t()

                split_q = min(self.dim, c_attn_w.shape[0] // 3)
                w_q = c_attn_w[:split_q, :]
                w_k = c_attn_w[split_q : 2 * split_q, :]
                w_v = c_attn_w[2 * split_q : 3 * split_q, :]

                out_w = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
                if out_w.dim() == 2 and (out_w.shape[0] != self.dim and out_w.shape[1] == self.dim):
                    out_w = out_w.t()
                w_o = out_w
                b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

            # Pattern 2: RoBERTa / BERT separate query, key, value
            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
                src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
                src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
                src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None

                if w_o is None:
                    for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, attr):
                            proj = getattr(src, attr)
                            w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                            b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                            break

            # Pattern 3: Qwen / LLaMA
            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
                src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
                src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
                src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None

                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

            # Pattern 4: DistilBERT
            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
                src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
                src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
                src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None

                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

            # Fallback: UniversalAdapter extraction
            if w_q is None or w_v is None or (w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder):
                for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
                    try:
                        w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, proj)
                        if w is not None:
                            if proj == "q" and w_q is None:
                                w_q, _b_q = w, b
                            elif proj == "k" and w_k is None:
                                w_k, _b_k = w, b
                            elif proj == "v" and w_v is None:
                                w_v, _b_v = w, b
                            elif proj == "o" and w_o is None:
                                w_o, b_o = w, b
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug("UniversalAdapter extraction for %s failed: %s", proj, e)

            missing = []
            if w_q is None:
                missing.append("Q")
            if w_v is None:
                missing.append("V")
            if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
                missing.append("O")
            if missing:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"SelectiveMamba: cannot extract {missing} from "
                    f"{old_attn.__class__.__name__}. Aborting to avoid "
                    f"silent model corruption."
                )

            def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
                if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                    return w.t()
                return w

            def _adapt_to_inner(w: torch.Tensor, name: str) -> torch.Tensor:
                """
                Adapt weight from source head count to inner_dim.
                If source is smaller: tile and truncate.
                If source is larger: slice to fit.
                """
                src_out, src_in = w.shape
                if src_out == self.inner_dim:
                    return w

                if src_out > self.inner_dim:
                    # Source has more heads — take the first inner_dim
                    logger.debug(
                        "SelectiveMamba: slicing %s from %d to %d",
                        name,
                        src_out,
                        self.inner_dim,
                    )
                    return w[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

                # Source is smaller — repeat to fill
                rep = math.ceil(self.inner_dim / src_out)
                logger.debug(
                    "SelectiveMamba: repeating %s from %d to %d (rep=%d)",
                    name,
                    src_out,
                    self.inner_dim,
                    rep,
                )
                return w.repeat(rep, 1)[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

            w_v_or = _orient(w_v, self.dim)
            w_q_or = _orient(w_q, self.dim)
            w_v_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_v_or, "W_V")
            w_q_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_q_or, "W_Q")

            v_chunk = w_v_or[:, : self.dim]
            q_chunk = w_q_or[:, : self.dim]

            self.in_proj.weight.data[: self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(v_chunk)
            self.in_proj.weight.data[self.inner_dim : 2 * self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(q_chunk)

            if w_o is not None:
                w_o_or = _orient(w_o, self.inner_dim)
                min_o_out = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[0])
                min_o_in = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[1])
                self.out_proj.weight.data[:min_o_out, :min_o_in].copy_(
                    w_o_or[:min_o_out, :min_o_in]
                )
                if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    b_val = b_o.data if isinstance(b_o, nn.Parameter) else b_o
                    self.out_proj.bias.data[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                        b_val[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))]
                    )

            # Frobenius norm checks
            for name, w in [
                ("in_proj", self.in_proj.weight),
                ("out_proj", self.out_proj.weight),
            ]:
                if torch.norm(w.float(), p="fro").item() < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(f"SelectiveMamba: {name} has near-zero Frobenius norm (< 1e-4)")

            self.in_proj.freeze_base_weights()
            self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()
            self.x_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
            self.dt_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
            self.dt_proj.bias.requires_grad = True

            # Preserve source LoRA adapters
            source_submodules = {
                "in": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q"],
                "out": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense", "out", "c_proj"],
            }
            for name in source_submodules["in"]:
                if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                    sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                    if (
                        hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                        and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                        and sub.lora_A is not None
                        and sub.lora_B is not None
                    ):
                        src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                        src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                        if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_A is not None:
                            r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                            in_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                            self.in_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                                src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                            )
                            self.in_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_B is not None:
                            out_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                            r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                            self.in_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                                src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                            )
                            self.in_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                            self.in_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                        break

            for name in source_submodules["out"]:
                if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                    sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                    if (
                        hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                        and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                        and sub.lora_A is not None
                        and sub.lora_B is not None
                    ):
                        src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                        src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                        if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_A is not None:
                            r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                            in_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                            self.out_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                                src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                            )
                            self.out_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_B is not None:
                            out_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                            r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                            self.out_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                                src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                            )
                            self.out_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                            self.out_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                        break

    def _selective_scan(
        self,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        dt: torch.Tensor,
        B_t: torch.Tensor,
        C_t: torch.Tensor,
        mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        reverse: bool = False,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Bounded chunked associative SSM scan in FP32 with O(chunk=64) memory."""
        if reverse:
            x = torch.flip(x, dims=[1])
            dt = torch.flip(dt, dims=[1])
            B_t = torch.flip(B_t, dims=[1])
            C_t = torch.flip(C_t, dims=[1])
            if mask is not None:
                mask = torch.flip(mask, dims=[1])

        batch, seqlen, _ = x.shape
        A = torch.exp(torch.clamp(self.A_log.float(), min=-20.0, max=10.0))  # [inner_dim, d_state]

        # Path 1: Hardware-fused Triton selective scan on CUDA
        if HAS_TRITON_UPI and triton_selective_scan is not None and x.is_cuda and mask is None:
            try:
                return triton_selective_scan(
                    x.contiguous(),
                    dt.contiguous(),
                    -A.contiguous(),
                    B_t.contiguous(),
                    C_t.contiguous(),
                    None,
                    delta_softplus=False,
                ).to(x.dtype)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Triton selective scan failed, falling back to chunked PyTorch: %s", e)

        # In case of padding mask, zero out invalid token inputs
        if mask is not None:
            m = mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
            x = x * m
            dt = dt * m

        chunk_size = 64
        num_chunks = (seqlen + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size
        h_running = x.new_zeros(batch, self.inner_dim, self.d_state, dtype=torch.float32)
        y_chunks = []
        A_view = A.view(1, 1, self.inner_dim, self.d_state)

        for c in range(num_chunks):
            s_idx = c * chunk_size
            e_idx = min(seqlen, s_idx + chunk_size)

            dt_c = dt[:, s_idx:e_idx]
            x_c = x[:, s_idx:e_idx]
            B_c = B_t[:, s_idx:e_idx]
            C_c = C_t[:, s_idx:e_idx]

            # Compute u and log_a strictly for current 64-token chunk to avoid buffer overflow
            log_a_c = -(dt_c.unsqueeze(-1) * A_view).float()
            u_c = (dt_c.unsqueeze(-1) * B_c.unsqueeze(2) * x_c.unsqueeze(-1)).float()

            lambda_c = torch.clamp(torch.cumsum(log_a_c, dim=1), min=-25.0, max=0.0)

            decay_c = torch.exp(lambda_c)
            decay_inv_c = torch.exp(-lambda_c).clamp(max=1e10)

            h_intra = decay_c * torch.cumsum(decay_inv_c * u_c, dim=1)
            h_inter = decay_c * h_running.unsqueeze(1)

            h_chunk = torch.clamp(h_intra + h_inter, min=-1e4, max=1e4)
            y_c = (C_c.unsqueeze(2).float() * h_chunk).sum(dim=-1).to(x.dtype)
            y_chunks.append(y_c)
            h_running = h_chunk[:, -1]

        y = torch.cat(y_chunks, dim=1)
        y = (y + self.D.to(x.dtype) * x).to(x.dtype)

        if reverse:
            y = torch.flip(y, dims=[1])

        return y

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError(
                "SelectiveMamba does not support cross-attention. "
                "This module replaces SELF-attention only. "
                "For cross-attention, use a different module."
            )

        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")

        if head_mask is not None:
            logger.warning(
                "SelectiveMamba does not use head_mask (SSM has no heads). Ignoring head_mask."
            )

        mask_bool = None
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 2:
                # Distinguish between int/bool masks and float additive masks
                if mask.dtype in (
                    torch.bool,
                    torch.int8,
                    torch.int16,
                    torch.int32,
                    torch.int64,
                ):
                    mask_bool = mask.bool().to(hidden_states.device)
                else:
                    # Float mask: -inf = invalid, 0 = valid
                    mask_bool = (mask > -1e4).to(hidden_states.device)
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                mask_bool = (mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e4).to(hidden_states.device)
            else:
                try:
                    mask_bool = get_token_valid_mask(
                        mask,
                        B,
                        L,
                        hidden_states.device,
                    )
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("get_token_valid_mask failed: %s", e)
                    mask_bool = None

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        xz = self.in_proj(hidden_states)
        x_in, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)

        conv_w = self.conv1d.weight.to(x_in.dtype)
        conv_b = self.conv1d.bias.to(x_in.dtype) if self.conv1d.bias is not None else None
        
        # FIXED: Apply explicit left padding for causal convolution
        x_pad = F.pad(x_in.transpose(1, 2), (self.d_conv - 1, 0))
        
        x_conv = (
            F.conv1d(
                x_pad,
                conv_w,
                conv_b,
                stride=self.conv1d.stride,
                padding=0,
                dilation=self.conv1d.dilation,
                groups=self.conv1d.groups,
            )[:, :, :L]
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

        # Selective projections: dt_raw [dt_rank], B_t [d_state], C_t [d_state]
        x_proj_out = self.x_proj(x_ssm)
        dt_raw, B_t, C_t = torch.split(
            x_proj_out,
            [self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state],
            dim=-1,
        )
        dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(dt_raw))
        dt = torch.clamp(dt, min=1e-3, max=1.0)

        mt = str(getattr(getattr(self, "config", None), "model_type", "") or "").lower()
        decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        is_decoder = (
            self.is_decoder
            or getattr(getattr(self, "config", None), "is_decoder", False)
            or any(mt.startswith(p) for p in decoder_prefixes)
        )

        y_fwd = self._selective_scan(x_ssm, dt, B_t, C_t, mask=mask_bool, reverse=False)

        if is_decoder:
            y = y_fwd
        else:
            y_bwd = self._selective_scan(x_ssm, dt, B_t, C_t, mask=mask_bool, reverse=True)
            y = 0.5 * (y_fwd + y_bwd)

        gated = (y.to(target_dtype) * F.silu(z.to(target_dtype))).to(target_dtype)

        # Inner SSM output is inner_dim; out_proj brings it back to dim.
        # Always apply out_proj.
        out = self.out_proj(gated).to(target_dtype)

        if mask_bool is not None:
            out = out * mask_bool.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)

        return (out, None)

    def step_recurrent(
        self,
        x_t: torch.Tensor,
        state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
        """
        Single-step streaming inference.

        x_t: (B, 1, D)
        state: dict with keys 'h', 'conv_buf' (optional)
        Returns: (y_t, new_state)
        """
        if x_t.dim() == 2:
            x_t = x_t.unsqueeze(1)
        B, L, D = x_t.shape
        assert L == 1, "step_recurrent expects L=1"

        # Initialize state
        if state is None:
            h = torch.zeros(
                B,
                self.inner_dim,
                self.d_state,
                device=x_t.device,
                dtype=torch.float32,
            )
            conv_buf = torch.zeros(
                B,
                self.d_conv - 1,
                self.inner_dim,
                device=x_t.device,
                dtype=x_t.dtype,
            )
        else:
            h = state["h"]
            conv_buf = state.get("conv_buf")

        # in_proj
        xz = self.in_proj(x_t)  # (B, 1, 2*inner_dim)
        x_in, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)

        # Conv1d with buffer
        x_conv_input = torch.cat([conv_buf, x_in], dim=1)  # (B, d_conv, inner_dim)
        x_conv = F.conv1d(
            x_conv_input.transpose(1, 2),
            self.conv1d.weight,
            self.conv1d.bias,
            groups=self.inner_dim,
        ).transpose(1, 2)  # (B, 1, inner_dim)
        new_conv_buf = x_conv_input[:, -(self.d_conv - 1) :]

        x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

        # Selective params
        x_proj_out = self.x_proj(x_ssm)
        dt_raw, B_t, C_t = torch.split(
            x_proj_out,
            [self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state],
            dim=-1,
        )
        dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(dt_raw)).clamp(1e-3, 1.0)

        # Single step scan
        A = torch.exp(torch.clamp(self.A_log.float(), min=-20.0, max=10.0))
        dA = torch.exp(-dt.unsqueeze(-1) * A.view(1, 1, self.inner_dim, self.d_state))
        dBx = dt.unsqueeze(-1) * B_t.unsqueeze(2) * x_ssm.unsqueeze(-1)

        h = dA.squeeze(1) * h + dBx.squeeze(1)  # (B, inner_dim, d_state)
        y = (C_t.squeeze(1).unsqueeze(1) * h).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, inner_dim)
        y = y + self.D * x_ssm.squeeze(1)

        # Gating
        gated = y * F.silu(z.squeeze(1))  # (B, inner_dim)

        # Output projection
        out = self.out_proj(gated.unsqueeze(1))  # (B, 1, D)

        new_state = {"h": h, "conv_buf": new_conv_buf}
        return out, new_state

Selective Mamba SSM block.

Implements a Mamba-style selective state space model with input-dependent B, C, and dt parameters: h_t = Ā_t · h_{t-1} + B̄_t · x_t y_t = C_t · h_t + D · x_t where Ā_t = exp(dt_t · A).

The "selectivity" comes from x_proj producing B_t and C_t from the input.

NOTE: The previous name "UPI-Stuffed" has been removed because none of the claimed features (Uncertainty Penalty, Interpolation, Stuffing) were actually implemented.

Registered as "upi_mamba" (legacy alias) and "selective_mamba" (canonical).

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
    """Robust weight copying from DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2 Conv1D, or Qwen/LLaMA."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        w_q, _b_q = None, None
        w_k, _b_k = None, None
        w_v, _b_v = None, None
        w_o, b_o = None, None

        src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
        if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
            hasattr(old_attn, "query")
            and not hasattr(old_attn, "dense")
            and not hasattr(old_attn, "out_proj")
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True

        if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
            src = old_attn.self
            if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                w_o = old_attn.output.dense.weight.detach()
                b_o = (
                    old_attn.output.dense.bias.detach()
                    if old_attn.output.dense.bias is not None
                    else None
                )
        else:
            src = old_attn

        # Pattern 1: GPT-2 Fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
            c_attn_w = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
            (src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None)
            if c_attn_w.dim() == 2 and (
                c_attn_w.shape[0] == self.dim and c_attn_w.shape[1] != self.dim
            ):
                c_attn_w = c_attn_w.t()

            split_q = min(self.dim, c_attn_w.shape[0] // 3)
            w_q = c_attn_w[:split_q, :]
            w_k = c_attn_w[split_q : 2 * split_q, :]
            w_v = c_attn_w[2 * split_q : 3 * split_q, :]

            out_w = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
            if out_w.dim() == 2 and (out_w.shape[0] != self.dim and out_w.shape[1] == self.dim):
                out_w = out_w.t()
            w_o = out_w
            b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

        # Pattern 2: RoBERTa / BERT separate query, key, value
        elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
            w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
            src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
            src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
            src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None

            if w_o is None:
                for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                    if hasattr(src, attr):
                        proj = getattr(src, attr)
                        w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                        b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                        break

        # Pattern 3: Qwen / LLaMA
        elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
            w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
            src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
            src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
            src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None

            if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

        # Pattern 4: DistilBERT
        elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
            w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
            src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
            src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
            src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None

            if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

        # Fallback: UniversalAdapter extraction
        if w_q is None or w_v is None or (w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder):
            for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
                try:
                    w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, proj)
                    if w is not None:
                        if proj == "q" and w_q is None:
                            w_q, _b_q = w, b
                        elif proj == "k" and w_k is None:
                            w_k, _b_k = w, b
                        elif proj == "v" and w_v is None:
                            w_v, _b_v = w, b
                        elif proj == "o" and w_o is None:
                            w_o, b_o = w, b
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("UniversalAdapter extraction for %s failed: %s", proj, e)

        missing = []
        if w_q is None:
            missing.append("Q")
        if w_v is None:
            missing.append("V")
        if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
            missing.append("O")
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"SelectiveMamba: cannot extract {missing} from "
                f"{old_attn.__class__.__name__}. Aborting to avoid "
                f"silent model corruption."
            )

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t()
            return w

        def _adapt_to_inner(w: torch.Tensor, name: str) -> torch.Tensor:
            """
            Adapt weight from source head count to inner_dim.
            If source is smaller: tile and truncate.
            If source is larger: slice to fit.
            """
            src_out, src_in = w.shape
            if src_out == self.inner_dim:
                return w

            if src_out > self.inner_dim:
                # Source has more heads — take the first inner_dim
                logger.debug(
                    "SelectiveMamba: slicing %s from %d to %d",
                    name,
                    src_out,
                    self.inner_dim,
                )
                return w[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

            # Source is smaller — repeat to fill
            rep = math.ceil(self.inner_dim / src_out)
            logger.debug(
                "SelectiveMamba: repeating %s from %d to %d (rep=%d)",
                name,
                src_out,
                self.inner_dim,
                rep,
            )
            return w.repeat(rep, 1)[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

        w_v_or = _orient(w_v, self.dim)
        w_q_or = _orient(w_q, self.dim)
        w_v_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_v_or, "W_V")
        w_q_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_q_or, "W_Q")

        v_chunk = w_v_or[:, : self.dim]
        q_chunk = w_q_or[:, : self.dim]

        self.in_proj.weight.data[: self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(v_chunk)
        self.in_proj.weight.data[self.inner_dim : 2 * self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(q_chunk)

        if w_o is not None:
            w_o_or = _orient(w_o, self.inner_dim)
            min_o_out = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[0])
            min_o_in = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[1])
            self.out_proj.weight.data[:min_o_out, :min_o_in].copy_(
                w_o_or[:min_o_out, :min_o_in]
            )
            if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                b_val = b_o.data if isinstance(b_o, nn.Parameter) else b_o
                self.out_proj.bias.data[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                    b_val[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))]
                )

        # Frobenius norm checks
        for name, w in [
            ("in_proj", self.in_proj.weight),
            ("out_proj", self.out_proj.weight),
        ]:
            if torch.norm(w.float(), p="fro").item() < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(f"SelectiveMamba: {name} has near-zero Frobenius norm (< 1e-4)")

        self.in_proj.freeze_base_weights()
        self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()
        self.x_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
        self.dt_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
        self.dt_proj.bias.requires_grad = True

        # Preserve source LoRA adapters
        source_submodules = {
            "in": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q"],
            "out": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense", "out", "c_proj"],
        }
        for name in source_submodules["in"]:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                if (
                    hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                    and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                    and sub.lora_A is not None
                    and sub.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                    src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                    if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_A is not None:
                        r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                        in_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                        self.in_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                            src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                        )
                        self.in_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_B is not None:
                        out_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                        r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                        self.in_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                            src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                        )
                        self.in_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                        self.in_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                    break

        for name in source_submodules["out"]:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                if (
                    hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                    and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                    and sub.lora_A is not None
                    and sub.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                    src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                    if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_A is not None:
                        r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                        in_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                        self.out_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                            src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                        )
                        self.out_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_B is not None:
                        out_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                        r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                        self.out_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                            src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                        )
                        self.out_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                        self.out_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                    break

Robust weight copying from DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2 Conv1D, or Qwen/LLaMA.

def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    *args: Any,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
    if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
        raise NotImplementedError(
            "SelectiveMamba does not support cross-attention. "
            "This module replaces SELF-attention only. "
            "For cross-attention, use a different module."
        )

    B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
    mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")

    if head_mask is not None:
        logger.warning(
            "SelectiveMamba does not use head_mask (SSM has no heads). Ignoring head_mask."
        )

    mask_bool = None
    if mask is not None:
        if mask.dim() == 2:
            # Distinguish between int/bool masks and float additive masks
            if mask.dtype in (
                torch.bool,
                torch.int8,
                torch.int16,
                torch.int32,
                torch.int64,
            ):
                mask_bool = mask.bool().to(hidden_states.device)
            else:
                # Float mask: -inf = invalid, 0 = valid
                mask_bool = (mask > -1e4).to(hidden_states.device)
        elif mask.dim() == 4:
            mask_bool = (mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e4).to(hidden_states.device)
        else:
            try:
                mask_bool = get_token_valid_mask(
                    mask,
                    B,
                    L,
                    hidden_states.device,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("get_token_valid_mask failed: %s", e)
                mask_bool = None

    target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
    xz = self.in_proj(hidden_states)
    x_in, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)

    conv_w = self.conv1d.weight.to(x_in.dtype)
    conv_b = self.conv1d.bias.to(x_in.dtype) if self.conv1d.bias is not None else None
    
    # FIXED: Apply explicit left padding for causal convolution
    x_pad = F.pad(x_in.transpose(1, 2), (self.d_conv - 1, 0))
    
    x_conv = (
        F.conv1d(
            x_pad,
            conv_w,
            conv_b,
            stride=self.conv1d.stride,
            padding=0,
            dilation=self.conv1d.dilation,
            groups=self.conv1d.groups,
        )[:, :, :L]
        .transpose(1, 2)
        .contiguous()
    )
    x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

    # Selective projections: dt_raw [dt_rank], B_t [d_state], C_t [d_state]
    x_proj_out = self.x_proj(x_ssm)
    dt_raw, B_t, C_t = torch.split(
        x_proj_out,
        [self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state],
        dim=-1,
    )
    dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(dt_raw))
    dt = torch.clamp(dt, min=1e-3, max=1.0)

    mt = str(getattr(getattr(self, "config", None), "model_type", "") or "").lower()
    decoder_prefixes = (
        "gpt",
        "qwen",
        "llama",
        "mistral",
        "gemma",
        "phi",
        "opt",
        "bloom",
        "falcon",
        "olmo",
        "mpt",
        "stablelm",
    )
    is_decoder = (
        self.is_decoder
        or getattr(getattr(self, "config", None), "is_decoder", False)
        or any(mt.startswith(p) for p in decoder_prefixes)
    )

    y_fwd = self._selective_scan(x_ssm, dt, B_t, C_t, mask=mask_bool, reverse=False)

    if is_decoder:
        y = y_fwd
    else:
        y_bwd = self._selective_scan(x_ssm, dt, B_t, C_t, mask=mask_bool, reverse=True)
        y = 0.5 * (y_fwd + y_bwd)

    gated = (y.to(target_dtype) * F.silu(z.to(target_dtype))).to(target_dtype)

    # Inner SSM output is inner_dim; out_proj brings it back to dim.
    # Always apply out_proj.
    out = self.out_proj(gated).to(target_dtype)

    if mask_bool is not None:
        out = out * mask_bool.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)

    return (out, None)

Must return (output, attention_weights_or_None).

def step_recurrent(self, x_t: torch.Tensor, state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]
Expand source code 
def step_recurrent(
    self,
    x_t: torch.Tensor,
    state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    """
    Single-step streaming inference.

    x_t: (B, 1, D)
    state: dict with keys 'h', 'conv_buf' (optional)
    Returns: (y_t, new_state)
    """
    if x_t.dim() == 2:
        x_t = x_t.unsqueeze(1)
    B, L, D = x_t.shape
    assert L == 1, "step_recurrent expects L=1"

    # Initialize state
    if state is None:
        h = torch.zeros(
            B,
            self.inner_dim,
            self.d_state,
            device=x_t.device,
            dtype=torch.float32,
        )
        conv_buf = torch.zeros(
            B,
            self.d_conv - 1,
            self.inner_dim,
            device=x_t.device,
            dtype=x_t.dtype,
        )
    else:
        h = state["h"]
        conv_buf = state.get("conv_buf")

    # in_proj
    xz = self.in_proj(x_t)  # (B, 1, 2*inner_dim)
    x_in, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)

    # Conv1d with buffer
    x_conv_input = torch.cat([conv_buf, x_in], dim=1)  # (B, d_conv, inner_dim)
    x_conv = F.conv1d(
        x_conv_input.transpose(1, 2),
        self.conv1d.weight,
        self.conv1d.bias,
        groups=self.inner_dim,
    ).transpose(1, 2)  # (B, 1, inner_dim)
    new_conv_buf = x_conv_input[:, -(self.d_conv - 1) :]

    x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

    # Selective params
    x_proj_out = self.x_proj(x_ssm)
    dt_raw, B_t, C_t = torch.split(
        x_proj_out,
        [self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state],
        dim=-1,
    )
    dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(dt_raw)).clamp(1e-3, 1.0)

    # Single step scan
    A = torch.exp(torch.clamp(self.A_log.float(), min=-20.0, max=10.0))
    dA = torch.exp(-dt.unsqueeze(-1) * A.view(1, 1, self.inner_dim, self.d_state))
    dBx = dt.unsqueeze(-1) * B_t.unsqueeze(2) * x_ssm.unsqueeze(-1)

    h = dA.squeeze(1) * h + dBx.squeeze(1)  # (B, inner_dim, d_state)
    y = (C_t.squeeze(1).unsqueeze(1) * h).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, inner_dim)
    y = y + self.D * x_ssm.squeeze(1)

    # Gating
    gated = y * F.silu(z.squeeze(1))  # (B, inner_dim)

    # Output projection
    out = self.out_proj(gated.unsqueeze(1))  # (B, 1, D)

    new_state = {"h": h, "conv_buf": new_conv_buf}
    return out, new_state
