@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("selective_mamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "upi_mamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True
)  # DEPRECATED alias
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "upi_stuffed_mamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True
)  # DEPRECATED alias
class SelectiveMamba(BaseAttention):
    r"""
    Selective Mamba SSM block.

    Implements a Mamba-style selective state space model with
    input-dependent B, C, and dt parameters:
        h_t = Ā_t · h_{t-1} + B̄_t · x_t
        y_t = C_t · h_t + D · x_t
    where Ā_t = exp(dt_t · A).

    The "selectivity" comes from x_proj producing B_t and C_t
    from the input.

    NOTE: The previous name "UPI-Stuffed" has been removed
    because none of the claimed features (Uncertainty Penalty,
    Interpolation, Stuffing) were actually implemented.

    Registered as "upi_mamba" (legacy alias) and
    "selective_mamba" (canonical).
    """

    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        d_model: int | Any | None = None,
        dim: int | None = None,
        d_state: int = 16,
        d_conv: int = 4,
        expand: int = 1,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        src_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("old_attn")

        if dim is None and d_model is None:
            if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
                dim = _cfg_get(
                    config,
                    "hidden_size",
                    _cfg_get(config, "dim", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)),
                )
            else:
                dim = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
        else:
            dim = dim or d_model

        if hasattr(dim, "hidden_size"):
            dim = dim.hidden_size

        self.dim: int = int(dim)
        self.d_state: int = d_state
        self.d_conv: int = d_conv
        self.expand: int = expand
        self.inner_dim: int = self.dim * expand
        self.lora_rank = kwargs.get("lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", lora_rank))
        self.lora_alpha = kwargs.get("lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))

        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder: bool = is_decoder
        elif config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            self.is_decoder = bool(getattr(config, "is_decoder", False))
        else:
            self.is_decoder = False

        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        self.in_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.inner_dim * 2,
            bias=False,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(
            self.inner_dim,
            self.inner_dim,
            kernel_size=d_conv,
            groups=self.inner_dim,
            padding=0,
        )
        # A_log init: log([1, 2, ..., d_state]) repeated.
        # In our scan, we compute A = +exp(A_log) (positive),
        # then log_decay = -dt * A (negative), giving decay < 1.
        # This is mathematically equivalent to Mamba's
        # A = -exp(A_log), decay = exp(dt * A).
        A_init = torch.arange(1, d_state + 1, dtype=torch.float32).repeat(self.inner_dim, 1)
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(A_init))

        # Trainable skip connection D matching inner_dim
        self.D = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.inner_dim))

        # Mamba paper: dt_rank = ceil(d_model / 16), not d_inner
        self.dt_rank = math.ceil(self.dim / 16)

        # x_proj maps inner_dim → dt_rank + 2*d_state
        self.x_proj = nn.Linear(
            self.inner_dim,
            self.dt_rank + 2 * self.d_state,
            bias=False,
        )
        self.x_proj.weight.requires_grad = True

        # dt_proj maps dt_rank → inner_dim
        self.dt_proj = nn.Linear(self.dt_rank, self.inner_dim, bias=True)

        # Initialize dt_proj bias (Mamba paper Eq. 12)
        dt_min, dt_max = 0.001, 0.1
        dt_init = torch.exp(
            torch.rand(self.inner_dim) * (math.log(dt_max) - math.log(dt_min)) + math.log(dt_min)
        )
        dt_init_inv = dt_init + torch.log(-torch.expm1(-dt_init))
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.dt_proj.bias.copy_(dt_init_inv)
            # Weight init: uniform(-1/sqrt(dt_rank), 1/sqrt(dt_rank))
            bound = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.dt_rank)
            nn.init.uniform_(self.dt_proj.weight, -bound, bound)
        self.dt_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
        self.dt_proj.bias.requires_grad = True

        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.inner_dim,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        if src_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src_attn)

    @property
    def query(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def key(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def value(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.in_proj

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        """Robust weight copying from DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2 Conv1D, or Qwen/LLaMA."""
        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q, _b_q = None, None
            w_k, _b_k = None, None
            w_v, _b_v = None, None
            w_o, b_o = None, None

            src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
            if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
                hasattr(old_attn, "query")
                and not hasattr(old_attn, "dense")
                and not hasattr(old_attn, "out_proj")
            ):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True

            if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
                src = old_attn.self
                if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                    w_o = old_attn.output.dense.weight.detach()
                    b_o = (
                        old_attn.output.dense.bias.detach()
                        if old_attn.output.dense.bias is not None
                        else None
                    )
            else:
                src = old_attn

            # Pattern 1: GPT-2 Fused c_attn
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                c_attn_w = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
                (src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None)
                if c_attn_w.dim() == 2 and (
                    c_attn_w.shape[0] == self.dim and c_attn_w.shape[1] != self.dim
                ):
                    c_attn_w = c_attn_w.t()

                split_q = min(self.dim, c_attn_w.shape[0] // 3)
                w_q = c_attn_w[:split_q, :]
                w_k = c_attn_w[split_q : 2 * split_q, :]
                w_v = c_attn_w[2 * split_q : 3 * split_q, :]

                out_w = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
                if out_w.dim() == 2 and (out_w.shape[0] != self.dim and out_w.shape[1] == self.dim):
                    out_w = out_w.t()
                w_o = out_w
                b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

            # Pattern 2: RoBERTa / BERT separate query, key, value
            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
                src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
                src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
                src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None

                if w_o is None:
                    for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, attr):
                            proj = getattr(src, attr)
                            w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                            b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                            break

            # Pattern 3: Qwen / LLaMA
            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
                src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
                src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
                src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None

                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

            # Pattern 4: DistilBERT
            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
                src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
                src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
                src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None

                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

            # Fallback: UniversalAdapter extraction
            if w_q is None or w_v is None or (w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder):
                for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
                    try:
                        w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, proj)
                        if w is not None:
                            if proj == "q" and w_q is None:
                                w_q, _b_q = w, b
                            elif proj == "k" and w_k is None:
                                w_k, _b_k = w, b
                            elif proj == "v" and w_v is None:
                                w_v, _b_v = w, b
                            elif proj == "o" and w_o is None:
                                w_o, b_o = w, b
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug("UniversalAdapter extraction for %s failed: %s", proj, e)

            missing = []
            if w_q is None:
                missing.append("Q")
            if w_v is None:
                missing.append("V")
            if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
                missing.append("O")
            if missing:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"SelectiveMamba: cannot extract {missing} from "
                    f"{old_attn.__class__.__name__}. Aborting to avoid "
                    f"silent model corruption."
                )

            def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
                if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                    return w.t()
                return w

            def _adapt_to_inner(w: torch.Tensor, name: str) -> torch.Tensor:
                """
                Adapt weight from source head count to inner_dim.
                If source is smaller: tile and truncate.
                If source is larger: slice to fit.
                """
                src_out, src_in = w.shape
                if src_out == self.inner_dim:
                    return w

                if src_out > self.inner_dim:
                    # Source has more heads — take the first inner_dim
                    logger.debug(
                        "SelectiveMamba: slicing %s from %d to %d",
                        name,
                        src_out,
                        self.inner_dim,
                    )
                    return w[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

                # Source is smaller — repeat to fill
                rep = math.ceil(self.inner_dim / src_out)
                logger.debug(
                    "SelectiveMamba: repeating %s from %d to %d (rep=%d)",
                    name,
                    src_out,
                    self.inner_dim,
                    rep,
                )
                return w.repeat(rep, 1)[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

            w_v_or = _orient(w_v, self.dim)
            w_q_or = _orient(w_q, self.dim)
            w_v_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_v_or, "W_V")
            w_q_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_q_or, "W_Q")

            v_chunk = w_v_or[:, : self.dim]
            q_chunk = w_q_or[:, : self.dim]

            self.in_proj.weight.data[: self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(v_chunk)
            self.in_proj.weight.data[self.inner_dim : 2 * self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(q_chunk)

            if w_o is not None:
                w_o_or = _orient(w_o, self.inner_dim)
                min_o_out = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[0])
                min_o_in = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[1])
                self.out_proj.weight.data[:min_o_out, :min_o_in].copy_(
                    w_o_or[:min_o_out, :min_o_in]
                )
                if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    b_val = b_o.data if isinstance(b_o, nn.Parameter) else b_o
                    self.out_proj.bias.data[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                        b_val[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))]
                    )

            # Frobenius norm checks
            for name, w in [
                ("in_proj", self.in_proj.weight),
                ("out_proj", self.out_proj.weight),
            ]:
                if torch.norm(w.float(), p="fro").item() < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(f"SelectiveMamba: {name} has near-zero Frobenius norm (< 1e-4)")

            self.in_proj.freeze_base_weights()
            self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()
            self.x_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
            self.dt_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
            self.dt_proj.bias.requires_grad = True

            # Preserve source LoRA adapters
            source_submodules = {
                "in": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q"],
                "out": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense", "out", "c_proj"],
            }
            for name in source_submodules["in"]:
                if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                    sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                    if (
                        hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                        and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                        and sub.lora_A is not None
                        and sub.lora_B is not None
                    ):
                        src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                        src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                        if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_A is not None:
                            r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                            in_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                            self.in_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                                src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                            )
                            self.in_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_B is not None:
                            out_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                            r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                            self.in_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                                src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                            )
                            self.in_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                            self.in_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                        break

            for name in source_submodules["out"]:
                if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                    sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                    if (
                        hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                        and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                        and sub.lora_A is not None
                        and sub.lora_B is not None
                    ):
                        src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                        src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                        if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_A is not None:
                            r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                            in_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                            self.out_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                                src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                            )
                            self.out_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_B is not None:
                            out_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                            r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                            self.out_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                                src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                            )
                            self.out_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                        if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                            self.out_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                        break

    def _selective_scan(
        self,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        dt: torch.Tensor,
        B_t: torch.Tensor,
        C_t: torch.Tensor,
        mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        reverse: bool = False,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Bounded chunked associative SSM scan in FP32 with O(chunk=64) memory."""
        if reverse:
            x = torch.flip(x, dims=[1])
            dt = torch.flip(dt, dims=[1])
            B_t = torch.flip(B_t, dims=[1])
            C_t = torch.flip(C_t, dims=[1])
            if mask is not None:
                mask = torch.flip(mask, dims=[1])

        batch, seqlen, _ = x.shape
        A = torch.exp(torch.clamp(self.A_log.float(), min=-20.0, max=10.0))  # [inner_dim, d_state]

        # Path 1: Hardware-fused Triton selective scan on CUDA
        if HAS_TRITON_UPI and triton_selective_scan is not None and x.is_cuda and mask is None:
            try:
                return triton_selective_scan(
                    x.contiguous(),
                    dt.contiguous(),
                    -A.contiguous(),
                    B_t.contiguous(),
                    C_t.contiguous(),
                    None,
                    delta_softplus=False,
                ).to(x.dtype)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Triton selective scan failed, falling back to chunked PyTorch: %s", e)

        # In case of padding mask, zero out invalid token inputs
        if mask is not None:
            m = mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
            x = x * m
            dt = dt * m

        chunk_size = 64
        num_chunks = (seqlen + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size
        h_running = x.new_zeros(batch, self.inner_dim, self.d_state, dtype=torch.float32)
        y_chunks = []
        A_view = A.view(1, 1, self.inner_dim, self.d_state)

        for c in range(num_chunks):
            s_idx = c * chunk_size
            e_idx = min(seqlen, s_idx + chunk_size)

            dt_c = dt[:, s_idx:e_idx]
            x_c = x[:, s_idx:e_idx]
            B_c = B_t[:, s_idx:e_idx]
            C_c = C_t[:, s_idx:e_idx]

            # Compute u and log_a strictly for current 64-token chunk to avoid buffer overflow
            log_a_c = -(dt_c.unsqueeze(-1) * A_view).float()
            u_c = (dt_c.unsqueeze(-1) * B_c.unsqueeze(2) * x_c.unsqueeze(-1)).float()

            lambda_c = torch.clamp(torch.cumsum(log_a_c, dim=1), min=-25.0, max=0.0)

            decay_c = torch.exp(lambda_c)
            decay_inv_c = torch.exp(-lambda_c).clamp(max=1e10)

            h_intra = decay_c * torch.cumsum(decay_inv_c * u_c, dim=1)
            h_inter = decay_c * h_running.unsqueeze(1)

            h_chunk = torch.clamp(h_intra + h_inter, min=-1e4, max=1e4)
            y_c = (C_c.unsqueeze(2).float() * h_chunk).sum(dim=-1).to(x.dtype)
            y_chunks.append(y_c)
            h_running = h_chunk[:, -1]

        y = torch.cat(y_chunks, dim=1)
        y = (y + self.D.to(x.dtype) * x).to(x.dtype)

        if reverse:
            y = torch.flip(y, dims=[1])

        return y

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError(
                "SelectiveMamba does not support cross-attention. "
                "This module replaces SELF-attention only. "
                "For cross-attention, use a different module."
            )

        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")

        if head_mask is not None:
            logger.warning(
                "SelectiveMamba does not use head_mask (SSM has no heads). Ignoring head_mask."
            )

        mask_bool = None
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 2:
                # Distinguish between int/bool masks and float additive masks
                if mask.dtype in (
                    torch.bool,
                    torch.int8,
                    torch.int16,
                    torch.int32,
                    torch.int64,
                ):
                    mask_bool = mask.bool().to(hidden_states.device)
                else:
                    # Float mask: -inf = invalid, 0 = valid
                    mask_bool = (mask > -1e4).to(hidden_states.device)
            elif mask.dim() == 4:
                mask_bool = (mask[:, 0, 0, :] > -1e4).to(hidden_states.device)
            else:
                try:
                    mask_bool = get_token_valid_mask(
                        mask,
                        B,
                        L,
                        hidden_states.device,
                    )
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("get_token_valid_mask failed: %s", e)
                    mask_bool = None

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        xz = self.in_proj(hidden_states)
        x_in, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)

        conv_w = self.conv1d.weight.to(x_in.dtype)
        conv_b = self.conv1d.bias.to(x_in.dtype) if self.conv1d.bias is not None else None
        
        # FIXED: Apply explicit left padding for causal convolution
        x_pad = F.pad(x_in.transpose(1, 2), (self.d_conv - 1, 0))
        
        x_conv = (
            F.conv1d(
                x_pad,
                conv_w,
                conv_b,
                stride=self.conv1d.stride,
                padding=0,
                dilation=self.conv1d.dilation,
                groups=self.conv1d.groups,
            )[:, :, :L]
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

        # Selective projections: dt_raw [dt_rank], B_t [d_state], C_t [d_state]
        x_proj_out = self.x_proj(x_ssm)
        dt_raw, B_t, C_t = torch.split(
            x_proj_out,
            [self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state],
            dim=-1,
        )
        dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(dt_raw))
        dt = torch.clamp(dt, min=1e-3, max=1.0)

        mt = str(getattr(getattr(self, "config", None), "model_type", "") or "").lower()
        decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        is_decoder = (
            self.is_decoder
            or getattr(getattr(self, "config", None), "is_decoder", False)
            or any(mt.startswith(p) for p in decoder_prefixes)
        )

        y_fwd = self._selective_scan(x_ssm, dt, B_t, C_t, mask=mask_bool, reverse=False)

        if is_decoder:
            y = y_fwd
        else:
            y_bwd = self._selective_scan(x_ssm, dt, B_t, C_t, mask=mask_bool, reverse=True)
            y = 0.5 * (y_fwd + y_bwd)

        gated = (y.to(target_dtype) * F.silu(z.to(target_dtype))).to(target_dtype)

        # Inner SSM output is inner_dim; out_proj brings it back to dim.
        # Always apply out_proj.
        out = self.out_proj(gated).to(target_dtype)

        if mask_bool is not None:
            out = out * mask_bool.unsqueeze(-1).to(out.dtype)

        return (out, None)

    def step_recurrent(
        self,
        x_t: torch.Tensor,
        state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
        """
        Single-step streaming inference.

        x_t: (B, 1, D)
        state: dict with keys 'h', 'conv_buf' (optional)
        Returns: (y_t, new_state)
        """
        if x_t.dim() == 2:
            x_t = x_t.unsqueeze(1)
        B, L, D = x_t.shape
        assert L == 1, "step_recurrent expects L=1"

        # Initialize state
        if state is None:
            h = torch.zeros(
                B,
                self.inner_dim,
                self.d_state,
                device=x_t.device,
                dtype=torch.float32,
            )
            conv_buf = torch.zeros(
                B,
                self.d_conv - 1,
                self.inner_dim,
                device=x_t.device,
                dtype=x_t.dtype,
            )
        else:
            h = state["h"]
            conv_buf = state.get("conv_buf")

        # in_proj
        xz = self.in_proj(x_t)  # (B, 1, 2*inner_dim)
        x_in, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)

        # Conv1d with buffer
        x_conv_input = torch.cat([conv_buf, x_in], dim=1)  # (B, d_conv, inner_dim)
        x_conv = F.conv1d(
            x_conv_input.transpose(1, 2),
            self.conv1d.weight,
            self.conv1d.bias,
            groups=self.inner_dim,
        ).transpose(1, 2)  # (B, 1, inner_dim)
        new_conv_buf = x_conv_input[:, -(self.d_conv - 1) :]

        x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

        # Selective params
        x_proj_out = self.x_proj(x_ssm)
        dt_raw, B_t, C_t = torch.split(
            x_proj_out,
            [self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state],
            dim=-1,
        )
        dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(dt_raw)).clamp(1e-3, 1.0)

        # Single step scan
        A = torch.exp(torch.clamp(self.A_log.float(), min=-20.0, max=10.0))
        dA = torch.exp(-dt.unsqueeze(-1) * A.view(1, 1, self.inner_dim, self.d_state))
        dBx = dt.unsqueeze(-1) * B_t.unsqueeze(2) * x_ssm.unsqueeze(-1)

        h = dA.squeeze(1) * h + dBx.squeeze(1)  # (B, inner_dim, d_state)
        y = (C_t.squeeze(1).unsqueeze(1) * h).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, inner_dim)
        y = y + self.D * x_ssm.squeeze(1)

        # Gating
        gated = y * F.silu(z.squeeze(1))  # (B, inner_dim)

        # Output projection
        out = self.out_proj(gated.unsqueeze(1))  # (B, 1, D)

        new_state = {"h": h, "conv_buf": new_conv_buf}
        return out, new_state

Selective Mamba SSM block.

Implements a Mamba-style selective state space model with input-dependent B, C, and dt parameters: h_t = Ā_t · h_{t-1} + B̄_t · x_t y_t = C_t · h_t + D · x_t where Ā_t = exp(dt_t · A).

The "selectivity" comes from x_proj producing B_t and C_t from the input.

NOTE: The previous name "UPI-Stuffed" has been removed because none of the claimed features (Uncertainty Penalty, Interpolation, Stuffing) were actually implemented.

Registered as "upi_mamba" (legacy alias) and "selective_mamba" (canonical).

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def copy_weights(self,
old_attn: Any) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
    """Robust weight copying from DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2 Conv1D, or Qwen/LLaMA."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        w_q, _b_q = None, None
        w_k, _b_k = None, None
        w_v, _b_v = None, None
        w_o, b_o = None, None

        src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
        if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
            hasattr(old_attn, "query")
            and not hasattr(old_attn, "dense")
            and not hasattr(old_attn, "out_proj")
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True

        if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True
            src = old_attn.self
            if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                w_o = old_attn.output.dense.weight.detach()
                b_o = (
                    old_attn.output.dense.bias.detach()
                    if old_attn.output.dense.bias is not None
                    else None
                )
        else:
            src = old_attn

        # Pattern 1: GPT-2 Fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
            c_attn_w = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
            (src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None)
            if c_attn_w.dim() == 2 and (
                c_attn_w.shape[0] == self.dim and c_attn_w.shape[1] != self.dim
            ):
                c_attn_w = c_attn_w.t()

            split_q = min(self.dim, c_attn_w.shape[0] // 3)
            w_q = c_attn_w[:split_q, :]
            w_k = c_attn_w[split_q : 2 * split_q, :]
            w_v = c_attn_w[2 * split_q : 3 * split_q, :]

            out_w = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
            if out_w.dim() == 2 and (out_w.shape[0] != self.dim and out_w.shape[1] == self.dim):
                out_w = out_w.t()
            w_o = out_w
            b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

        # Pattern 2: RoBERTa / BERT separate query, key, value
        elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
            w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
            src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
            src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
            src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None

            if w_o is None:
                for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                    if hasattr(src, attr):
                        proj = getattr(src, attr)
                        w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                        b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                        break

        # Pattern 3: Qwen / LLaMA
        elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
            w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
            src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
            src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
            src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None

            if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

        # Pattern 4: DistilBERT
        elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
            w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
            src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
            src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
            w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
            src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None

            if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

        # Fallback: UniversalAdapter extraction
        if w_q is None or w_v is None or (w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder):
            for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
                try:
                    w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, proj)
                    if w is not None:
                        if proj == "q" and w_q is None:
                            w_q, _b_q = w, b
                        elif proj == "k" and w_k is None:
                            w_k, _b_k = w, b
                        elif proj == "v" and w_v is None:
                            w_v, _b_v = w, b
                        elif proj == "o" and w_o is None:
                            w_o, b_o = w, b
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("UniversalAdapter extraction for %s failed: %s", proj, e)

        missing = []
        if w_q is None:
            missing.append("Q")
        if w_v is None:
            missing.append("V")
        if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
            missing.append("O")
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"SelectiveMamba: cannot extract {missing} from "
                f"{old_attn.__class__.__name__}. Aborting to avoid "
                f"silent model corruption."
            )

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t()
            return w

        def _adapt_to_inner(w: torch.Tensor, name: str) -> torch.Tensor:
            """
            Adapt weight from source head count to inner_dim.
            If source is smaller: tile and truncate.
            If source is larger: slice to fit.
            """
            src_out, src_in = w.shape
            if src_out == self.inner_dim:
                return w

            if src_out > self.inner_dim:
                # Source has more heads — take the first inner_dim
                logger.debug(
                    "SelectiveMamba: slicing %s from %d to %d",
                    name,
                    src_out,
                    self.inner_dim,
                )
                return w[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

            # Source is smaller — repeat to fill
            rep = math.ceil(self.inner_dim / src_out)
            logger.debug(
                "SelectiveMamba: repeating %s from %d to %d (rep=%d)",
                name,
                src_out,
                self.inner_dim,
                rep,
            )
            return w.repeat(rep, 1)[: self.inner_dim, :].contiguous()

        w_v_or = _orient(w_v, self.dim)
        w_q_or = _orient(w_q, self.dim)
        w_v_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_v_or, "W_V")
        w_q_or = _adapt_to_inner(w_q_or, "W_Q")

        v_chunk = w_v_or[:, : self.dim]
        q_chunk = w_q_or[:, : self.dim]

        self.in_proj.weight.data[: self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(v_chunk)
        self.in_proj.weight.data[self.inner_dim : 2 * self.inner_dim, : self.dim].copy_(q_chunk)

        if w_o is not None:
            w_o_or = _orient(w_o, self.inner_dim)
            min_o_out = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[0])
            min_o_in = min(self.dim, w_o_or.shape[1])
            self.out_proj.weight.data[:min_o_out, :min_o_in].copy_(
                w_o_or[:min_o_out, :min_o_in]
            )
            if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                b_val = b_o.data if isinstance(b_o, nn.Parameter) else b_o
                self.out_proj.bias.data[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                    b_val[: min(len(self.out_proj.bias), len(b_val))]
                )

        # Frobenius norm checks
        for name, w in [
            ("in_proj", self.in_proj.weight),
            ("out_proj", self.out_proj.weight),
        ]:
            if torch.norm(w.float(), p="fro").item() < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(f"SelectiveMamba: {name} has near-zero Frobenius norm (< 1e-4)")

        self.in_proj.freeze_base_weights()
        self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()
        self.x_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
        self.dt_proj.weight.requires_grad = True
        self.dt_proj.bias.requires_grad = True

        # Preserve source LoRA adapters
        source_submodules = {
            "in": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q"],
            "out": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense", "out", "c_proj"],
        }
        for name in source_submodules["in"]:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                if (
                    hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                    and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                    and sub.lora_A is not None
                    and sub.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                    src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                    if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_A is not None:
                        r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                        in_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                        self.in_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                            src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                        )
                        self.in_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.in_proj.lora_B is not None:
                        out_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                        r_min = min(self.in_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                        self.in_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                            src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                        )
                        self.in_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                        self.in_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                    break

        for name in source_submodules["out"]:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                sub = getattr(old_attn, name)
                if (
                    hasattr(sub, "lora_A")
                    and hasattr(sub, "lora_B")
                    and sub.lora_A is not None
                    and sub.lora_B is not None
                ):
                    src_lA = getattr(sub.lora_A, "weight", sub.lora_A)
                    src_lB = getattr(sub.lora_B, "weight", sub.lora_B)
                    if hasattr(src_lA, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_A is not None:
                        r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[0], src_lA.shape[0])
                        in_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_A.shape[1], src_lA.shape[1])
                        self.out_proj.lora_A.data[:r_min, :in_min].copy_(
                            src_lA.data[:r_min, :in_min]
                        )
                        self.out_proj.lora_A.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(src_lB, "data") and self.out_proj.lora_B is not None:
                        out_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[0], src_lB.shape[0])
                        r_min = min(self.out_proj.lora_B.shape[1], src_lB.shape[1])
                        self.out_proj.lora_B.data[:out_min, :r_min].copy_(
                            src_lB.data[:out_min, :r_min]
                        )
                        self.out_proj.lora_B.requires_grad = True
                    if hasattr(sub, "lora_scaling"):
                        self.out_proj.scaling = sub.lora_scaling
                    break

Robust weight copying from DistilBERT, RoBERTa, GPT-2 Conv1D, or Qwen/LLaMA.

def step_recurrent(self, x_t: torch.Tensor, state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]
Expand source code 
def step_recurrent(
    self,
    x_t: torch.Tensor,
    state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    """
    Single-step streaming inference.

    x_t: (B, 1, D)
    state: dict with keys 'h', 'conv_buf' (optional)
    Returns: (y_t, new_state)
    """
    if x_t.dim() == 2:
        x_t = x_t.unsqueeze(1)
    B, L, D = x_t.shape
    assert L == 1, "step_recurrent expects L=1"

    # Initialize state
    if state is None:
        h = torch.zeros(
            B,
            self.inner_dim,
            self.d_state,
            device=x_t.device,
            dtype=torch.float32,
        )
        conv_buf = torch.zeros(
            B,
            self.d_conv - 1,
            self.inner_dim,
            device=x_t.device,
            dtype=x_t.dtype,
        )
    else:
        h = state["h"]
        conv_buf = state.get("conv_buf")

    # in_proj
    xz = self.in_proj(x_t)  # (B, 1, 2*inner_dim)
    x_in, z = xz.chunk(2, dim=-1)

    # Conv1d with buffer
    x_conv_input = torch.cat([conv_buf, x_in], dim=1)  # (B, d_conv, inner_dim)
    x_conv = F.conv1d(
        x_conv_input.transpose(1, 2),
        self.conv1d.weight,
        self.conv1d.bias,
        groups=self.inner_dim,
    ).transpose(1, 2)  # (B, 1, inner_dim)
    new_conv_buf = x_conv_input[:, -(self.d_conv - 1) :]

    x_ssm = F.silu(x_conv)

    # Selective params
    x_proj_out = self.x_proj(x_ssm)
    dt_raw, B_t, C_t = torch.split(
        x_proj_out,
        [self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state],
        dim=-1,
    )
    dt = F.softplus(self.dt_proj(dt_raw)).clamp(1e-3, 1.0)

    # Single step scan
    A = torch.exp(torch.clamp(self.A_log.float(), min=-20.0, max=10.0))
    dA = torch.exp(-dt.unsqueeze(-1) * A.view(1, 1, self.inner_dim, self.d_state))
    dBx = dt.unsqueeze(-1) * B_t.unsqueeze(2) * x_ssm.unsqueeze(-1)

    h = dA.squeeze(1) * h + dBx.squeeze(1)  # (B, inner_dim, d_state)
    y = (C_t.squeeze(1).unsqueeze(1) * h).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, inner_dim)
    y = y + self.D * x_ssm.squeeze(1)

    # Gating
    gated = y * F.silu(z.squeeze(1))  # (B, inner_dim)

    # Output projection
    out = self.out_proj(gated.unsqueeze(1))  # (B, 1, D)

    new_state = {"h": h, "conv_buf": new_conv_buf}
    return out, new_state

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("simplessm")
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("selective_ssm")
class SelectiveSSM(BaseAttention):
    r"""
    Faithful Mamba-style selective SSM block with hardened execution paths.
    """

    supports_causal: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    triton_kernel: str = "state_update"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        d_state: int = 16,
        d_conv: int = 4,
        expand: int = 2,
        dt_rank: int | None = None,
        conv_bias: bool = True,
        bias: bool = False,
        bidirectional: bool = False,
        lora_rank: int = 0,
        weights_init: str = "mamba",
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        if d_model is not None:
            self.d_model = int(d_model)
        else:
            self.d_model = int(_cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)))

        self.d_state = int(_cfg_get(config, "d_state", kwargs.get("d_state", d_state)))
        self.d_conv = int(_cfg_get(config, "d_conv", kwargs.get("d_conv", d_conv)))
        self.expand = int(_cfg_get(config, "expand", kwargs.get("expand", expand)))
        self.d_inner = int(self.expand * self.d_model)
        self.dt_rank = math.ceil(self.d_model / 16) if dt_rank is None else int(dt_rank)
        self.bidirectional = bool(
            _cfg_get(config, "bidirectional", kwargs.get("bidirectional", bidirectional))
        )
        self.weights_init = weights_init

        # Input projection: x -> (x_ssm, z)
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(self.d_model, self.d_inner * 2, bias=bias)

        # Depthwise 1D conv
        self.conv1d = nn.Conv1d(
            self.d_inner,
            self.d_inner,
            kernel_size=self.d_conv,
            groups=self.d_inner,
            padding=self.d_conv - 1,
            bias=conv_bias,
        )

        # Delta, B, C projections from x_ssm
        self.x_proj = nn.Linear(self.d_inner, self.dt_rank + 2 * self.d_state, bias=False)

        # Delta projection from dt_rank -> d_inner
        self.dt_proj = nn.Linear(self.dt_rank, self.d_inner, bias=True)

        # A parameterization: A = -exp(A_log)
        A = torch.arange(1, self.d_state + 1, dtype=torch.float32).repeat(self.d_inner, 1)
        self.A_log = nn.Parameter(torch.log(A))

        # D skip connection
        self.D = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.d_inner))

        # Output projection
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(self.d_inner, self.d_model, bias=bias)

        # Bidirectional components if requested
        if self.bidirectional:
            self.conv1d_bwd = nn.Conv1d(
                self.d_inner,
                self.d_inner,
                kernel_size=self.d_conv,
                groups=self.d_inner,
                padding=self.d_conv - 1,
                bias=conv_bias,
            )
            self.x_proj_bwd = nn.Linear(self.d_inner, self.dt_rank + 2 * self.d_state, bias=False)
            self.dt_proj_bwd = nn.Linear(self.dt_rank, self.d_inner, bias=True)
            A_bwd = torch.arange(1, self.d_state + 1, dtype=torch.float32).repeat(self.d_inner, 1)
            self.A_log_bwd = nn.Parameter(torch.log(A_bwd))
            self.D_bwd = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(self.d_inner))
            self.out_proj_bwd = nn.Linear(self.d_inner, self.d_model, bias=bias)
        
        self.triton_kwargs = {}
        self._reset_parameters()

    def _reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        if self.weights_init == "mamba":
            # Delta bias init: softplus^-1(Uniform(0.001, 0.1))
            dt_min, dt_max = 0.001, 0.1
            dt_init = torch.exp(
                torch.rand(self.d_inner) * (math.log(dt_max) - math.log(dt_min)) + math.log(dt_min)
            )
            dt_init_inv = dt_init + torch.log(-torch.expm1(-dt_init))
            with torch.no_grad():
                self.dt_proj.bias.copy_(dt_init_inv)
                self.dt_proj.weight.copy_(
                    torch.ones(self.d_inner, self.dt_rank) * (0.5 / self.dt_rank) ** 0.5
                )
                if self.bidirectional:
                    self.dt_proj_bwd.bias.copy_(dt_init_inv)
                    self.dt_proj_bwd.weight.copy_(
                        torch.ones(self.d_inner, self.dt_rank) * (0.5 / self.dt_rank) ** 0.5
                    )
        elif self.weights_init == "xavier":
            nn.init.xavier_uniform_(self.in_proj.weight)
            nn.init.xavier_uniform_(self.out_proj.weight)
            nn.init.xavier_uniform_(self.x_proj.weight)

    def _forward_core(
        self,
        x_ssm: torch.Tensor,
        z: torch.Tensor,
        conv1d_layer: nn.Module,
        x_proj_layer: nn.Module,
        dt_proj_layer: nn.Module,
        A_log_param: nn.Parameter,
        D_param: nn.Parameter,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, L, _ = x_ssm.shape

        # FIX: Ensure transposed inputs to Conv1D are strictly contiguous
        x_ssm_t = x_ssm.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        x_conv = conv1d_layer(x_ssm_t)[..., :L].transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        x_conv = F.silu(x_conv)

        # Selective projections
        x_dbl = x_proj_layer(x_conv)  # (B, L, dt_rank + 2 * d_state)
        dt, B_ssm, C_ssm = x_dbl.split([self.dt_rank, self.d_state, self.d_state], dim=-1)
        dt = dt_proj_layer(dt)  # (B, L, d_inner)
        dt = F.softplus(dt)

        A = -torch.exp(A_log_param.float())  # (d_inner, d_state)

        # FIX: Force strict contiguous memory layout for all inputs to Triton/CUDA kernel
        y = selective_scan(
            u=x_conv.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
            delta=dt.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
            A=A,
            B=B_ssm.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
            C=C_ssm.transpose(1, 2).contiguous(),
            D=D_param,
        )  # (B, d_inner, L)
        y = y.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()  # (B, L, d_inner)

        # Gating
        target_dtype = x_ssm.dtype
        y = (y.to(target_dtype) * F.silu(z.to(target_dtype)))
        return y

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("SelectiveSSM does not support cross-attention.")

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape

        # Project directly into SSM and gating paths without duplicating LayerNorm
        x_proj = self.in_proj(hidden_states).to(target_dtype)
        x_ssm, z = x_proj.chunk(2, dim=-1)

        # FIX: Prevent State Poisoning from Padded Tokens
        # Zeros out embeddings where padding exists so the state ignores them entirely
        if attention_mask is not None:
            valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, hidden_states.device)
            if valid_mask is not None:
                mask_float = valid_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(target_dtype)
                x_ssm = x_ssm * mask_float
                z = z * mask_float

        y_fwd = self._forward_core(
            x_ssm=x_ssm,
            z=z,
            conv1d_layer=self.conv1d,
            x_proj_layer=self.x_proj,
            dt_proj_layer=self.dt_proj,
            A_log_param=self.A_log,
            D_param=self.D,
        )
        out = self.out_proj(y_fwd.to(target_dtype))

        if self.bidirectional:
            # FIX: Memory layouts are immediately made contiguous after any flip operations
            x_ssm_bwd = torch.flip(x_ssm, [1]).contiguous()
            z_bwd = torch.flip(z, [1]).contiguous()
            
            y_bwd = self._forward_core(
                x_ssm=x_ssm_bwd,
                z=z_bwd,
                conv1d_layer=self.conv1d_bwd,
                x_proj_layer=self.x_proj_bwd,
                dt_proj_layer=self.dt_proj_bwd,
                A_log_param=self.A_log_bwd,
                D_param=self.D_bwd,
            )
            
            y_bwd = torch.flip(y_bwd, [1]).contiguous()
            out = out + self.out_proj_bwd(y_bwd.to(target_dtype))

        return (out, None)

Faithful Mamba-style selective SSM block with hardened execution paths.

class SimpleSSM (config: Any | None = None,

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("simple_ssm")
class SimpleSSM(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    triton_kernel: str = "state_update"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        num_heads: int | None = None,
        num_kv_heads: int | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.config = config

        def _get(k: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
            for obj in (config, getattr(config, "compression_config", None)):
                if obj is not None:
                    if isinstance(obj, dict) and k in obj and obj[k] is not None:
                        return obj[k]
                    elif hasattr(obj, k):
                        val = getattr(obj, k)
                        if val is not None:
                            return val
            return kwargs.get(k, default)

        dim = d_model or _get("hidden_size") or _get("d_model") or _get("dim") or 64
        self.dim = int(dim)

        heads = num_heads or _get("num_attention_heads") or _get("num_heads") or _get("n_head") or 4
        self.num_heads = int(heads)

        kv_heads = (
            num_kv_heads or _get("num_key_value_heads") or _get("num_kv_heads") or self.num_heads
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(kv_heads)
        self.head_dim = int(_get("head_dim", self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads)))
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        self.lora_rank = int(_get("lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(_get("lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", lora_alpha)))
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # Projections
        self.in_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Recurrence decay gate
        self.decay_param = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads, 1, 1))

        # Architecture detection
        model_type = str(_get("model_type", "")).lower()
        is_dec = _get("is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        if any(
            model_type.startswith(pfx)
            for pfx in ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "opt", "bloom")
        ):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)

        is_nested = False
        if old_attn is not None:
            src_cls = old_attn.__class__.__name__
            if (
                "Roberta" in src_cls
                or "Bert" in src_cls
                or (hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"))
            ):
                is_nested = True
        if not is_nested and model_type in ["roberta", "bert"] and not self.is_decoder:
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)
        self.triton_kwargs = {}

        if old_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(old_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        if old_attn is None:
            return

        src_cls = old_attn.__class__.__name__
        if (
            "Roberta" in src_cls
            or "Bert" in src_cls
            or (hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"))
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True

        target_dtype = self.in_proj.weight.dtype
        with torch.no_grad():
            w, b = None, None
            try:
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "q")
                if w is None:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "in")
            except Exception:
                pass

            if w is None and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
                c_w = getattr(
                    getattr(old_attn.c_attn, "base_layer", old_attn.c_attn),
                    "weight",
                    None,
                )
                if c_w is not None:
                    w_t = c_w.data.t() if c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1] else c_w.data
                    split = w_t.shape[0] // 3
                    w = w_t[:split, :]
                    self.is_decoder = True

            if w is None:
                for cand in ("q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "in_proj"):
                    mod = getattr(old_attn, cand, None)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        w = mod.weight.data
                        b = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
                        if b is not None:
                            b = b.data
                        break

            if w is not None:
                d_out = min(w.shape[0], self.dim)
                d_in = min(w.shape[1], self.dim)
                self.in_proj.weight.data[:d_out, :d_in].copy_(w[:d_out, :d_in].to(target_dtype))
                if b is not None and self.in_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.in_proj.bias.data[: min(b.shape[0], self.dim)].copy_(
                        b[: min(b.shape[0], self.dim)].to(target_dtype)
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            self.in_proj.freeze_base_weights()
            self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            return hidden_states, None

        while isinstance(hidden_states, (tuple, list)):
            hidden_states = hidden_states[0]

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        d = self.head_dim

        # In-projection
        u = self.in_proj(hidden_states).to(target_dtype)
        u_heads = u.view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2)  # [B, H, L, d]

        # RoPE
        pos_emb = position_embeddings or kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                u_heads, _ = apply_rotary_pos_emb(u_heads, u_heads, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        decay = torch.sigmoid(self.decay_param.to(target_dtype)).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        log_decay = torch.log(decay.clamp(min=1e-4, max=1.0 - 1e-4))

        pos = torch.arange(L, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        pos_diff = pos.unsqueeze(-1) - pos.unsqueeze(-2)

        if self.is_decoder:
            # Replace O(L^2) dense matrix with O(L) recurrent scan
            y_heads = torch.empty_like(u_heads)
            state = torch.zeros(B, H, 1, d, device=u_heads.device, dtype=target_dtype)
            decay_val = decay.to(target_dtype)
            for t in range(L):
                state = state * decay_val + u_heads[:, :, t:t+1, :]
                y_heads[:, :, t:t+1, :] = state
            y = y_heads.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, H * d)
        else:
            dist = torch.abs(pos_diff).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
            decay_mat = torch.exp((dist * log_decay).clamp(min=-30.0, max=0.0))
            y = (decay_mat.to(target_dtype) @ u_heads).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, H * d)
        if y.shape[-1] != self.dim:
            if y.shape[-1] < self.dim:
                y = F.pad(y, (0, self.dim - y.shape[-1]))
            else:
                y = y[..., : self.dim]

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = y.to(target_dtype)
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(y).to(target_dtype)

        if attention_mask is not None and attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            out = out * attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(target_dtype)

        return out.to(target_dtype), None

class SpectraformerRectify (head_dim: int, rff_features: int = 128, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class SpectraformerRectify(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, head_dim: int, rff_features: int = 128, **kwargs) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.rff_features = rff_features
        proj_init = torch.randn(head_dim, rff_features)
        if head_dim <= rff_features:
            proj_q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(proj_init.T)
            proj_init = proj_q.T
        else:
            proj_q, _ = torch.linalg.qr(proj_init)
            proj_init = proj_q
        self.proj = nn.Parameter(proj_init / math.sqrt(head_dim))

    def forward(self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        F_dim = self.rff_features

        orig_dtype = Q.dtype
        Q, K, V = Q.float(), K.float(), V.float()
        proj = self.proj.to(Q.device).float()

        Q_proj = torch.matmul(F.normalize(Q, p=2, dim=-1), proj).clamp(max=11.0)
        K_proj = torch.matmul(F.normalize(K, p=2, dim=-1), proj).clamp(max=11.0)

        Q_feat = torch.exp(Q_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
        K_feat = torch.exp(K_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)

        KV = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhnd->bhfd", K_feat, V)
        Z = K_feat.sum(dim=2)
        ctx = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhfd->bhnd", Q_feat, KV)
        norm = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhf->bhn", Q_feat, Z).unsqueeze(-1)
        return (ctx / norm.clamp(min=1e-5)).to(orig_dtype)

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def forward(self, Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    F_dim = self.rff_features

    orig_dtype = Q.dtype
    Q, K, V = Q.float(), K.float(), V.float()
    proj = self.proj.to(Q.device).float()

    Q_proj = torch.matmul(F.normalize(Q, p=2, dim=-1), proj).clamp(max=11.0)
    K_proj = torch.matmul(F.normalize(K, p=2, dim=-1), proj).clamp(max=11.0)

    Q_feat = torch.exp(Q_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)
    K_feat = torch.exp(K_proj) / math.sqrt(F_dim)

    KV = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhnd->bhfd", K_feat, V)
    Z = K_feat.sum(dim=2)
    ctx = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhfd->bhnd", Q_feat, KV)
    norm = torch.einsum("bhnf,bhf->bhn", Q_feat, Z).unsqueeze(-1)
    return (ctx / norm.clamp(min=1e-5)).to(orig_dtype)

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class SpectralMamba (d_model: int = 768,
d_state: int = 16,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
class SpectralMamba(nn.Module):
    """
    Spectral modulation with actual frequency filtering and low-rank state projection.

    Uses a learned frequency mask over FFT bins followed by low-rank adaptation.
    """

    def __init__(self, d_model: int = 768, d_state: int = 16, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        # Use a fixed resolution for frequency weights and interpolate to sequence length
        self.register_buffer(
            "freq_weights",
            torch.linspace(0.1, 1.0, 256).unsqueeze(1).expand(256, d_model).clone(),
        )
        self.register_buffer("dt_scale", torch.zeros(d_model))
        # Low-rank adaptation projection
        self.lora_down = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(d_model, d_state) * 0.01)
        self.lora_up = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(d_state, d_model))

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Spectral modulation with actual frequency filtering.
        """
        B, L, D = x.shape
        dtype = x.dtype

        # FFT along sequence axis
        x_float = x.float()
        x_fft = torch.fft.rfft(x_float, dim=1)  # (B, L//2+1, D)

        freq_weights = self.freq_weights
        if freq_weights.shape[0] != x_fft.shape[1]:
            freq_weights = (
                F.interpolate(
                    freq_weights.transpose(0, 1).unsqueeze(0),
                    size=x_fft.shape[1],
                    mode="linear",
                    align_corners=False,
                )
                .squeeze(0)
                .transpose(0, 1)
            )

        x_fft_filtered = x_fft * freq_weights.unsqueeze(0)

        # Back to time domain
        x_filtered = torch.fft.irfft(x_fft_filtered, n=L, dim=1).to(dtype)

        # Low-rank state projection
        state_act = F.linear(
            F.linear(x_filtered, self.lora_down.t()),
            self.lora_up.t(),
        )

        # Learned channel gate
        gate = torch.sigmoid(self.dt_scale).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        return (x_filtered + state_act) * gate

Spectral modulation with actual frequency filtering and low-rank state projection.

Uses a learned frequency mask over FFT bins followed by low-rank adaptation.

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """
    Spectral modulation with actual frequency filtering.
    """
    B, L, D = x.shape
    dtype = x.dtype

    # FFT along sequence axis
    x_float = x.float()
    x_fft = torch.fft.rfft(x_float, dim=1)  # (B, L//2+1, D)

    freq_weights = self.freq_weights
    if freq_weights.shape[0] != x_fft.shape[1]:
        freq_weights = (
            F.interpolate(
                freq_weights.transpose(0, 1).unsqueeze(0),
                size=x_fft.shape[1],
                mode="linear",
                align_corners=False,
            )
            .squeeze(0)
            .transpose(0, 1)
        )

    x_fft_filtered = x_fft * freq_weights.unsqueeze(0)

    # Back to time domain
    x_filtered = torch.fft.irfft(x_fft_filtered, n=L, dim=1).to(dtype)

    # Low-rank state projection
    state_act = F.linear(
        F.linear(x_filtered, self.lora_down.t()),
        self.lora_up.t(),
    )

    # Learned channel gate
    gate = torch.sigmoid(self.dt_scale).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

    return (x_filtered + state_act) * gate
