@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("spectral_mamba")
class SpectralMambaAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = _cfg_get(config, "compression_config", kwargs.get("comp_config"))

        self.hidden_size: int = kwargs.get(
            "hidden_size",
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                _cfg_get(config, "dim", _cfg_get(config, "d_model", 768)),
            ),
        )
        self.dim = self.hidden_size
        self.num_heads: int = kwargs.get(
            "num_heads",
            kwargs.get(
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(
                    config,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    _cfg_get(config, "num_heads", _cfg_get(config, "n_heads", 12)),
                ),
            ),
        )
        self.num_kv_heads: int = kwargs.get(
            "num_kv_heads",
            kwargs.get(
                "num_key_value_heads",
                _cfg_get(
                    config,
                    "num_key_value_heads",
                    _cfg_get(config, "num_kv_heads", self.num_heads),
                ),
            ),
        )
        self.head_dim: int = kwargs.get(
            "head_dim",
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "head_dim",
                self.dim // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64,
            ),
        )
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        is_dec = _cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        model_type = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
        decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if (
            any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in decoder_prefixes)
            or hasattr(config, "architectures")
            and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "lora_rank",
            _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
        )
        self.lora_alpha = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "lora_alpha",
            _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # Projections
        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        self.q_proj = self.q_lin
        self.k_proj = self.k_lin
        self.v_proj = self.v_lin
        self.out_proj = self.out_lin
        self.query = self.q_lin
        self.key = self.k_lin
        self.value = self.v_lin

        d_state = _cfg_get(comp_config, "d_state", _cfg_get(config, "d_state", 16))
        self.spectral_mamba = SpectralMamba(self.hidden_size, d_state=d_state)
        # Gate implemented with frozen buffer weight/bias + LoRA adaptation so zero base parameters are added
        # Gate initialization:
        # output = gate * attn + (1 - gate) * mamba
        # With gate_bias=10.0, sigmoid(10.0) ≈ 0.99995, so the model starts as ~pure attention (identical to pretrained).
        self.register_buffer("gate_weight", torch.zeros(1, self.hidden_size))
        self.register_buffer("gate_bias", torch.tensor([10.0]))
        self.gate_lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(4, self.hidden_size) * 0.01)
        self.gate_lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1, 4))

        dropout_p = _cfg_get(
            config, "dropout", _cfg_get(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1)
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                w, b = _unpack_weight_bias(mod)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj_key] = (w, b)
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                w, b = _unpack_weight_bias(out_mod)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted["o"] = (w, b)

        # 3. Flat modules
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        w, b = _unpack_weight_bias(mod)
                        if w is not None:
                            extracted[proj_key] = (w, b)
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                f"SpectralMambaAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )
            return

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_lin,
            "k": self.k_lin,
            "v": self.v_lin,
            "o": self.out_lin,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue
                    else:
                        logger.warning(f"Missing required weights for {proj_key}")
                        return

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    # For GQA expansion or repeating
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] > target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and w.shape[0] % target_mod.weight.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = w.shape[0] // target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    # For GQA: mean-average the head groups from source
                    w_grouped = w.view(target_mod.weight.shape[0], repeats, w.shape[1]).mean(dim=1)
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w_grouped)
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] > target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and b.shape[0] % target_mod.bias.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = b.shape[0] // target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        b_grouped = b.view(target_mod.bias.shape[0], repeats).mean(dim=1)
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b_grouped)
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                source_norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float()).item()
                fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    if source_norm > 1e-3:
                        raise RuntimeError(
                            f"SpectralMambaAttention: weight copy for {proj_key} produced near-zero norm "
                            f"({fnorm:.2e}) from source norm ({source_norm:.2e}). Aborting."
                        )
                    logger.warning(f"Weight copy notice for {proj_key}: norm {fnorm:.6f} < 1e-4")

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    _copy_pretrained = copy_weights

    @classmethod
    def from_distilbert_pretrained(
        cls, old_attn: nn.Module, model_config: Any | None = None, **kwargs: Any
    ) -> SpectralMambaAttention:
        return cls(config=model_config, pretrained_attn=old_attn, **kwargs)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if hidden_states is None:
            if "hidden_states" in kwargs and kwargs["hidden_states"] is not None:
                hidden_states = kwargs["hidden_states"]
            elif "x" in kwargs and kwargs["x"] is not None:
                hidden_states = kwargs["x"]
            elif "input_ids" in kwargs and kwargs["input_ids"] is not None:
                hidden_states = kwargs["input_ids"]
            elif len(args) > 0 and args[0] is not None:
                hidden_states = args[0]
        if hidden_states is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "SpectralMambaAttention forward requires hidden_states "
                "(pass as positional or via kwargs['hidden_states'])"
            )
        if attention_mask is None:
            attention_mask = kwargs.get("attention_mask")
        if hidden_states.dim() == 2:
            hidden_states = hidden_states.unsqueeze(0)
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape

        q = self.q_lin(hidden_states).view(B, N, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        k = (
            self.k_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )
        v = (
            self.v_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, N, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        )

        # Apply RoPE if provided
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # Repeat KV for GQA
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            k = repeat_kv(k, repeats)
            v = repeat_kv(v, repeats)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        # Memory-efficient: use SDPA when attention weights are not requested
        attn_out = None
        attn_weights = None

        if not output_attentions and head_mask is None:
            try:
                _qs = q.half() if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
                _ks = k.half() if k.dtype == torch.float32 else k
                _vs = v.half() if v.dtype == torch.float32 else v
                _sdpa_out = torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    _qs,
                    _ks,
                    _vs,
                    is_causal=getattr(self, "is_decoder", False) and N > 1,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                )
                _sdpa_out = (
                    _sdpa_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                    .transpose(1, 2)
                    .contiguous()
                    .view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
                )
                _sdpa_out = torch.nan_to_num(_sdpa_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                attn_out = (
                    self.out_proj(_sdpa_out)
                    if not getattr(self, "is_nested_encoder", False)
                    else _sdpa_out
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SDPA failed, falling back to manual attention: %s", e)
                attn_out = None

        if attn_out is None:
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            # Causal masking
            is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)
            if is_causal:
                causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.full((N, N), -1e4, device=scores.device), diagonal=1)
                scores = scores + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

            # Attention mask
            if attention_mask is not None:
                scores = apply_attention_mask(scores, attention_mask)

            attn_weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
            attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0)
            attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

            # head_mask handling
            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                    attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(
                        attn_weights.dtype
                    )
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                    attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.view(
                        head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1
                    ).to(attn_weights.dtype)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                    attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.to(attn_weights.dtype)

            attn_ctx = (
                torch.matmul(attn_weights, v)
                .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
                .contiguous()
                .view(B, N, self.all_head_size)
            )

            if self.is_nested_encoder:
                attn_out = attn_ctx
            else:
                attn_out = self.out_lin(attn_ctx)

        # Spectral Mamba stream
        # FIXED: FFT violates causality; bypass Spectral Mamba stream for decoders
        if self.is_decoder:
            mamba_out = torch.zeros_like(attn_out)
        else:
            mamba_out = self.spectral_mamba(hidden_states)
            while isinstance(mamba_out, (tuple, list)):
                mamba_out = mamba_out[0]

        if mamba_out.shape[-1] != attn_out.shape[-1]:
            if mamba_out.shape[-1] < attn_out.shape[-1]:
                mamba_out = F.pad(mamba_out, (0, attn_out.shape[-1] - mamba_out.shape[-1]))
            else:
                mamba_out = mamba_out[..., : attn_out.shape[-1]]

        # Adaptive gating: base linear + lora adapter
        gate_logits = F.linear(hidden_states, self.gate_weight, self.gate_bias)
        gate_logits = (
            gate_logits
            + F.linear(F.linear(hidden_states, self.gate_lora_A), self.gate_lora_B) * 2.0
        )
        g = torch.sigmoid(gate_logits)  # (B, N, 1)
        out = g * attn_out + (1.0 - g) * mamba_out

        if out.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            if out.shape[-1] < hidden_states.shape[-1]:
                out = F.pad(out, (0, hidden_states.shape[-1] - out.shape[-1]))
            else:
                out = out[..., : hidden_states.shape[-1]]

        weights_to_return = None
        if output_attentions:
            weights_to_return = locals().get("attn_weights", None)
        return out, weights_to_return

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

class TokenRouter (hidden_dim: int, keep_ratio: float = 0.75, min_tokens: int = 8)
Expand source code 
class TokenRouter(nn.Module):
    r"""Top-k token selector based on learned saliency scores with guaranteed [CLS] retention."""

    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int, keep_ratio: float = 0.75, min_tokens: int = 8) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 32), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(32, 1))
        self.keep_ratio = keep_ratio
        self.min_tokens = min_tokens

    def forward(
        self,
        x: torch.Tensor,
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        is_decoder: bool = False,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        B, N, D = x.shape
        net_dtype = next(self.net.parameters()).dtype
        scores = torch.sigmoid(self.net(x.to(net_dtype)).squeeze(-1))  # (B, N)
        if valid_mask is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask, -10000.0)

        if is_decoder and N > 1:
            causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=x.device, dtype=torch.bool))
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_mask[0], -10000.0)

        # Guarantee preservation of first token (index 0)
        scores[:, 0] = 1e4

        keep = min(
            N,
            max(
                1,
                (
                    min(self.min_tokens, N)
                    if self.min_tokens >= N
                    else max(self.min_tokens, int(N * self.keep_ratio))
                ),
            ),
        )
        idx = scores.topk(keep, dim=1).indices
        idx, _ = torch.sort(idx, dim=1)

        routed = torch.gather(x, 1, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D))
        return routed, idx

Top-k token selector based on learned saliency scores with guaranteed [CLS] retention.

def forward(
    self,
    x: torch.Tensor,
    valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    is_decoder: bool = False,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    B, N, D = x.shape
    net_dtype = next(self.net.parameters()).dtype
    scores = torch.sigmoid(self.net(x.to(net_dtype)).squeeze(-1))  # (B, N)
    if valid_mask is not None:
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask, -10000.0)

    if is_decoder and N > 1:
        causal_mask = torch.tril(torch.ones(N, N, device=x.device, dtype=torch.bool))
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal_mask[0], -10000.0)

    # Guarantee preservation of first token (index 0)
    scores[:, 0] = 1e4

    keep = min(
        N,
        max(
            1,
            (
                min(self.min_tokens, N)
                if self.min_tokens >= N
                else max(self.min_tokens, int(N * self.keep_ratio))
            ),
        ),
    )
    idx = scores.topk(keep, dim=1).indices
    idx, _ = torch.sort(idx, dim=1)

    routed = torch.gather(x, 1, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D))
    return routed, idx

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class UniversalAdapter
Expand source code 
class UniversalAdapter:
    """Universal adapter supporting BERT, DistilBERT, RoBERTa, LLaMA, GPT2, DeBERTa, etc."""

    @staticmethod
    def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any, proj: str) -> tuple:
        """Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

        Handles:
        - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module
        - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly
        - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj
        - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin
        - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight
        - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
        """
        target = getattr(old_attn, "self", old_attn)
        candidate_names = CANONICAL_PROJECTION_ALIASES.get(proj, [])

        def _unwrap(mod: Any) -> Any:
            """Unwrap LoRALinear / merged adapter layers to their base_layer."""
            if mod is None:
                return mod
            # LoRALinear stores the original frozen layer as base_layer
            if hasattr(mod, "base_layer") and hasattr(mod.base_layer, "weight"):
                return mod.base_layer
            return mod

        def _extract(mod: Any):
            """Return (weight_data, bias) from a module, or None if not a valid weight-holder."""
            mod = _unwrap(mod)
            if mod is None:
                return None
            if not hasattr(mod, "weight") or mod.weight is None:
                return None
            import torch
            import torch.nn as nn

            w = mod.weight
            # Must be either an nn.Parameter or a plain Tensor (Conv1D)
            if not (isinstance(w, nn.Parameter) or isinstance(w, torch.Tensor)):
                return None
            w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
            # Projections must strictly be 2D weight matrices (excludes 1D norms, 3D convolutions, etc.)
            if w.ndim != 2:
                return None
            # Conv1D stores weights transposed: [in_features, out_features]
            is_conv1d = (mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D") or hasattr(mod, "nf")
            if is_conv1d or (w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]):
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            bias = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
            bias = bias.data if (bias is not None and hasattr(bias, "data")) else bias
            # Qwen3.5 Gated Q: query is fused with gate (dim 0 has 2 * num_heads * head_dim)
            if proj == "q" and (
                hasattr(old_attn, "q_norm")
                or old_attn.__class__.__name__ == "Qwen3_5Attention"
                or hasattr(old_attn, "k_norm")
            ):
                cfg = getattr(old_attn, "config", None)
                tc = getattr(cfg, "text_config", cfg)
                h_dim = getattr(tc, "head_dim", 256)
                n_heads = getattr(tc, "num_attention_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", 8))
                if w.shape[0] == n_heads * h_dim * 2:
                    w = (
                        w.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim, -1)[:, 0, :, :]
                        .reshape(n_heads * h_dim, -1)
                        .contiguous()
                    )
                    if bias is not None:
                        bias = (
                            bias.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim)[:, 0, :]
                            .reshape(n_heads * h_dim)
                            .contiguous()
                        )
            return w, bias

        # 1. Search in target (old_attn.self or old_attn)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(target, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(target, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 2. Search in old_attn.output (BERT / RoBERTa output projection 'o')
        if proj == "o" and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
            out_mod = old_attn.output
            if hasattr(out_mod, "dense"):
                result = _extract(out_mod.dense)
                if result is not None:
                    return result
            result = _extract(out_mod)
            if result is not None:
                return result

        # 3. Search directly in old_attn (catches cases where there's no .self sub-module)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(old_attn, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 4. Fallback for Fused GPT-2 c_attn (must precede positional fallback)
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.c_attn)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :], (b[:split_b] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :], (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :], (b[2 * split_b :] if b is not None else None)

        # 4b. Fallback for Qwen3.5 / fused in_proj_qkv
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "in_proj_qkv"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.in_proj_qkv)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                k_dim = getattr(old_attn, "key_dim", getattr(old_attn, "k_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                v_dim = getattr(old_attn, "value_dim", getattr(old_attn, "v_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                q_dim = w.size(0) - k_dim - v_dim if (k_dim + v_dim < w.size(0)) else k_dim
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:q_dim, :], (b[:q_dim] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )

        # 4c. PyTorch nn.MultiheadAttention in_proj_weight / separate proj_weight
        for obj in (target, old_attn):
            if (
                proj in ("q", "k", "v")
                and hasattr(obj, "in_proj_weight")
                and obj.in_proj_weight is not None
            ):
                w = (
                    obj.in_proj_weight.data
                    if hasattr(obj.in_proj_weight, "data")
                    else obj.in_proj_weight
                )
                b = getattr(obj, "in_proj_bias", None)
                b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :].clone(), (b[:split_b].clone() if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :].clone(), (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :].clone(), (
                        b[2 * split_b :].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )

            if proj in ("q", "k", "v"):
                attr_w = f"{proj}_proj_weight"
                if hasattr(obj, attr_w) and getattr(obj, attr_w) is not None:
                    w = getattr(obj, attr_w)
                    w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
                    b = getattr(obj, f"{proj}_proj_bias", None)
                    b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                    return w.clone(), (b.clone() if b is not None else None)

        # 5. Positional fallback: scan all Linear children of target in declaration order.
        #    Only valid if at least 3 children exist (the typical pattern for BERT/RoBERTa variants).
        ordered_proj_map = {"q": 0, "k": 1, "v": 2}
        if proj in ordered_proj_map:
            children_with_weight = [
                (n, m) for n, m in target.named_children() if _extract(m) is not None
            ]
            if len(children_with_weight) >= 3:
                idx = ordered_proj_map[proj]
                if idx < len(children_with_weight):
                    return _extract(children_with_weight[idx][1])

        # 6. Fallback for 'o' projection when replacing leaf self-attention without local out_proj
        # (e.g. RoBERTa/BERT RobertaSelfAttention where output.dense resides in the parent container).
        # Returning an Identity matrix ensures:
        #   (a) custom modules don't crash during weight copying, and
        #   (b) context passes through transparently to parent.output.dense without double projection!
        if proj == "o":
            for mod in target.modules():
                if (
                    hasattr(mod, "weight")
                    and isinstance(getattr(mod, "weight", None), torch.Tensor)
                    and mod.weight.ndim == 2
                ):
                    d = mod.weight.shape[0]
                    eye_w = torch.eye(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    zero_b = torch.zeros(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    return eye_w, zero_b

        raise ValueError(
            f"Could not extract '{proj}' projection weights from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def get_rotary_emb(old_attn: Any) -> Any | None:
        """Return rotary embeddings if present."""
        return getattr(old_attn, "rotary_emb", None)

    @staticmethod
    def get_kv_heads(config: Any) -> int:
        """Return number of key-value heads for GQA/MQA."""
        return getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", 12))

@staticmethod
def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any, proj: str) -> tuple:
    """Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

    Handles:
    - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module
    - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly
    - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj
    - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin
    - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight
    - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
    """
    target = getattr(old_attn, "self", old_attn)
    candidate_names = CANONICAL_PROJECTION_ALIASES.get(proj, [])

    def _unwrap(mod: Any) -> Any:
        """Unwrap LoRALinear / merged adapter layers to their base_layer."""
        if mod is None:
            return mod
        # LoRALinear stores the original frozen layer as base_layer
        if hasattr(mod, "base_layer") and hasattr(mod.base_layer, "weight"):
            return mod.base_layer
        return mod

    def _extract(mod: Any):
        """Return (weight_data, bias) from a module, or None if not a valid weight-holder."""
        mod = _unwrap(mod)
        if mod is None:
            return None
        if not hasattr(mod, "weight") or mod.weight is None:
            return None
        import torch
        import torch.nn as nn

        w = mod.weight
        # Must be either an nn.Parameter or a plain Tensor (Conv1D)
        if not (isinstance(w, nn.Parameter) or isinstance(w, torch.Tensor)):
            return None
        w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
        # Projections must strictly be 2D weight matrices (excludes 1D norms, 3D convolutions, etc.)
        if w.ndim != 2:
            return None
        # Conv1D stores weights transposed: [in_features, out_features]
        is_conv1d = (mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D") or hasattr(mod, "nf")
        if is_conv1d or (w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]):
            w = w.t().contiguous()
        bias = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
        bias = bias.data if (bias is not None and hasattr(bias, "data")) else bias
        # Qwen3.5 Gated Q: query is fused with gate (dim 0 has 2 * num_heads * head_dim)
        if proj == "q" and (
            hasattr(old_attn, "q_norm")
            or old_attn.__class__.__name__ == "Qwen3_5Attention"
            or hasattr(old_attn, "k_norm")
        ):
            cfg = getattr(old_attn, "config", None)
            tc = getattr(cfg, "text_config", cfg)
            h_dim = getattr(tc, "head_dim", 256)
            n_heads = getattr(tc, "num_attention_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", 8))
            if w.shape[0] == n_heads * h_dim * 2:
                w = (
                    w.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim, -1)[:, 0, :, :]
                    .reshape(n_heads * h_dim, -1)
                    .contiguous()
                )
                if bias is not None:
                    bias = (
                        bias.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim)[:, 0, :]
                        .reshape(n_heads * h_dim)
                        .contiguous()
                    )
        return w, bias

    # 1. Search in target (old_attn.self or old_attn)
    for name in candidate_names:
        if hasattr(target, name):
            result = _extract(getattr(target, name))
            if result is not None:
                return result

    # 2. Search in old_attn.output (BERT / RoBERTa output projection 'o')
    if proj == "o" and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
        out_mod = old_attn.output
        if hasattr(out_mod, "dense"):
            result = _extract(out_mod.dense)
            if result is not None:
                return result
        result = _extract(out_mod)
        if result is not None:
            return result

    # 3. Search directly in old_attn (catches cases where there's no .self sub-module)
    for name in candidate_names:
        if hasattr(old_attn, name):
            result = _extract(getattr(old_attn, name))
            if result is not None:
                return result

    # 4. Fallback for Fused GPT-2 c_attn (must precede positional fallback)
    if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
        fused_result = _extract(old_attn.c_attn)
        if fused_result is not None:
            w, b = fused_result
            split_w = w.size(0) // 3
            split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:split_w, :], (b[:split_b] if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :], (
                    b[split_b : 2 * split_b] if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[2 * split_w :, :], (b[2 * split_b :] if b is not None else None)

    # 4b. Fallback for Qwen3.5 / fused in_proj_qkv
    if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "in_proj_qkv"):
        fused_result = _extract(old_attn.in_proj_qkv)
        if fused_result is not None:
            w, b = fused_result
            k_dim = getattr(old_attn, "key_dim", getattr(old_attn, "k_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
            v_dim = getattr(old_attn, "value_dim", getattr(old_attn, "v_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
            q_dim = w.size(0) - k_dim - v_dim if (k_dim + v_dim < w.size(0)) else k_dim
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:q_dim, :], (b[:q_dim] if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :], (
                    b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim] if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :], (
                    b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim] if b is not None else None
                )

    # 4c. PyTorch nn.MultiheadAttention in_proj_weight / separate proj_weight
    for obj in (target, old_attn):
        if (
            proj in ("q", "k", "v")
            and hasattr(obj, "in_proj_weight")
            and obj.in_proj_weight is not None
        ):
            w = (
                obj.in_proj_weight.data
                if hasattr(obj.in_proj_weight, "data")
                else obj.in_proj_weight
            )
            b = getattr(obj, "in_proj_bias", None)
            b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
            split_w = w.size(0) // 3
            split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
            if proj == "q":
                return w[:split_w, :].clone(), (b[:split_b].clone() if b is not None else None)
            elif proj == "k":
                return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :].clone(), (
                    b[split_b : 2 * split_b].clone() if b is not None else None
                )
            elif proj == "v":
                return w[2 * split_w :, :].clone(), (
                    b[2 * split_b :].clone() if b is not None else None
                )

        if proj in ("q", "k", "v"):
            attr_w = f"{proj}_proj_weight"
            if hasattr(obj, attr_w) and getattr(obj, attr_w) is not None:
                w = getattr(obj, attr_w)
                w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
                b = getattr(obj, f"{proj}_proj_bias", None)
                b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                return w.clone(), (b.clone() if b is not None else None)

    # 5. Positional fallback: scan all Linear children of target in declaration order.
    #    Only valid if at least 3 children exist (the typical pattern for BERT/RoBERTa variants).
    ordered_proj_map = {"q": 0, "k": 1, "v": 2}
    if proj in ordered_proj_map:
        children_with_weight = [
            (n, m) for n, m in target.named_children() if _extract(m) is not None
        ]
        if len(children_with_weight) >= 3:
            idx = ordered_proj_map[proj]
            if idx < len(children_with_weight):
                return _extract(children_with_weight[idx][1])

    # 6. Fallback for 'o' projection when replacing leaf self-attention without local out_proj
    # (e.g. RoBERTa/BERT RobertaSelfAttention where output.dense resides in the parent container).
    # Returning an Identity matrix ensures:
    #   (a) custom modules don't crash during weight copying, and
    #   (b) context passes through transparently to parent.output.dense without double projection!
    if proj == "o":
        for mod in target.modules():
            if (
                hasattr(mod, "weight")
                and isinstance(getattr(mod, "weight", None), torch.Tensor)
                and mod.weight.ndim == 2
            ):
                d = mod.weight.shape[0]
                eye_w = torch.eye(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                zero_b = torch.zeros(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                return eye_w, zero_b

    raise ValueError(
        f"Could not extract '{proj}' projection weights from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
    )

Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

Handles: - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "vspec", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "vspec_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class VSpecAttention(BaseAttention):
    r"""VSpec++ Attention: Variational Spectral Token-Adaptive Attention Module."""

    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        src_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("old_attn")

        cfg_head_dim = _cfg_get(config, "head_dim", kwargs.get("head_dim"))
        if cfg_head_dim is None and src_attn is not None:
            for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin"):
                mod = getattr(src_attn, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    num_h = _cfg_get(config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_heads", 12))
                    cfg_head_dim = mod.weight.shape[0] // max(1, num_h)
                    break

        _mt = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if config else ""
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        is_dec = (
            (
                getattr(config, "is_decoder", False)
                or getattr(config, "causal", False)
                or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            )
            if config
            else False
        )
        if hasattr(config, "architectures") and any(
            "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
            for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True

        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            self.adapter = ModelAdapter(config)
            self.num_heads: int = self.adapter.num_heads
            self.num_kv_heads: int = self.adapter.num_key_value_heads
            self.hidden_size: int = self.adapter.hidden_size
            self.head_dim: int = (
                int(cfg_head_dim) if cfg_head_dim is not None else self.adapter.head_dim
            )
            self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
            self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
            self.kv_repeat: int = self.adapter.kv_repeat
            self.is_decoder: bool = is_dec
        else:
            self.adapter = None
            self.hidden_size = _cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
            self.num_heads = _cfg_get(config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_heads", 12))
            self.num_kv_heads = _cfg_get(config, "num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads)
            self.head_dim = (
                int(cfg_head_dim)
                if cfg_head_dim is not None
                else (self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads))
            )
            self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
            self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
            self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
            self.is_decoder = is_dec

        # Explicit overrides
        if "hidden_size" in kwargs:
            self.hidden_size = kwargs["hidden_size"]
        if "num_heads" in kwargs:
            self.num_heads = kwargs["num_heads"]
        if "head_dim" in kwargs:
            self.head_dim = kwargs["head_dim"]
        elif cfg_head_dim is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(cfg_head_dim)
        else:
            self.head_dim = self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads)

        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder

        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False
        self.rank: int = rank
        self.lora_rank = kwargs.get("lora_rank", _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank", lora_rank))
        self.lora_alpha = kwargs.get("lora_alpha", _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha", lora_alpha))

        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and src_attn is not None:
            target_dtype = next(src_attn.parameters(), torch.tensor([])).dtype
            if not isinstance(target_dtype, torch.dtype):
                target_dtype = torch.float32

        self.Pq = DynamicLowRankProjector(
            rank=self.rank,
            in_dim=self.hidden_size,
            out_dim=self.all_head_size,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.Pk = DynamicLowRankProjector(
            rank=self.rank,
            in_dim=self.hidden_size,
            out_dim=self.kv_head_size,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.Pv = DynamicLowRankProjector(
            rank=self.rank,
            in_dim=self.hidden_size,
            out_dim=self.kv_head_size,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.Po = DynamicLowRankProjector(
            rank=self.rank,
            in_dim=self.all_head_size,
            out_dim=self.hidden_size,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )

        self.sg_q = VariationalSpectralGate(self.hidden_size, self.rank)
        self.sg_k = VariationalSpectralGate(self.hidden_size, self.rank)
        self.sg_v = VariationalSpectralGate(self.hidden_size, self.rank)

        self.head_gate = HeadGate(self.hidden_size, self.num_heads)
        self.router = TokenRouter(self.hidden_size, keep_ratio=0.75)

        dropout_p = _cfg_get(
            config,
            "dropout",
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "hidden_dropout_prob",
                _cfg_get(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
            ),
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)
        self.rotary_emb = getattr(src_attn, "rotary_emb", None)

        if src_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src_attn)

        if target_dtype is not None:
            self.to(target_dtype)

    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.Pq

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.Pk

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.Pv

    @property
    def q_proj(self):
        return self.Pq

    @property
    def k_proj(self):
        return self.Pk

    @property
    def v_proj(self):
        return self.Pv

    @property
    def out_proj(self):
        return self.Po

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.Po

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.Po

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.Po

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. UniversalAdapter extraction
        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 2. GPT-2 Fused c_attn Conv1D handling
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            c_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_mod, "base_layer", c_mod)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_mod, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_mod, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
                is_conv1d = (
                    "Conv1D" in c_mod.__class__.__name__
                    or "Conv1D" in base_c.__class__.__name__
                    or hasattr(c_mod, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or (weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[0] < weight.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv1d:
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
                q_w = weight[:split_size, :]
                k_w = weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :]
                v_w = weight[2 * split_size :, :]
                q_b = bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                k_b = bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                v_b = bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (q_w, q_b)
                extracted["k"] = (k_w, k_b)
                extracted["v"] = (v_w, v_b)

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            p_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(p_mod, "base_layer", p_mod)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(p_mod, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                is_conv1d = (
                    "Conv1D" in p_mod.__class__.__name__
                    or "Conv1D" in base_o.__class__.__name__
                    or hasattr(p_mod, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                    or (w_o.dim() == 2 and w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv1d:
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(p_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o.data.clone() if b_o is not None else None)

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT Nested Encoder handling
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query"):
                extracted["q"] = (s.query.weight.data, getattr(s.query, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "key"):
                extracted["k"] = (s.key.weight.data, getattr(s.key, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "value"):
                extracted["v"] = (s.value.weight.data, getattr(s.value, "bias", None))

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (dense.weight.data, getattr(dense, "bias", None))

        # 4. Modern Decoders (Qwen3.5, LLaMA, Mistral) & DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key", "W_k"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value", "W_v"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense", "out", "c_proj"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        base = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
                        if hasattr(base, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                base.weight.data,
                                getattr(base, "bias", None),
                            )
                            break

        # Detect nested encoder architecture
        cls_name = getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__
        has_out = (
            hasattr(src, "dense")
            or hasattr(src, "out_proj")
            or hasattr(src, "out_lin")
            or hasattr(src, "c_proj")
            or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if cls_name in [
            "BertSelfAttention",
            "RobertaSelfAttention",
            "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
            "ElectraSelfAttention",
            "DebertaSelfAttention",
        ] or (hasattr(src, "query") and not has_out):
            is_nested = True
        if hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src, "output"):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning(
                "VSpecAttention missing required weights for projections: %s; keeping defaults",
                missing,
            )

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        # Copy into projectors
        if extracted["q"][0] is not None:
            self.Pq.set_weight(_orient(extracted["q"][0], self.hidden_size), extracted["q"][1])
        if extracted["k"][0] is not None:
            self.Pk.set_weight(_orient(extracted["k"][0], self.hidden_size), extracted["k"][1])
        if extracted["v"][0] is not None:
            self.Pv.set_weight(_orient(extracted["v"][0], self.hidden_size), extracted["v"][1])

        if extracted["o"][0] is not None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
            self.Po.set_weight(_orient(extracted["o"][0], self.all_head_size), extracted["o"][1])
        elif self.is_nested_encoder:
            eye_w = torch.eye(self.all_head_size, self.all_head_size)
            self.Po.set_weight(eye_w, None)

        # Preserve source LoRA adapters if present
        source_submodules = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query", "W_q"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key", "W_k"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value", "W_v"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense", "out", "c_proj"],
        }
        for proj_key, target in [
            ("q", self.Pq),
            ("k", self.Pk),
            ("v", self.Pv),
            ("o", self.Po),
        ]:
            for name in source_submodules[proj_key]:
                if hasattr(src, name):
                    sub = getattr(src, name)
                    target.copy_lora_from(sub)
                    break

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None] | torch.Tensor:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None or kwargs.get("encoder_hidden_states") is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("Cross-attention is not implemented for VSpecAttention")

        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        # Mask handling
        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")
        valid = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N, device) if mask is not None else None

        is_decoder = (
            self.is_decoder
            or getattr(self.config, "is_decoder", False)
            or getattr(self.config, "causal", False)
            or any(
                str(getattr(self.config, "model_type", "") or "").lower().startswith(p)
                for p in (
                    "gpt",
                    "qwen",
                    "llama",
                    "mistral",
                    "gemma",
                    "phi",
                    "opt",
                    "bloom",
                    "falcon",
                    "olmo",
                    "mpt",
                    "stablelm",
                )
            )
        )

        # Token routing: Skip for nested encoders (RoBERTa/BERT) to preserve context
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            routed = hidden_states
            idx = torch.arange(N, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            k_len = N
            valid_routed = valid
        else:
            routed, idx = self.router(hidden_states, valid, is_decoder=is_decoder)
            k_len = routed.shape[1]
            valid_routed = torch.gather(valid, 1, idx) if valid is not None else None

        # Variational spectral gates using masked mean over routed tokens
        gq, _, _ = self.sg_q(routed, valid_routed)
        gk, _, _ = self.sg_k(routed, valid_routed)
        gv, _, _ = self.sg_v(routed, valid_routed)

        Q = self.Pq(routed, gq)
        K = self.Pk(routed, gk)
        V = self.Pv(routed, gv)

        H = self.num_heads
        H_kv = self.num_kv_heads
        Dh = self.head_dim

        Q = Q.view(B, k_len, H, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = K.view(B, k_len, H_kv, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = V.view(B, k_len, H_kv, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # Rotary position embeddings
        pos_emb = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            cos_b = cos.expand(B, -1, -1) if cos.size(0) == 1 else cos
            sin_b = sin.expand(B, -1, -1) if sin.size(0) == 1 else sin
            cos_routed = torch.gather(cos_b, 1, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, cos.size(-1)))
            sin_routed = torch.gather(sin_b, 1, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, sin.size(-1)))
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos_routed, sin_routed)
        elif getattr(self, "rotary_emb", None) is not None:
            try:
                from neuralops.utils.compat import maybe_apply_rotary

                Q, K = maybe_apply_rotary(
                    Q,
                    K,
                    getattr(self, "adapter", None),
                    self.rotary_emb,
                    kwargs.get("position_ids"),
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"maybe_apply_rotary failed: {e}")

        # GQA expansion
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        hg = self.head_gate(routed, valid_routed).unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(Dh)
        scores = (
            torch.matmul(Q.float(), K.float().transpose(-1, -2).contiguous()) * scale * hg.float()
        )

        # Check causal decoder
        if is_decoder and k_len > 1:
            causal_mask = torch.triu(
                torch.full((k_len, k_len), float("-inf"), device=device, dtype=scores.dtype),
                diagonal=1,
            )
            scores = scores + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        # Apply padding mask to attention scores over routed tokens
        if valid_routed is not None:
            pad_mask = (
                (~valid_routed).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2).expand(-1, self.num_heads, k_len, k_len)
            )
            scores = scores.masked_fill(pad_mask, float("-inf"))

        attn_weights = torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        attn_weights = torch.nan_to_num(attn_weights, nan=0.0).to(dtype)
        attn_weights = self.dropout(attn_weights)

        # Head mask broadcasting
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), head_mask.size(1), 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                if (
                    head_mask.shape[-1] == head_mask.shape[-2]
                    and head_mask.shape[-1] != attn_weights.shape[-1]
                ):
                    head_mask = head_mask.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            attn_weights = attn_weights * head_mask.to(attn_weights.dtype)

        context = torch.matmul(attn_weights, V)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, k_len, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return (context, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None))

        out = self.Po(context, gq)

        if out.shape[-1] != D:
            out = F.pad(out, (0, D - out.shape[-1])) if out.shape[-1] < D else out[..., :D]

        # Scatter back to full sequence length preserving residual:
        # Non-routed tokens have zero attention delta, so outer block residual keeps them unchanged.
        if k_len == N:
            output = out
        else:
            zeros = torch.zeros(B, N, D, device=device, dtype=out.dtype)
            output = zeros.scatter(1, idx.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, D), out)

        return (output, (attn_weights if output_attentions else None))

class VariationalSpectralGate (hidden_dim: int, rank: int)
Expand source code 
class VariationalSpectralGate(nn.Module):
    r"""Input-dependent variational gate over truncated singular values with masked pooling."""

    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int, rank: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 64), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(64, rank * 2))
        # Initialize gate to neutral (near 1.0) so SVD representations are preserved at step 0
        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].weight)
        nn.init.constant_(self.net[-1].bias[:rank], 3.0)  # mu -> sigmoid(3.0) ≈ 0.95
        nn.init.constant_(self.net[-1].bias[rank:], -3.0)  # log_sigma -> low variance

    def forward(
        self, x: torch.Tensor, valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        net_dtype = next(self.net.parameters()).dtype
        pooled = _masked_mean(x, valid_mask).to(net_dtype)
        stats = self.net(pooled)
        mu, log_sigma = stats.chunk(2, dim=-1)
        log_sigma = torch.clamp(log_sigma, min=-10.0, max=2.0)
        if self.training:
            eps = torch.randn_like(mu)
            z = mu + eps * torch.exp(log_sigma)
        else:
            z = mu
        g = torch.sigmoid(z).to(x.dtype)
        return g, mu, log_sigma

Input-dependent variational gate over truncated singular values with masked pooling.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

Methods

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]
Expand source code 
def forward(
    self, x: torch.Tensor, valid_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    net_dtype = next(self.net.parameters()).dtype
    pooled = _masked_mean(x, valid_mask).to(net_dtype)
    stats = self.net(pooled)
    mu, log_sigma = stats.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    log_sigma = torch.clamp(log_sigma, min=-10.0, max=2.0)
    if self.training:
        eps = torch.randn_like(mu)
        z = mu + eps * torch.exp(log_sigma)
    else:
        z = mu
    g = torch.sigmoid(z).to(x.dtype)
    return g, mu, log_sigma

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class XAttentionScorer
Expand source code 
class XAttentionScorer:
    @staticmethod
    def score_blocks(
        Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, block_size: int, stride: int = 2
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, N, D = Q.shape
        n_blocks = (N + block_size - 1) // block_size

        Q_sample = Q[:, :, ::stride, :]
        K_sample = K[:, :, ::stride, :]
        N_s = Q_sample.size(2)

        scale = D**-0.5
        attn_sample = torch.matmul(Q_sample, K_sample.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        b_s = max(1, block_size // stride)
        target = n_blocks * b_s
        if N_s < target:
            pad = target - N_s
            attn_sample = F.pad(attn_sample, (0, pad, 0, pad), value=0.0)
        attn_sample = attn_sample[:, :, :target, :target]

        attn_blocks = attn_sample.view(B, H, n_blocks, b_s, n_blocks, b_s)
        scores = attn_blocks.mean(dim=(3, 5))
        return scores

Static methods

def score_blocks(Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, block_size: int, stride: int = 2) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
@staticmethod
def score_blocks(
    Q: torch.Tensor, K: torch.Tensor, block_size: int, stride: int = 2
) -> torch.Tensor:
    B, H, N, D = Q.shape
    n_blocks = (N + block_size - 1) // block_size

    Q_sample = Q[:, :, ::stride, :]
    K_sample = K[:, :, ::stride, :]
    N_s = Q_sample.size(2)

    scale = D**-0.5
    attn_sample = torch.matmul(Q_sample, K_sample.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

    b_s = max(1, block_size // stride)
    target = n_blocks * b_s
    if N_s < target:
        pad = target - N_s
        attn_sample = F.pad(attn_sample, (0, pad, 0, pad), value=0.0)
    attn_sample = attn_sample[:, :, :target, :target]

    attn_blocks = attn_sample.view(B, H, n_blocks, b_s, n_blocks, b_s)
    scores = attn_blocks.mean(dim=(3, 5))
    return scores
